本文件是跨 S1–S5、跨数据面、控制面、产品面和仓库面的技术执行权威。它把此前口头说明的 Phase 0–7 变成可逐票执行、逐门验收、可停止、可回滚、可复审的工程程序。计划允许后续根据实测证据修订，但修订必须进入 Git、机器合同和工作记录，不得只依赖聊天记忆。

本文件本身不授权跳过前置门。计划冻结后，第一项实施仅为 Phase 0 治理重基线；组件下载、网络研究、模型调用、旧索引删除、代码迁移和产品切换分别受后续门约束。

## 1. Owner 决定与本程序解决的问题

Owner 已明确要求：

1. 不再沿 R14 继续扩写确定性开放英文语义系统；
2. 正确收口 R14，并过渡到一个新的全产品技术阶段；
3. 对 S1–S5、RAG、embedding、reranker、Agent、Evidence、S2、报告、Workbench、运维和安全做完整 Build / Adopt / Hold / Retire 审计；
4. 对应采用成熟栈的能力做广泛调研，而不是只列四五个知名项目；
5. 候选必须在本机或隔离环境真实下载、安装、固定版本并测试；
6. 旧代码、新成熟栈、当前必须补的适配/自研代码和未来接口必须形成清晰仓库结构；
7. 使用真实 case 验证完整产品链；允许在独立权限和预算合同下调用 DeepSeek API；
8. 最终交付必须是已集成、已验证、可回滚的可行架构，不是只写一份调研报告。

本程序把上述要求解释为一项新的“架构迁移程序”，不是新的产品版本、不是新的 S-stage，也不是 `dell_03b_s1_internal_chain` 的 R15/R16 successor。程序阶段使用 Phase 0–7，只描述迁移工作的成熟度；FIN 0.1.3、S0–S5、合同版本和执行 attempt 继续保持相互独立。

### 2.2 `dell_03b_s1_internal_chain` 的 R14 不是 PASS，也不再是活动实现路线

本节以及全文中未另行限定的“R14 implementation freeze”“R14 replacement”“不得创建 R15/R16”和“没有 R15/R16 successor”，只指 `dell_03b_s1_internal_chain` / DELL 03B S1 corpus-parity replacement 这一责任命名空间。它们不指 S3 Writer R15、VS2 lineage R16、其他 S-stage、其他能力或历史文件名中的 R-number attempt；Phase 0 supersession 也不得重写那些其他轨道的 R15/R16 历史。机器键必须使用 `dell_03b_s1_internal_chain.*`，不得使用无命名空间的 `r14/r15/r16` 全局字段。

唯一 R14 corpus parity preview 的不可变事实为：

- total cases：27,026；
- pass：26,787；
- fail：239；
- event-semantics failures：228；
- assertion-semantics failures：11；
- event mismatches：277；
- I2 governance：PASS，仅表示失败清单、输入、两类最早责任层、验收和停止条件已冻结；
- R14 product capability delta：none；
- `dell_03b_s1_internal_chain.r15_successor_created=false`、`dell_03b_s1_internal_chain.r16_successor_created=false`；不得因本迁移程序创建该命名空间的 R15/R16 successor。

该命名空间 R14 的程序处置定义为：

> active implementation strategically terminated / failed evidence preserved / regression baseline retained / no PASS / no root-cause erasure。

这意味着：

- 不再修改 R14 production implementation；
- 不进入 R14 pre-formal、policy 或 formal；
- 不用新框架或新模型把 R14 失败改写成成功；

- replacement 的产品身份属于同一 S1 责任面，不使用 `dell_03b_s1_internal_chain` 的 R15/R16 编号。

Phase 0 的 typed machine disposition 必须至少冻结：

- `dell_03b_s1_internal_chain.r14_implementation_freeze="7e25cad95ee84b39fb2a51063100405bc27da6e5"`；
- `dell_03b_s1_internal_chain.r14_implementation_lifecycle="regression_only"`；

- `dell_03b_s1_internal_chain.r15_successor_created=false`；
- `dell_03b_s1_internal_chain.r16_successor_created=false`。

`r14_is_current=false` 与 `r14_is_product_pass=false` 只能是从这些 typed fields、冻结失败 receipt 与 current-consumer map 重算出的 derived booleans，不得作为另一个自由状态源。

## 3. 身份轴、状态词和禁止混用

### 3.1 六条独立身份轴

| 身份轴 | 示例 | 用途 | 禁止误用 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 产品版本 | FIN 0.1.3 | 对用户承诺的一轮产品范围 | 不能给失败或迁移尝试编号 |
| S-stage | S1–S5 | 同一产品内的数据、研究、审阅、发布成熟度 | 不能替代产品版本 |
| 合同版本 | SemanticCandidate v1、SourceCapture v1 | schema、协议或行为变化 | 不能表示一次重跑 |
| 架构程序阶段 | Phase 0–7 | 本迁移程序的治理、审计、调研、资格、集成和收口 | 不能冒充 S-stage |
| authority epoch | `E0`、`E1` | 同一 program 内一套 exact plan、H0 governance root 与其 grants 的有效期 | 不能当产品版本、S-stage、合同版本或 execution attempt；旧 epoch 不给新 epoch 借权 |
| execution/run/attempt | 日期＋能力＋候选＋split＋rN | 一次安装、测试、模型调用或迁移执行 | 失败不得自动创建产品/R编号 |

machine identity 必须写全：`authority_epoch_id=E{e}`、`plan_revision_id=E{e}-C{j}`、`phase0_attempt_id=E{e}-H{n}`、`ordinary_ticket_attempt_id=E{e}-{stable_ticket_id}-C{a}`。`e/j/n/a` 都从 0 开始、使用无前导零十进制；`j` 与 `n` 在每个 epoch 内重新从 0 开始。裸 `C6`、`H1` 或 `P1-01-C0` 只可作为有人类上下文的显示 alias，不能成为 machine predecessor。本payload的committed predecessor chain严格为 `E0-C0`–`E0-C5`（human aliases `C0`–`C5`）；其designated next lineage slot为`E0-C6`。plan-only commit前它只是prospective `E0-C6` payload，任意working-tree/precheck candidate ordinal都不增加`j`、不进入历史且不能成为machine predecessor；只有§19门控的plan-only commit/push/identity成功后才产生committed `E0-C6`事实，且只有后续exact committed review P0/P1/P2=0才能把它选为accepted Ck。本payload不能自证这些receipt；若committed `E0-C6`的exact review FAIL，修正版占用`E0-C7`。

### 3.2 状态轴与唯一 machine vocabulary

机器合同不得使用一个通用 `status` 混装阶段、票据、动作、证据和程序收口。唯一允许的canonical状态轴如下；除表内或后文明确标为pure reducer output的轴外，其余才是receipt/candidate中的原始状态。reducer output不得由candidate、materializer或人工pointer直接写入：

| 轴/字段 | 允许值 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|
| `phase_status` | `planned / candidate / in_progress / passed / failed / stopped / blocked / superseded` | Phase 0–7 的阶段状态 |
| `phase_review_status` | `not_started / pending / passed / failed` | 对当前 phase candidate/result 的独立 review 状态 |
| `phase_entry_eligible` | `true / false` | 上一阶段已允许为本阶段建立逐票候选；只表示可申请，不是任何 action grant |
| `ticket_applicability_status` | `pending_evaluation / applicable / not_applicable` | stable ticket 在当前 phase/scope 是否进入 required set；独立于 lifecycle、terminal 和 downstream activation |
| `ticket_lifecycle_stage` | `planned / candidate / candidate_reviewed / execution_authorized / result_frozen / result_reviewed / terminal` | 单票从事前合同到不可变结果的生命周期 |
| `ticket_terminal_status` | `null / passed / failed / stopped / held / superseded` | 仅当 lifecycle=`terminal` 时才允许非 null |
| `attempt_terminal_status` | `null / passed / failed / stopped / superseded` | 历史 execution/run/attempt 的结局，不能替代 phase/ticket/program 状态 |
| `downstream_activation_status` | `not_applicable / pending / activated / denied / revoked` | terminal PASS 后是否已独立激活下游消费或下一阶段 |
| `action_grant_status` | `not_granted / authorized / active / consumed / expired / revoked` | 某个 exact action 当前是否可执行；同时必须有 `granted:boolean` |
| `primary_claim_state` | `absent / prepared_unaccepted / remote_accepted` | primary start claim 的原始事实轴；只有冻结 endpoint/ref 的 exact readback 可以确认 `remote_accepted`，本地 prepared、日志、超时或事后 result 都不能升级该值 |
| `primary_effect_observation_state` | `no_observation / no_call_proven / in_flight / succeeded / failed / unknown` | 已承诺 logical invocation 的外部观测输入；它不能反向改变 claim acceptance。`unknown` 只能触发同一 endpoint/invocation 的只读 query，`no_call_proven` 是负向闭包而不是重新发起 primary 的许可 |
| `primary_physical_effect_state` | `pre_dispatch_no_call_proven / no_call_proven / no_observation / in_flight_same_server_operation_proven / primary_effect_complete / primary_effect_absent_proven / effect_unknown / terminal_no_effect` | 只由真实 adapter call/observation/readback receipt 纯投影。`dispatch_primary` choice被接受后、第一次adapter call前仍保持`pre_dispatch_no_call_proven`；choice本身绝不能伪造server-operation ID或in-flight证明。`no_call_proven` 后 primary initiate/resume/complete 永久为 false；compensation选择/调用只属于route/member轴，不得复制成physical state |
| `physical_operation_kind` | `query_readback / initiate_first_physical_call / continue_same_server_operation / complete_same_operation / cancel_before_effect / invoke_prearmed_compensation` | 每次 adapter 操作的 closed enum；不得再用“继续/恢复”自由文本扩大副作用。除 query 外每一次call都须exact one-shot operation-intent winner、operation/root/subject/result-boundary门；原`dispatch_primary` winner不能同时授权continue/complete/cancel/compensation |
| `physical_operation_intent_state` | `absent / prepared_unaccepted / remote_accepted / observation_recorded / consumed` | 每个非query物理call在调用前的durable单赢家事实轴；generation绑定exact predecessor intent/observation与operation kind。同generation至多一个winner，上一generation没有observation receipt时不得创建下一generation |
| `primary_effect_route_state` | `pre_dispatch_unselected / primary_effecting / primary_quiesced / pre_dispatch_safe_branch_selected / compensation_selected / compensation_invoked` | initial primary choice只选route，不改写物理state；后继route只能由真实observation与独立safety-suboperation choice单调推进。`pre_dispatch_safe_branch_selected`须先赢typed safety choice才可到`compensation_selected`；进入`compensation_selected|compensation_invoked`后不得回到primary effecting。physical `terminal_no_effect`不属于本轴 |
| `prearmed_safety_suboperation_state` | `not_armed / armed_not_selected / selected_not_invoked / in_flight / succeeded / failed / unknown` | accepted primary claim 中事前冻结的 exact compensation/recovery member 状态；可物理调用的canonical authority basis tag只能是 `q_quiescence / index_under_lock_recovery / ordinary_same_claim_recovery` 之一，三分支不可互换或事后补签。terminal Git stale使用独立no-retry lifecycle closure，不是本轴member |
| `safety_suboperation_choice_state` | `not_applicable / absent / prepared_unaccepted / remote_accepted / consumed` | ordinary/Q/IDX recovery的独立single-winner CAS；它绑定initial primary winner、typed predecessor（safe-if-no-call sentinel或exact operation observation）、三选一basis和预装member，不是第二primary claim、alternate primary choice或第17类control action。terminal Git profile显式不注册本choice |
| `action_execution_state` | `not_started / started / started_unknown / result_recorded` | 上述原始轴的 pure projection：`remote_accepted` 表示一个 logical primary invocation 已不可撤销地 committed；`no_observation|effect_unknown` 投影 `started_unknown`，但默认只允许 query；冻结 lifecycle result 后为 `result_recorded`，同一旧 attempt 的全部 physical operations（包括 query/补偿/恢复）均为 false |
| `authority_epoch_stage` | `plan_revisioning / phase0_governance / activated / quiescing / finalizing / terminal` | 同一 program 内当前 authority epoch 的阶段；`current` 不是可写 raw value |
| `authority_epoch_activation_status` | `pending / activated / revoked` | epoch governance root 是否已由本 epoch G(e) 激活或永久撤销 |
| `authority_epoch_grant_issuance_status` | `closed / open / frozen / revoked` | 是否可签发新的普通 ticket/action grant；quiescing 时必须 frozen |
| `authority_epoch_quiescence_status` | `not_started / pending / satisfied / failed` | rollover cutoff 后 in-flight/unknown/writer/job 是否已安全收口 |
| `authority_epoch_terminal_status` | `null / superseded / stopped / failed` | 单个 epoch 的结局；与整个 program terminal 独立 |
| `program_terminal_ready_status` | `null / ready_complete / ready_partial / ready_stopped / ready_failed` | `program_terminal_ready_reducer_v1`的pure output；只冻结终局事实并关闭产品动作，尚未等于final terminal |
| `terminal_incident_stream_state` | `open_clean / intake_pending / gap_waiting / invalid_corrupt / integrity_failure_closed / sealed` | `terminal_incident_stream_state_v1`或最终joint-seal reducer的pure output；pending/gap只关闭sync/final并保留exact next-I intake；corrupt只表示仍有未被确定性I覆盖的异常；covered failure进入failed-only收口而非伪装clean |
| `program_terminal_status` | `null / complete / partial / stopped / failed` | `program_terminal_and_incident_seal_reducer_v1` canonical pair的program projection（`program_terminal_projection_v1`只读该pair）；整个 Phase 0–7 程序最终状态，非可写raw phase/ticket状态 |
| `artifact_immutability` | `mutable_candidate / frozen_immutable` | manifest/result/corpus/receipt 的证据属性，`frozen` 不是 phase 状态 |
| `implementation_lifecycle` | `current / shadow / compatibility / regression_only / retired` | 代码/组件实现所处产品生命周期，非程序状态 |
| `consumer_route_availability` | `available / degraded / suspended / unavailable / retired` | 某个 consumer route 的可用性；原因另写 `availability_reason`，不能塞进状态值 |

有执行动作且真正进入执行的正向 ticket 必须按 `planned → candidate → candidate_reviewed → execution_authorized → result_frozen → result_reviewed → terminal` 前进；`execution_authorized` 必须有独立、decision=authorized 的 `execution_authority_receipt`，它发生在动作和结果之前，但仍只产生`action_execution_state=not_started`。任何真实读写、网络、进程、安装、模型、删除、cutover、publication或其他副作用前，还必须按6.4的`substantive_action_start_linearization_v1`让exact one-shot start claim在冻结remote/ref上赢得expected-old lease；remote接受claim只令一个logical invocation committed并投影为`started|started_unknown`，不直接许可任意adapter调用。initial primary choice只选择dispatch/no-call route且不得改变physical state；每次非query物理调用还必须由当前generation的remote-accepted one-shot operation-intent、adapter capability与typed physical-state/route-state matrices共同允许；adapter支持的safe-if-no-call或post-dispatch recovery另须独立safety-suboperation choice先选中exact预装member，实际recovery/compensation call仍须自己的operation intent。本地“再检查一次”、缓存grant、prepared/local-only claim或事后result都不能替代。pre-execution negative 是唯一例外：candidate-review FAIL 可从 `candidate_reviewed → result_frozen`；authority denied/held 时 lifecycle 保持 `candidate_reviewed`，随后进入 `result_frozen`；已 authorized 但start claim尚未remote accepted即expired/revoked可从 `execution_authorized → result_frozen`。若且唯若registry-static列出的pre-Q rollover late-I或terminal-sync stale supersession发生在candidate-review PASS之后、任何authority decision之前，必须先复用`ticket_execution_authority_receipt_materialization_write`在exact interrupt/stale root下物化denial-only `execution_authority_decision=denied_due_to_registered_control_supersession`：其`action_grant_status=not_granted`、`granted=false`，逐字节绑定old candidate/review、首个superseding evidence、current parent与独立issuer，且不得授任何execution/sync/domain/product action；随后才按既有authority-denied路径写negative result。不得用`execution_authority_receipt=null`直接result-before-authority，也不得把该variant泛化到ordinary ticket。所有pre-execution negative都必须写 `action_started=false`、start-claim full ref=null、无执行输出、exact negative evidence，且 terminal 不能是 passed。无执行动作的纯只读/聚合 ticket 可以不经过 `execution_authorized`，但必须显式写 `execution_authority_receipt=null/not_applicable`且其registry actions=[]；任何会访问本地受保护数据、网络、service或写artifact的“只读审计”仍是action-bearing并必须claim。`ticket_terminal_status=passed` 只能在 result review PASS 后物化；`downstream_activation_status=activated` 又只能在 terminal PASS 后由另一个 `downstream_activation_receipt` 物化。execution authority、start claim与downstream activation永不共用同一receipt。

`action_grant_status=not_granted/consumed/expired/revoked` 时 `granted=false`；只有 `authorized/active` 时 `granted=true`。`authorized`只允许竞争start claim，不允许副作用；remote accepted claim后才为`active`，但每个非query adapter call仍须同一claim的initial primary-choice receipt、当前operation-intent winner、typed predecessor（generation-0 null predecessor或successor exact observation）、fence、operation identity、capability profile及当前physical-state/route-state rows全部匹配；`query_readback`是唯一无本次call operation-intent的例外，必须改走`physical_operation_observation_receipt_v2`的typed query分支、绑定exact claim/fence/endpoint/adapter/root/result-boundary及query binding kind，并且不产生effect/choice/intent/safety/result authority；recovery/compensation还必须绑定独立safety-choice winner。safe-if-no-call safety generation 0须使用exact accepted choice sentinel、null observation、`no_call_proven`和intent/call count=0；post-dispatch/successor才允许exact observation predecessor。query只生成readback receipt，不选择effect；idempotency key相同不够。result冻结后为`consumed`且旧ticket全部physical operation关闭。缺 receipt、前驱、预算、allowlist、reviewer、start claim、primary choice、operation intent、适用的safety choice或operation proof时必须 fail closed。`held` 是一次 ticket 的 terminal 结果；`HOLD_TRIGGER` 是 3.3 的 capability decision，两者不能互换。

start claim 的线性化边界采用“逻辑调用承诺”，而不是不可实现的跨 Git/外部系统原子调用承诺。R/I/Q 在 claim remote acceptance 之前赢得 expected-old 时，primary logical invocation 和 physical adapter call 均为 0；claim 先被 remote 接受时，它恰好承诺一个 logical primary invocation，但还不等于外部 effect 已发生。initial primary choice另对 `(accepted claim,fence,invocation,primary-choice-generation)` 做durable expected-old CAS；winner=`dispatch_primary`只把route置为selected/primary-effecting，physical state在adapter call前仍是`pre_dispatch_no_call_proven`。随后`initiate_first_physical_call`还须赢得绑定该winner与pre-call state的one-shot operation intent，真实call/receipt才可投影`no_observation|in_flight_same_server_operation_proven|primary_effect_complete|primary_effect_absent_proven|effect_unknown`。之后每个continue/complete/cancel call都要在自己的successor operation generation以exact predecessor observation竞争单赢家；compensation operation-intent若来自safe-if-no-call safety generation 0则两个operation predecessor refs必须为null，若来自post-dispatch/successor则必须绑定exact predecessor observation；`in_flight`还须`same_server_operation_proof_v1`，`no_observation|unknown`默认只query，`no_call_proven`永久禁止primary initiate/resume/complete。只有capability允许的ordinary/Q/IDX安全收口可由独立safety-suboperation choice选中一个预装member；它不是alternate primary。terminal Git stale同attempt只readback并non-PASS收口，不走本补偿路径。R/I/Q只能阻止第二claim、新scope、同generation第二winner或第二logical invocation，不能把已接受的第一次承诺改写为“从未开始”。生命周期result/result-review/terminal任一边界一旦成立，同一旧attempt的所有physical operation均关闭；尚未安全关闭的义务必须形成non-PASS并由事前注册的新recovery/cleanup ticket承接。

`phase0_candidate_awaiting_fresh_review`、`plan_candidate`、`phase6_s4_candidate_authorized`、`phase*_active/pass`、`r14_is_current`、`r14_is_product_pass` 等长标签只能作为由上述原始字段计算出的 boolean `derived_state_flags`，不能作为 enum 输入、receipt verdict 或 action grant。例：当前 Hn 的 `phase0_candidate_awaiting_fresh_review=true` 必须等价于 `phase_status=candidate AND phase_review_status=pending AND downstream_activation_status=pending AND accepted_phase0_terminal_receipt=null AND G0_absent=true`；`active` 只能从 `phase_status=in_progress` 与有效 action grant 推导。`current_authority_epoch_id` 同样只能由 6.3 的唯一 reducer 输出；mutable `current.json` 或人工 pointer 只可导航，不能授权。

为避免 self-reference，candidate manifest 只冻结 `candidate_state` 原始字段与 transition rules；`effective_state` 由 validator 把不可变 candidate manifest、exact review/terminal receipts 和 downstream-activation receipts 联合计算，不能回写 candidate。以 Phase 0 为例，当前 Hn 中 `candidate_state.phase_status=candidate`；valid Tn只能令effective Phase0 passed/review passed/activation pending，same-epoch valid G(e)才令activation=activated，并按`phase_entry_gate_registry_v1`同时令Phase1与Phase2的candidate eligibility=true。H/F/S/T历史对象保持不变；G(e)不签Phase0 PASS，也不授Phase1/2 substantive action。

validator 必须拒绝非法组合，包括：terminal status 在非 terminal lifecycle 中非 null、ticket 未审即 execution-authorized、result 先于authority decision receipt、control supersession review PASS却跳过上述denial-only receipt、denial-only receipt被写成authorized/granted或用于ordinary ticket、terminal PASS 缺 result-review PASS、terminal PASS 前 downstream activated、`ticket_applicability_status=not_applicable` 却存在 candidate/action/result、applicability selector 空/歧义/陈旧或 candidate binding 漂移却进入 phase-exit reducer、candidate 创建后追加 applicability attempt、phase PASS 缺 required ticket terminal PASS、cleanup expansion 缺失/漂移/漏 target、program complete 缺全部 completion gate/terminal sync。它还必须拒绝任何未注册的原始 `status`、`active`、`pass/PASS`、`regression` 字段，拒绝用 `phase_entry_eligible=true` 或 applicability receipt 推导 substantive action grant，拒绝跨 authority epoch 消费旧 root/PASS/grant/activation，并拒绝把 `availability_reason` 拼进 `consumer_route_availability`。selector 的 mode 必须是无参数固定 enum；`program-finalizing:*`、`program-finalizing-intake:*`、`program-terminal:*` 全部非法，ready 与 final terminal status 只能进入各自独立 typed 字段。还必须拒绝initial primary choice直接伪造`in_flight_same_server_operation_proven`、无one-shot operation-intent winner的非query call、同predecessor generation的continue/complete/cancel并发winner、cancel timeout直接投影effect absent、`no_call_proven→primary dispatch/resume/complete`、`unknown→alternate choice/member`、同generation双winner、没有独立safety-choice winner的ordinary/Q/IDX补偿、safe-if-no-call sentinel缺失或与非null observation/dispatch choice/既有intent或call混用、successor缺exact observation、result冻结后的任一旧票physical operation、把同一idempotency key冒充exactly-once、三类physical recovery branch互换，以及terminal Git stale借任一physical recovery basis重试或补偿。禁止使用含义不明的 done、ready、green；只有完整字段名`program_terminal_ready_status`及其四个`ready_*`枚举可表示终局待同步态，每个 passed 必须带 scope、证据和 known boundary。

`physical_operation_observation_receipt_v2`只使用既有`compact_physical_operation_observation` locator，是以`receipt_kind`判别的exact union：`non_query_operation_observation`必须绑定同claim、同generation/kind的remote-accepted current-call intent；`query_readback`本次call不得创建或伪造intent，并再以`accepted_claim_pre_choice|accepted_choice_pre_intent|accepted_intent`三种`query_readback_binding_kind`冻结choice/intent nullability。所有`*_or_null`字段都必须实际存在，missing不得被宽松解释成null。前两种intentless query只对`terminal_git_stale_no_retry_lifecycle_closure_v2`开放，`projected_physical_effect_state_or_null=null`、same-server-operation proof=null、safety predecessor=false，同attempt后续nonquery永久false；一次stale read cap耗尽后即使classification仍unknown也只能truthful non-PASS→独立review→old terminal。`accepted_intent` query只有在claim/choice/prior intent/generation/fence/endpoint/adapter/result-boundary全部exact且adapter classification映射合格时才可细化physical state，query本身仍不推进route或intent generation。

observation leaf继续是`b/O$OPERATION_GENERATION.json`的generation-scoped monotonic Git head：revision 0绑定null predecessor，后继revision必须以exact current remote-accepted observation full ref做expected-old CAS；program/epoch/ticket/attempt/action/target/claim/generation/fence/endpoint/profile/result-boundary identity tuple不得改变，旧full ref继续是immutable evidence。safety choice只可消费latest、intent non-null且projection non-null的nonquery receipt，或`accepted_intent` query receipt；intentless query、旧revision、`no_observation|effect_unknown`与projection=null永远不能成为post-dispatch safety predecessor。

### 3.3 决策标签

能力决策仅使用：

- KEEP_BUILD：FIN 必须拥有的金融权威或产品差异化；
- ADOPT_MATURE：成熟组件承担通用实现，FIN 保留薄 adapter 和业务合同；
- HOLD_TRIGGER：当前不进入主线，达到预声明触发条件再开；
- RETIRE_AFTER_PROOF：在 shadow、回滚和验收通过后退出活动主路径；
- REGRESSION_ONLY：只保留为历史、fixture、oracle attack 或对照；
- EXCLUDE：许可、安全、复杂度、锁定或能力边界不适合。

组件角色仅使用 default、challenger、managed ceiling、excluded。每类能力最终最多保留一个 default、一个 challenger 和一个 managed ceiling；选型结束后失败候选不进入正式依赖。

## 4. 全程序不可破坏的不变量

以下规则适用于 Phase 0–7，任何一项被破坏都必须停止当前 slice：

1. Candidate、Evidence、NumericFact、Research Judgment 和 Release Authority 永不合并。
2. 第三方输出只能进入其对应的未准入 envelope：
   - crawler/WARC → SourceCapture；
   - parser/OCR → ParsedElement、TableCell、Locator；
   - XBRL processor → unadmitted FactObservation；
   - retrieval/reranker → RetrievalCandidate；
   - LLM extraction/judge → SemanticCandidate；
   - 只有 FIN admission 才能产生 Evidence 或 NumericFact。
3. source identity、issuer、as-of、period、unit、scale、vintage、conflict、rights、citation eligibility、GapEligibility、financial bridge、WWC、人审和 release authority 由 FIN 持有。
4. 任何框架的 state、score、confidence、citation 或 grounded 标签都不自动获得 FIN 权威。
5. 旧失败和正式证据不可覆盖；修复或迁移使用新 attempt ID。
6. 失败留在最早责任层，不用 downstream fallback 掩盖。
7. 模型、Provider、付费 API 和真实外源动作必须有 task-specific TokenBudgetBasis。
8. 默认 SDK retry 为 0；不能在 FIN receipt 之外透明重试。
9. 未证明 external exactly-once 时，timeout/reset 必须写 unknown_external_completion 或 duplicate_risk 并停止。
10. 不可信网页、PDF、Office、图片、压缩包和 source-derived instruction 必须在解析、索引和模型前经过 MIME/magic、资源、malware、sandbox、egress/SSRF 与 prompt/tool/path/query injection 门。
11. frozen test/holdout 不用于调参；看到 hidden outcome 的实现上下文不得继续声称 blind。
12. 任何迁移都必须有 old path、new path、shadow/dual-read、rollback、exit 和 retire 条件。
13. 不为追求更便宜或更快静默删除必需研究工作。
14. 不把通用框架的安装成功、平均 benchmark 或 vendor claim 写成 FIN 产品通过。
15. MLflow、OTel、LangGraph、PostgreSQL、搜索引擎或任何第三方平台丢失，都不能改变 FIN 已签发的 Evidence 或 release 状态。
16. 同一时刻最多一个 authority epoch 可处于 active/quiescing/planning current selector；selector 为空时所有 substantive action fail closed。
17. authority epoch rollover 必须先冻结 grant issuance、收口 cutoff 前已开始动作、撤销未开始 grants并冻结真实 runtime/side-effect baseline；旧 epoch 的 PASS、grant、activation 与 phase-entry eligibility 不跨 epoch 继承。
18. cleanup denominator 按 exact `source attempt × target identity` 计算；family 名、source stable ID 或一次批量清理不能代表多个具体义务。
19. 同一 idempotency key 不是 exactly-once 证明；每个effectful call必须命中冻结adapter capability、typed generation-0 predecessor或successor exact observation、同一server operation/fence与本generation唯一remote-accepted operation-intent winner，否则只允许query或fail closed。原primary choice winner不能无限授权后继call。
20. `no_observation|effect_unknown` 默认 query-only；`no_call_proven` 是 primary generation 的终结事实。SDK 自动 retry、换 key、换 member 或“试一次看看”均禁止。
21. lifecycle result 一旦冻结，同一旧 attempt 的所有 physical operation、compensation 和 recovery 都关闭；尚未安全收口的 external effect必须先形成 non-PASS 与预注册的新恢复义务，不能先写 PASS/result 再回旧票补动作。
22. Q quiescence、IDX under-lock recovery与ordinary same-claim recovery是三类互斥physical recovery authority basis；failure、stop、expiry、result或terminal只能消费事前预装member，不能生成或补签。terminal Git stale sync固定走no-retry lifecycle closure，不是physical recovery basis。
23. initial primary choice、physical operation intent、safety-suboperation choice 与 compensation/recovery suboperation 都属于既有 substantive action 的子协议，不得被算作第17个 control action，也不得反向产生domain authority。
24. initial primary choice、每次physical operation intent和post-dispatch safety-suboperation choice是三个不同责任面：前者只选primary/no-call route，中者逐call单赢家，后者只从预装member中选exact recovery；任何一个receipt不得冒充另两个。
25. `cancel_before_effect`是会触达外部adapter的effecting operation；只有qualified readback明确证明primary未发生才能投影`primary_effect_absent_proven`，timeout/response-lost/ambiguous必须保持`no_observation|effect_unknown`。

26. `physical_operation_observation_receipt_v2`的query分支只是受限readback证据，不是“免intent的副作用通道”：intentless query最多消费terminal Git stale profile的一次read cap，physical projection必须null、同attempt后续nonquery必须永久为false；selected safety member、route或旧intent都不能覆盖current `no_observation|effect_unknown`的query-only deny。

## 5. 目标架构原则与候选仓库结构

### 5.1 逻辑架构

~~~text
成熟数据与运行基础设施
  source/XBRL/crawl/WARC/document AI/storage/search/rerank
  provider SDK/workflow/checkpoint/trace/eval/render/security
                              |
                              | typed ports and thin adapters
                              v
FIN 金融权威内核
  identity/as-of/source role/rights
  Candidate/Evidence/NumericFact authority
  PIT/unit/period/vintage/conflict/financial bridge
  Gap/materiality/causal boundary/counter/WWC
  claim locator/human review/release/immutable receipt
                              |
                              v
FIN 产品应用
  Evidence Workspace / Workpaper / Review / Repair / Approval / Deliverable
~~~

所有可能产生外部副作用的 port/adapter 还必须暴露冻结 capability profile：服务端 idempotency/fence 的真实作用域和保留窗口、query/status、same-operation resume、cancel-before-effect、initial primary-choice backend/locator/readback、逐次 one-shot operation-intent backend/locator/generation/readback、独立 safety-choice backend/locator/readback、compensation/recovery 与 crash readback。`cancel_before_effect`按effecting operation处理；response lost/timeout只能保持unknown，除非qualified readback明确证明primary未发生。wrapper 不能把 vendor SDK retry 或“同一个 key”包装成 exactly-once；若既不能可靠查询/围栏，也没有事前可验证的补偿/恢复，则该 adapter 只能 query-only 或在 qualification 被排除，不能获得 execution authority。

### 5.2 候选代码结构

以下是 Phase 4 前的候选目标，不授权 Phase 0/1 立即创建整套新目录或批量移动文件。Phase 1 必须先冻结活动 consumer、真实 import cycle、runtime resource exact-digest 绑定和按责任命名空间拆分的历史 R-attempt reachability（现有文件名范围可见 R3–R14，但不是全局产品序列）；Phase 3 spike 可能修正命名，Phase 4 ADR 才能把候选结构变成迁移权威，但职责分层不得反向合并。

~~~text
src/finsight/
  domain/
    identity/
    source_authority/
    evidence/

    financial_bridge/

    provider.py

    renderer.py
  adapters/
    capture/

    xbrl/
    retrieval/

    storage/
    rendering/
  platform/

    security/

  compat/
    retrieval_import_shim.py
    research_runtime_adapter.py
    provider_transport_adapter.py

apps/workbench/
  backend/
  frontend/

scripts/
  qualification/
  migration/
  operations/

configs/
  repository/
  qualification/
  deployments/
  audits/

tests/
  contract/

    legacy_r14/
  qualification/

archive/versions/
  # 完整旧实现、origin/redirect/digest 与历史审计；不进入 package discovery
~~~

### 5.3 与当前代码的过渡关系

| 当前区域 | Phase 1 初始假设 | 目标关系 |
|---|---|---|
| src/evidence | KEEP_BUILD | 迁入 domain/evidence，保持兼容 import |
| src/financial_facts | KEEP_BUILD | 迁入 domain/numeric_facts，先双读后切换 |
| identity/as-of/source role 分散实现 | KEEP_BUILD + consolidate | 收敛为一个 domain contract source |
| src/connectors、src/ingestion | WRAP/REPLACE | 成熟 source/document adapters，保留 FIN source authority |
| src/indexing、src/retrieval | WRAP/REPLACE/PARTIAL KEEP | 通用 index/search/rerank 由成熟栈承担；FIN query constraints 与 admission 保留 |
| src/sec_agent/providers | REPLACE | 官方 SDK＋薄 capability/receipt adapter |
| src/sec_agent/research | SPLIT | 金融方法和 domain state 保留；通用 orchestration/session/trace 迁出 |
| project_os_preflight.py | SHRINK | 跨版本不变量保留；attempt-specific 分支数据化/退役 |
| namespaced historical R-attempt modules（现有文件名可含 R3–R14） | REGRESSION_ONLY | 完整实现只留 Git/history archive；有界 harness/case 放非 package regression root；不进入新生产主路径 |
| apps/workbench | KEEP_PRODUCT + THIN | 留金融 Evidence/Gap/Review/Release；通用 trace/run UI 交成熟平台 |

`src/finsight/compat` 只能容纳当前仍被消费、具 removal ticket 的薄 shim/adapter，不得复制任何 namespaced historical R-attempt implementation。历史 replay 只能由显式 `historical_audit`/qualification profile 执行，默认 wheel、Runtime Registry、API/UI 和 CI 主路径不得发现或导入它。

任何真实目录创建、模块移动或 import 重写必须等 Phase 4 ADR 冻结，并在 Phase 5 通过 compatibility shim、import map、consumer tests 和 rollback slice 完成。不得先建立空的“目标架构骨架”，再用它反向证明目标结构合理。

### 5.4 当前真实实现锚点与 Phase 1 必须复现的初查事实

以下是 plan revision 前的只读 surface scan；它们是 Phase 1 的待复现输入，不是未经机器 artifact 固定的最终审计结论：

- 当前产品 composition roots 是 `apps/workbench/backend/app.py`、`scripts/dev/run_workbench_backend.py` 和前端 ResearchWorkspace/Operations，而不是候选 `src/finsight`；
- 当前 `src` 下约有 retrieval 118、sec_agent 65、ingestion 8、financial_facts 5、connectors/evidence 3、indexing 2 个 Python 文件；`sec_agent.research` 约 20 处依赖 retrieval，retrieval 又有 2 处反向依赖 sec_agent，形成真实 package cycle；
- `financial_facts` 约有 4 处依赖 retrieval，属于需要在迁移前拆开的 domain inversion；
- `research_retrieval_service.py` 直接组合约 19 个 retrieval 模块及其他 domain，不是可直接替换的薄 adapter；
- runtime registry 当前按 exact digest 绑定约 28 个资源；移动文件、改内容或只改 import 都可能让 application/replay gate 失败；
- `configs` 约 1,190 份，混合 policy、result、current、attempt 和 audit；`scripts/data_retrieval` 与 `scripts/research` 混合正式 runner、一次性工具、live/zero-call 路径；
- `data` 总量约 74.69 GiB，其中 `data/indexes` 约 25.93 GiB；容量不是删除授权，producer/input/consumer/rebuild map 仍是前置条件；
- `pyproject.toml` 已声明 contract、integration、full-chain、paid/network/local-data 等 marker vocabulary，但约 200 个 test 尚未完成逐测试分类；默认 pytest 是否会意外触发本地数据、网络或付费调用仍需证明；
- 当前仓库没有已冻结的 ports/adapters/infrastructure/legacy package 边界，不能把候选目录图误报为现状。

Phase 1 必须以可重放命令和机器输出复现或修正这些数字；若结果不同，保留差异并解释时间点、扫描规则和 consumer 定义，不得静默覆盖。

## 6. 程序拓扑、提交拓扑与变更控制

### 6.1 程序依赖

~~~text
Plan-only revisions C0 -> ... -> Ck
  Ck = unique latest exact-review PASS revision accepted for this authority epoch
        |
        v fresh read-only exact-Ck plan review
Phase 0 candidate H0
  machine manifest + source docs + tests
  Phase 1/2 candidate eligibility and all action grants still false
        |
        v fresh read-only exact-H0 review
        +---------------- PASS ----------------+
        |                                      |
        v                                      |
Phase 0 accepted terminal PASS receipt Tn       |
  logical predecessor=Hn; Git parent=current materialization parent
  one terminal-receipt path                      |
        |                                      |
        v                                      |
Phase 0 downstream-activation receipt G0       |
  logical predecessor=accepted Tn; Git parent=current materialization parent
  remote expected-parent CAS; one activation path|
  Phase 1/2 candidate eligible=true; P1 audit/RSH network grants=false
        |
        v
Phase 1 P1 / Phase 2 RSH applicability -> candidate -> review -> own execution authority
        |
        v
Phase 1 capability/import/data/consumer migration inventory/result
        |
        +----------------------+
        |                      |
        v                      v
Phase 2 broad research     frozen benchmark/gold/security fixture preparation
        |                      |
        +----------+-----------+
                   v
Phase 3 isolated qualification lab
                   |
                   v
Phase 4 target architecture and migration ADR freeze
                   |
                   v
Phase 5 release-sliced integration and compatibility migration
                   |
                   v
Phase 6 S1–S5 real-case acceptance
                   |
                   v
Phase 7 closeout, retirement and release recommendation

Hn -- exact review FAIL --> Fn terminal FAIL receipt
Fn -- implementation finding --> successor materialization authority --> Sn
Sn -- exact changed-path allowlist --> H(n+1) candidate
Fn -- material-plan finding + empty-authority review --> pre-activation Z(e)=superseded

Any activated E(e) -- approved PROGRAM-EPOCH-ROLLOVER --> quiescence cutoff Q(e)
Q(e) -- close started and Q-revoked authorized actions / consume interrupts --> epoch terminal Z(e)
Z(e)=superseded --> (post-Z I0...Ii)* --> E(e+1)-C0 plan-only --> ... --> E(e+1)-H0/Tn/G(e+1)

Reserved non-activated E(t) -- pre_activation_epoch_governance_v1 --> Z(t)=superseded|stopped
~~~

G(e)只让Phase1与Phase2进入“可以提出逐票candidate”的资格态，不直接授权审计、network research或任何bounded write。Phase1每票取得自己的execution authority后才执行；Phase2每张RSH还须绑定registry映射的relevant P1 constraint terminal/activation。两者可在各自授权后受控并行，但Phase2 close/shortlist必须消费P1-15、Phase1 close及全部relevant constraints；Phase3只消费二者close aggregate与冻结fixture。后续Phase3–7 entry只来自6.5固定close-ticket activation，不可自行推断。

### 6.2 提交拓扑

1. 计划 revision 使用 epoch-scoped index `E{e}-C{j}`，Phase 0 implementation/review attempt 使用独立 `E{e}-H{n}`，`e/j/n` 严禁混用。首 epoch E0 的恢复基线是 `34589b6b8552e0236912dacb0664d7714bbf275c`；未审 plan seed commit 是 `9b848b3fe372f908b27bc541682b8af656562a0a`，其 parent=恢复基线；`initial_plan_revision=E0-C0` 的 parent=该 seed。seed 不是 Cj、没有 review authority、不能授权 H0。对E(e>0)，`predecessor_epoch_terminal_ref`/`epoch_plan_lineage_seed_ref`恒为前一epoch positive Z full ref；但`initial_plan_materialization_parent_ref`恒由`successor_pre_activation_control_head_v1`计算：无post-Z I时等于Z，有连续I0..Ii时等于latest Ii。E(e)-C0必须是该materialization parent的direct child，同时payload逐字节绑定lineage seed Z、完整handoff chain及latest head；seed与Git parent不可混为一字段。每个同 epoch `Cj` 都是 plan-only、single-parent、non-merge commit，changed path 恰为本计划，且在 review 前已用lease-guarded exact-expected-old fast-forward发布、local origin-tracking aligned。若尚无accepted Ck且exact review有任一 P0/P1/P2，下一 revision 只能是同 epoch `C(j+1).parent=Cj`；失败 review payload 保持不可变。一旦Ck成为accepted PASS，同epoch plan-revision creation立即closed；后到material change必须走6.3 pre-activation Z并进入新epoch，不能再追加第二个同epochPASS C。post-H0 successor epoch使用`Z(e)→(post-Z I0...Ii)*→E(e+1)-C0` grammar，不追加全局 C(k+1)。
2. 每个 authority epoch 的 `accepted_plan_revision=E{e}-C{k}` 必须由 validator 在该 epoch 内唯一选择：`k` 为该 epoch canonical plan-revision chain 中无后继 plan revision的最新连续 index；Ck 已 commit/push/aligned，exact author-separated review=`PASS 0/0/0/*`；同 epoch所有 `j<k` review均为immutable FAIL。Ck接受后不得有C(k+1)；若存在，只能由explicit positive Z先关闭旧epoch后在next epoch从C0重新编号。前 epoch 的 accepted PASS 不进入新 epoch 的 `j<k` 集合，也不授权新 H0；latest 未 review、review FAIL、tracking drift、merge、编号/epoch跳跃、多个 latest、PASS后同epoch新增C或review object 漂移都使 accepted selector 为空。
   accepted Ck后至remote G前，机器另维护pure `pre_g_control_head_v1=(logical_head_ref,materialization_parent_ref,continuous_intake_I_set,effective_event_class)`。无I时两ref相同；若remote-accepted pre-G event/rejection/integrity I存在，Git materialization parent是latest连续I，但logical head仍是最近Ck/Hn/Fn/Sn/Tn。`effective_event_class=nonblocking_rejected_or_resolved`且全部anomaly已覆盖时可从latest I继续normal reducer；material/authorized-stop/integrity-failure时normal successor=false并走Z。任何H/T/F/S/Hnext/G/Z payload都同时绑定logical predecessor与actual Git parent/I-set；不得把I伪装成H/F/S/T、跳过I、或仍强求Git parent等于旧logical head。下述“X.parent=Y”在I-set为空时是Git parent；非空时精确解释为`logical_parent=Y, git_parent=latest I`。
3. H0：exact accepted epoch-scoped Ck plan review PASS payload只有在6.3定义的current `pre_g_transition_snapshot_v1`逐字节绑定本计划内嵌的exact seed、Ck review cutoff、current logical/materialization parents与完整连续per-source remote-accepted receipt high-watermarks，且total reducer唯一输出`accepted_Ck_no_event_to_H0`、pending material/authorized-stop/unknown/gap/duplicate/replay/uncovered anomaly全部为空时，才授权`phase0_governance_candidate_materialization_write`的`E{e}-H0` one-shot；任一seed source未观测、事件存在、stopper无效、异常未覆盖或grant消费前remote parent/high-watermark漂移都令H0 normal grant为空，并必须先按`pre_g_canonical_head_transition_table_v1`走exact receipt、recoverable intake、reviewed Z或integrity-I＋failed-Z，不能停在无出口的invalid。H0 logical parent=Ck；无pre-G I时Git parent=Ck，有连续I时Git parent=latest I，prepared H0以该current materialization parent做lease-guarded exact-expected-old remote fast-forward CAS。H0物化plan review/epoch C0..Ck registry、Phase0 candidate manifest、source supersession、Project OS、checklist/worklog/tests。manifest原始字段写Phase0 candidate/review pending/activation pending、Phase1与Phase2均planned且entry eligible=false、Phase3–7同样false、accepted Tn=null、G(e) absent；derived `phase0_candidate_awaiting_fresh_review=true`，所有Phase1+ action grants=false。H0 changed paths排除plan且plan identity等于accepted Ck。E(e>0)-H0另冻结前epoch Z、handoff chain、runtime baseline与cross-epoch evidence continuity；旧authority/PASS不carry。
4. Hn-review：另一名 fresh、作者分离、只读 reviewer 只审 exact Hn；reviewer 不写仓库，也不修 candidate。其 payload 必须绑定 exact Hn commit/tree/parent/changed paths/manifest digests，并给出 `PASS 0/0/0/*` 或不可变 FAIL findings。
5. Tn/Fn：`phase0_review_terminal_receipt_materialization_write` 的本 attempt one-shot instance只能在current `pre_g_transition_snapshot_v1`完整且total reducer按exact review class选择`Hn_no_external_event_to_Tn|Hn_implementation_finding_to_Fn|Hn_material_finding_to_Fn_then_Z`之一时，把 exact external Hn review payload 的原始字节未经修改地嵌入 6.4.1 定义的 canonical `lifecycle_envelope_v1`；独立post-head material/stop优先改由Z，T/F grant=false。envelope metadata、canonical body hash 与后继绑定的完整 raw-file/Git-blob digest 均按 6.4.1 计算，不能声称 envelope 与 external payload 逐字节相等。PASS 时创建 Tn terminal PASS receipt commit：logical parent=Hn；无中间I时Git parent=Hn，有连续I时Git parent=latest I，changed path只有fixed Tn receipt；它在同一不可变aggregate envelope中绑定Hn review、aggregate result-review与terminal PASS语义，但不激活下游。FAIL时同理创建Fn terminal FAIL receipt：logical parent=Hn、Git parent=current materialization parent、changed path只有fixed Fn receipt；不得修复Hn或改写reviewer findings。prepared T/F都以current materialization parent做lease-guarded exact-expected-old remote fast-forward CAS；每个action instance物化前重算source high-watermark，remote接受后立即`consumed`并只做exact local fast-forward reconciliation。
6. Sn 与 H(n+1)：只有 Fn 后且finding被registry分类为Hn implementation/fixture/manifest allowlist内可修、current `pre_g_transition_snapshot_v1`完整且total reducer依次输出`Fn_implementation_no_event_to_Sn`与`Sn_no_event_to_H(n+1)`时，`phase0_successor_authority_receipt_materialization_write` 的本 attempt instance 才能创建 one-path Sn，随后才可创建H(n+1)；任一head后的material/authorized-stop使normal successor grant失效并改走Z。Sn logical parent=Fn，H(n+1) logical parent=Sn；各自Git parent均由物化当时的`pre_g_control_head_v1.materialization_parent_ref`决定，并以该remote parent做lease-guarded exact-expected-old fast-forward CAS。Sn payload必须绑定exact failed Hn、exact review/findings、非零P0/P1/P2、exact H(n+1) changed-path allowlist、plan deny/immutability、无downstream/Phase1 grant；H(n+1)只改allowlist、保留Hn/Fn/Sn、plan blob/raw SHA-256/bytes不变。若finding证明accepted plan的行为/门/权限/接口本身需要变化，Fn后必须走下述pre-activation epoch governance，Sn/H(n+1) grant=false；两条分支互斥，历史Hn/Fn永不覆盖。
7. G(e)：仅当canonical recovery lineage的Hn产生exact Tn terminal PASS，且current `pre_g_transition_snapshot_v1`绑定本计划内嵌的exact finite seed、logical head=Tn、remote canonical ref=`pre_g_control_head_v1.materialization_parent_ref`、complete continuous per-source receipt sequences、pending/uncovered-integrity=[]并由total reducer唯一输出`Tn_no_event_to_G`时，one-shot action才可能创建fixed epoch-scoped G(e)。pre-G event receipt与H/T/F/S/Hnext/G/Z全部使用`phase0_remote_ref_acceptance_v1`：先创建不移动任何canonical local/tracking ref的prepared commit object，随后对同一`origin/refs/heads/codex/fin013-dell-s1-s2-product-bridge`执行expected-remote-parent的lease-guarded exact-expected-old fast-forward CAS；remote ref接受exact commit是唯一linearization point，竞争者CAS失败并从新remote head重算。G candidate的logical parent=Tn、Git parent=current materialization parent且one changed path；G remote acceptance full ref就是pre-G direct-Z与post-G rollover的唯一route boundary，不再创建自研global sequencer、route token、pending-token状态或第17类action。G prepared object、local object存在、push请求发出、客户端超时或本地ref都不构成boundary或authority；remote结果未知时只读查询exact remote ref：仍为expected materialization parent则prepared G无效并可从同一current事实重算，等于exact G则boundary已成立并只允许本地exact fast-forward reconciliation，其他值则重读其canonical ancestry：合法竞争transition则按新head重算，foreign/非法ref才进入预注册integrity-control，任何情况下不得任选。remote接受G后本地canonical branch及origin-tracking只允许向exact remote G做幂等fast-forward；在local/tracking aligned前Phase1/2 candidate eligibility仍false，但严格后到并由remote canonical ancestry接受的event已经属于post-G rollover。remote拒绝或仍在expected parent时canonical local branch始终不得预先移动，prepared orphan不在canonical universe且不阻断event receipt/direct-Z；不得reset、unconstrained force-push、ref delete/recreate、把orphan冒充G或删除失败证据。G payload绑定accepted Ck/review、Hn/Tn、完整H/F/S/I chain、snapshot/reducer、logical predecessor、expected remote materialization parent/ref、canonical body/external-payload digest、fixed output path（自己的output commit/tree/blob/raw/full ref明确禁止进入body/grant；只由同endpoint remote readback与独立post-materialization verification记录）、历史不变和plan identity。对每个`0<=i<n`，canonical logical lineage必须恰有Fi、Si、H(i+1)且不得有Ti；最终Hn必须恰有Tn且不得有sibling Fn；整条canonical logical lineage恰有一个T terminal。旧/失败/superseded/跨epoch Cj或Tn、双terminal、缺Sn、跳号、merge/tracking drift/断链、I漏绑/换序、同一expected parent被多个remote accepted successor占用均fail closed。G(e)只按phase-entry registry令Phase1/2 candidate eligibility=true；两phase仍planned，P1 audit与RSH network grants均false。G(e)从不签PASS，也不冒充execution authority。
8. Phase 1 的 P1-01 至 P1-15 每个 action-bearing ticket 都独立提交：applicability evaluation（条件票）→ candidate contract → read-only candidate review → execution-authority receipt → remote-accepted primary start claim → bounded audit read/write → exact artifact publication（有repo输出时）→ frozen result → read-only result review → terminal receipt → 必要的 downstream-activation receipt。`phase_entry_eligible`或applicability只允许提出candidate，execution authority只允许claim竞争；remote claim acceptance前不得审计，downstream activation必须后于terminal PASS；失败ticket不覆盖。
9. 后续每个 action-bearing release slice 使用同样拓扑：applicability evaluation（条件票）→ candidate contract → candidate review → execution-authority receipt → remote-accepted primary start claim＋initial primary route choice＋frozen typed operation profile＋每次非query调用的one-shot operation intent＋prearmed tagged compensation/recovery member set＋适用时独立safety-suboperation choice → implementation/run → ticket-bound artifact publication（适用时）→ frozen result → result review → terminal receipt → 可选 downstream-activation receipt。primary choice、operation intent、safety choice与member仍是该既有 substantive action 的子协议，不新增 control action。
10. 大型组件安装和 run artifact 不进 Git；Git 只保存 lock、manifest、digest、license/SBOM 结论、测试代码和有界结果。
11. 不为每个小状态创建独立 current manifest。维护一个 canonical program manifest，加 append-only applicability/candidate/candidate-review/execution-authority/result/result-review/terminal/downstream-activation receipts；任何 PASS 都不能在被审 candidate 中自我声明。

Phase 0 的“current/accepted lineage”只在明确的 canonical universe 中判断，不试图证明整个 Git object database 或其他 refs 没有 sibling。H0 machine contract 必须冻结 canonical branch、对应 local origin-tracking ref、expected head、receipt namespace 和以下activated-success single-parent、non-merge grammar：

~~~text
C0 -> C1 -> ... -> Ck(accepted) -> (pre-G I*) -> H0 -> ((pre-G I*) -> Fi -> (pre-G I*) -> Si -> (pre-G I*) -> H(i+1))* -> (pre-G I*) -> Tn -> (pre-G I*) -> G0
~~~

另有互斥pre-activation terminal grammar，由6.3的`pre_g_canonical_head_transition_table_v1`唯一裁决：Hn review自身发现material-plan问题时是`Hn→Fn(material)→Z(e)=superseded`；accepted Ck后、或Hn/Fn/Sn/Tn logical head之后收到独立新material event时先remote-accept I，再由latest I经独立review写positive Z；authorized stop同理写`Z(t)=stopped`；irrecoverable integrity写I/review/`Z=failed`。同一Fn/same finding不得同时产生Sn与Z；历史Sn之后到达的distinct event可从以Sn为logical head的latest I写Z。remote G一旦accepted只能走Q rollover，不能再走pre-activation。

在每个 epoch 的 H0 前，E0-C0.parent必须是historical seed；E(e>0)-C0保存`epoch_plan_lineage_seed_ref=Z(e-1)`，Git parent取`successor_pre_activation_control_head_v1`（Z或latest handoff I）。Cj 从0连续到k、plan-only/non-merge，Ck是同epoch唯一latest exact-review PASS。H0 logical parent必须Ck，Git parent按`pre_g_control_head_v1`为Ck或latest连续pre-G I。activated-success分支中，每个H/F/S/T/G的logical chain仍按index唯一，actual Git chain允许registry-valid连续I夹在相邻logical objects之间；remote CAS expected parent永远是current materialization parent。G prepared前remote ref必须精确为以Tn为logical head的materialization parent，local/origin tracking与该remote ref aligned；G logical parent=Tn，Git parent为Tn或latest I。i从0连续到n-1，每个i<n恰有Fi、Si、H(i+1)且无Ti；最终Hn恰有Tn且无Fn；整链恰一T。pre-activation分支由head/review/empty-authority证明验证。任一I漏绑/换序/foreign、logical/Git parent混用、Hi的Ti＋Fi、多个T、缺Fi/Si、old T、same-Fn Sn＋Z、remote accepted Z＋G、index/epoch不一致或merge均拒绝；orphan objects无authority且不得为过测试删除。validator参数化e/k/n及pre/post-Z I count各0/1/2/9/10，并覆盖无I与有I的logical/Git parent向量、E0→Z0→E1、Hn/Fn→Z、historical Sn→distinct-event Z、negative Z、seed/parent错位、stale/gap/foreign-I、跨epoch、baseline/seed冒充Cj、旧Cj PASS、latest未review、tracking drift、duplicate terminal、断链与跳号。

### 6.3 计划修订

本计划可修正，但需同时满足：

- 新证据写入 issue/capability ledger；
- 说明原假设、证据、影响、选择与 rollback；
- 计划合同版本仅在行为/门/接口变化时递增；
- H0尚未创建且该epoch尚无accepted Ck时，计划行为、门、权限或接口变化必须形成连续的下一plan-only `C(j+1)`并重新接受exact review；一旦Ck accepted，即使H0尚未创建，material change也必须用pre-activation positive Z关闭本epoch，再从next epoch C0重修，不能在同epoch创建第二个PASS C。任何plan change都不得夹带在Hn、Tn/Fn、Sn、G或普通phase/ticket commit；
- H0已创建但G(e)尚不存在时，如必须改变计划行为、门、权限或接口，只能按`pre_g_canonical_head_transition_table_v1`从current Ck/Hn/Fn/Sn/Tn head形成作者分离reviewed pre-activation Z；不得创建需要G(e)的rollover A/Q，也不得为取得Q而先激活已知失效的G。只有G(e)已经存在时，才必须完整执行下述`PROGRAM-EPOCH-ROLLOVER→Q→Z`；两段都不能把后插入plan commit塞进既有lineage，也不能只写一句stopped/superseded假装旧grants已失效；
- 纯运行重试只增加 rN，不增加合同版本；
- 涉及产品范围、发布含义、付费规模、安全或不可逆动作时再次向 Owner 报告；
- 已完成/失败的历史证据不回写。

accepted Ck前仍按同一epoch连续plan-only Cj修订；Ck接受后不再追加同epochC。Ck接受后至G(e)前的普通合同修正保持同一`program_id`并按pre-G head table以pre-activation Z开启下一authority epoch；G(e)后则必须走rollover/Q/Z。只有产品范围、Phase 0–7 含义、核心domain-authority不变量或completion definition根本变化时，才把当前program终止并由Owner另建新program。独立新program也不能跳过已激活旧epoch的quiescence/revocation；从未激活的旧epoch仍须按其bootstrap/empty-authority Z诚实收口。

为消除accepted Ck后、H0/G前以及`between_epochs/plan_revisioning/phase0_governance`中的治理真空，本计划冻结`pre_activation_epoch_bootstrap_bundle_v4`与`pre_activation_epoch_governance_v1`。bundle不是旧epoch authority carry，也不授任何product/domain/substantive action；它只预承诺pre-activation Z、post-Z event handoff和H0-less negative-Z之后的`PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01`控制闭环，并复用既有`authority_epoch_terminal_receipt_materialization_write`、generic lifecycle actions与`ticket_applicability_receipt_materialization_write` special variants，不新增第17类control action。E0 bundle只能在unique latest exact-review PASS的E0-Ck及其review存在后纯导出；E0尚无accepted Ck时程序仍是未接受proposal，全部动作false，Owner withdrawal只允许追加一张plan-only withdrawal revision并停止队列，不产生Z/ready/program terminal，也不能自签一个尚未接受的root。E(t>0) bundle只由前一epoch唯一positive `Z(t-1)=superseded` full ref、其显式successor ID与下述static schema纯导出。

bundle中的`pre_activation_epoch_reservation_v1`固定`schema_version/program_id/target_epoch_id/predecessor_positive_Z_full_ref_or_E0_accepted_Ck_full_ref+review/plan_path/static action IDs/role inequalities/path templates/guards/deny-surface/E0-bootstrap-seed/pre-G-event-receipt+integrity-intake/post-Z-destination-template/terminal-incident/terminal-sync/stale-sync-closure registry digests`并作RFC 8785 SHA-256。closed-world成员不是H0 author自由填写：本计划在下方逐字节冻结canonical `e0_pre_activation_bootstrap_seed_v4`；E0 accepted Ck exact review覆盖该preimage和expected digest，bootstrap只能copy/verify。E(e>0)只允许把前一positive Z full ref、epoch ID和由Z/template纯导出的namespace替换进同schema；source/stopper/principal/action/role/guard语义不得由H0或materializer选择。

`e0_pre_activation_bootstrap_seed_v4`的canonical preimage固定为下列单行UTF-8 JSON；键序、数组序、大小写和`/`均是identity，`expected_seed_sha256`不进入preimage。这里明确选择Git remote canonical branch作为唯一durable admission medium，不保留“endpoint或repo namespace”二选一：

`expected_seed_sha256=b81f9e514e2f22645cf31149f389a18e4c0069f15b2bf08bf12a57896574c05e`；其中`canonical_remote_identity`子对象的RFC 8785 SHA-256固定为`2b355a3fbf4118aa1915d026c8e4307279cd29d8976f4f28dd620f34fbd57ea9`。validator以原始code-block内容（不含换行与fence）重算seed SHA-256、以该子对象canonical bytes重算remote identity SHA，并要求exact equality；任何source/class-map增删换序、principal/namespace/branch/remote name、fetch/push URL、pushurl presence、repository tuple、common-dir/worktree、object format、refspec/upstream、transport/action/allowlist/expiry/role变化都先进入deterministic integrity I，normal grant=false，material change另走plan revision/new authority epoch，不能在H0注入或把同名`origin`指向另一repository。上述principal IDs是本program内稳定principal slots：Owner slot只接受当前Owner签名/明确指令绑定；其他slot在每次grant中另绑实际actor identity并执行不等式，不能用同一执行agent同时冒充reviewer/evaluator/materializer。

`git_exact_ref_lease_transport_mechanism_v1`是authority-neutral机制，只定义冻结endpoint/repository/ref、exact expected-old、direct-parent/fast-forward、unknown-result readback、Git object closure transfer与local reconciliation；它本身不授control、artifact、research、Provider、模型、外源、package或domain authority。E0 seed预先冻结的`git_control_transport_v1`是该机制的control profile，也是Phase 0唯一网络profile；后续只能由valid governance/lifecycle/start-claim transition grant消费，changed paths只能是对应exact control paths。每次snapshot、grant、prepared-object push、unknown-result readback及local reconciliation前后都重新解析seed中的remote identity projection；fetch URL与push URL必须逐字节等于冻结normalized值，`pushurl`必须absent并按Git规则等于fetch URL，repository tuple/common-dir/worktree/object-format/refspec/upstream也必须一致。control profile只可访问该endpoint的该branch ref；为验证canonical ancestry或创建direct child，可传输该ref所需的最小Git object closure，但materializer不得把其中substantive blob bytes读取、导出或当作研究/Evidence/domain input，control grant也绝不能发布substantive artifact。

`lease_guarded_fast_forward_expected_old_v1`不把普通non-force push冒充CAS。prepared transition必须先证明`new.parents=[expected_materialization_parent]`、tree/body/path合法，再用同一个remote ref transaction原子要求`old_oid==expected_materialization_parent`且`new_oid==prepared_transition_commit`。reference implementation可使用exact `--force-with-lease=<canonical_branch_ref>:<expected_materialization_parent>`传输prepared direct child，但这里仅允许“exact lease条件下仍为fast-forward”的update：任何`--force`、空lease、different expected OID、non-fast-forward、ref delete/recreate或其他ref永远禁止。branch missing、ancestor rewind、foreign history或lease mismatch都不能因`A→N`仍是普通fast-forward而接受，统一进入integrity intake。push结果未知时只从同一冻结endpoint/repository/ref readback：exact new=accepted；exact expected old=not accepted/recompute；另一个registry-valid direct child=competing transition/recompute；missing、ancestor rewind、foreign/non-direct history或endpoint drift=deterministic integrity I。先读后普通push不满足本协议；transport receipt必须保存resolved remote identity digest、expected old/new OID、direct-parent proof、lease invocation digest与readback classification。

H0 registry预先冻结共享的 `substantive_repository_artifact_publication_v1` tagged-union profile。authority basis 恰好二选一：`ordinary_g_ticket` 分支要求 same-epoch G(e) remote accepted/aligned、selector=`active`、ordinary ticket candidate-review PASS、独立 execution authority、remote-accepted primary claim 与 exact G root；`terminal_ready_finalization` 分支不要求 H0 或 G，但必须同时满足 selector.`mode=program-finalizing`、`program_terminal_ready_status_or_null=ready_complete|ready_partial|ready_stopped|ready_failed`、`program_terminal_status_or_null=null`，并绑定`PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01`、current terminal-ready root/observation/source-projection/incident snapshot、candidate-review PASS、独立 authority、remote-accepted primary claim，且只能消费下述 terminal 三路径 profile。final pair形成后 selector.`mode=program-terminal`，此时ready字段必须null、`program_terminal_status_or_null=complete|partial|stopped|failed`承载终局，所有sync publication grant均为false。两个 basis 同时成立、同时不成立、kind/root 不匹配或用历史 G 代替 finalization root 都 fail closed。profile 不是 standing network authority，也不是 control Action ID；`program_terminal_project_os_sync_write` 是既有 substantive action，不能计为第17个 control action。

ordinary 与 finalization 两个分支都逐字节绑定ticket/attempt/action/claim full ref与fence、canonical endpoint/ref、expected parent、direct-child commit、exact changed-path allowlist、content class、逐path blob/raw digest、maximum resulting bytes、materializer/commit-writer、expiry、idempotency、readback/result path和deny surface；artifact remote acceptance/full ref必须进入该票 result predecessor。ordinary 分支可发布各票已reviewed exact output；finalization 分支固定使用 `terminal_project_os_three_path_publication_v3`，governed output set恰为 `docs/project_os/capability_status_ledger.jsonl`、`docs/project_os/current_context_pack.zh-CN.md`、`docs/project_os/root_cause_issue_ledger.jsonl`，actual Git changed-path set必须是该三项的nonempty subset且max=3；未变化的governed path仍须对resulting bytes执行同一validator，不得为凑三条制造伪改动。resulting-size 上限依次为 4 MiB、2 MiB、4 MiB且总计不超过10 MiB。两个 JSONL 只能追加完整 UTF-8 行；context pack 只可投影 effective ready 与 immutable evidence refs。三份输出正文不得预写尚未产生的 final program terminal/full ref、publication grant digest、prepared commit OID或自身 future ref；accepted publication full ref 只进入后继 result。finalization的artifact surface另与`terminal_git_publication_adapter_effect_capability_profile_v3`分立绑定：后者冻结exact endpoint/repository/ref、expected-old direct-child、primary-choice/operation-intent/observation locators、internal retry=0、只允许query与first initiate、其余四operation=false及total readback classification；control transport不得冒充该substantive adapter。

H0-backed 与 H0-less terminal sync 共享完全相同的 finalization profile、Git adapter capability、action、path、role 和 digest 语义，唯一允许不同的是 static source kind=`H0_REGISTRY|PRE_ACTIVATION_BUNDLE`。finalization profile 仍必须走 candidate→作者分离 review→独立 authority→remote-accepted exact primary claim→initial primary choice→generation-0 publication operation intent→exact 三路径 publication/readback→result→作者分离 result review→terminal；它不产生 plan/source worklog/R14、research、package、model、Evidence、S2/S3、migration、cutover、product publication/release 或 index-delete 权限。control-only claim发布substantive blob、research-network grant移动Git ref、artifact profile跨path/content-class/ref/remote、缺choice/intent、未accepted/stale claim publication、任意clone/fetch另一repo/ref或用Git bytes充当研究/Provider/domain truth全部fail closed。artifact commit与R/I/Q/start竞争、push unknown/readback、intervening canonical transition及local reconciliation仍使用同一exact-lease/ancestry规则；后继control materializer可以取得验证parent所需的opaque Git object closure，但不能代替substantive executor上传产物或语义消费其内容。compact authority namespace 不因该source kind分叉：两类都按`compact_authority_namespace_key_derivation_v2`只hash exact `pre_activation_reservation_root_digest+program_id+authority_epoch_id+compact_lifecycle_path_schema_sha256`，`static_contract_source_kind`只作provenance evidence；H0/future Z/ready/sync/self均排除。

该bundle原始单行的`expected_e0_bootstrap_bundle_preimage_sha256=b77cb0c7e34d89c93c55e17985cea536fabab86e07ee072cb7d45db27b7fce68`。reservation与stable root也不允许materializer自由组装；下面两条canonical template把尚未存在的runtime full refs保留为typed literal token，因此template本身可在Ck前审阅而不把future commit写进自身： `compact_lifecycle_path_schema_sha256=52a3150a12dc028e7b634840f7a28895780b7c3a1c7c5bcd6516551f70340332`；bundle内synthetic E0/E1 authority-key分别为`1a9f07665f4fabba709661736e632249b1776cf4a06dd53db0cd4d4361b46016`与`3e77e8410e977beae3c281695ef91b8dcd4d1e62e4dd1573a34384e3b467d695`，其完整root/target/claim/operation-generation/safety-generation/path inputs逐字节内嵌；E0/E1均冻结action-start=232、primary-choice=167、operation-intent=189、operation-observation=189、safety-choice=189 UTF-16 units，全部不超过240。

~~~json
{"accepted_plan_review_full_ref":"$ACCEPTED_PLAN_REVIEW_FULL_REF","accepted_plan_revision_full_ref":"$ACCEPTED_PLAN_REVISION_FULL_REF","bootstrap_bundle_preimage_sha256":"b77cb0c7e34d89c93c55e17985cea536fabab86e07ee072cb7d45db27b7fce68","bootstrap_seed_sha256":"b81f9e514e2f22645cf31149f389a18e4c0069f15b2bf08bf12a57896574c05e","canonical_remote_identity_sha256":"2b355a3fbf4118aa1915d026c8e4307279cd29d8976f4f28dd620f34fbd57ea9","derivation_mode":"$DERIVATION_MODE","derived_namespace_prefix":"$DERIVED_NAMESPACE_PREFIX","post_z_destination_contract_template_sha256":"9ca4ca6afc030773c36699cf3b7a26b9056ca2d567b436422d8378bd9f916c10","predecessor_authority_full_ref":"$PREDECESSOR_AUTHORITY_FULL_REF","program_id":"fin_0_1_3_product_wide_architecture_rebase_20260830","schema_version":"pre_activation_epoch_reservation_preimage_template_v1","substitution_algorithm":"exact_token_substitution_then_rfc8785_sha256_v1","target_epoch_id":"$TARGET_EPOCH_ID","target_epoch_index":"$TARGET_EPOCH_INDEX"}
~~~

`expected_pre_activation_reservation_template_sha256=55aead4b6bd5ae4264db36021d9d4599c12c93a8806cbb6558c3e505d1f9cef5`。

~~~json
{"bootstrap_bundle_preimage_sha256":"b77cb0c7e34d89c93c55e17985cea536fabab86e07ee072cb7d45db27b7fce68","bootstrap_seed_sha256":"b81f9e514e2f22645cf31149f389a18e4c0069f15b2bf08bf12a57896574c05e","deny_surface_source":"bootstrap_bundle_preimage_v4","guard_source":"bootstrap_bundle_preimage_v4","program_id":"fin_0_1_3_product_wide_architecture_rebase_20260830","reservation_digest":"$RESERVATION_DIGEST","schema_version":"pre_activation_reservation_root_preimage_template_v1","static_action_subject_path_role_source":"bootstrap_bundle_preimage_v4"}
~~~

`expected_pre_activation_root_template_sha256=f86032168f90351c2e292322a29e8de4f774afd22188b84c92f0b5acdd928fdc`。runtime算法严格为：验证上述四个static hashes→按mode替换全部且仅允许的tokens→对替换后reservation RFC8785 bytes算`reservation_digest`→只替换root template中的`$RESERVATION_DIGEST`→算stable root。E0 accepted-plan mode要求actual Ck/review full refs、`DERIVATION_MODE=E0_ACCEPTED_PLAN`、target=`E0/0`、predecessor=Ck；E(e>0)在新Ck尚不存在的successor-preplan mode要求accepted-plan两字段均为literal `null_external_payload_v1`、predecessor=前一positive Z full ref、namespace由该Z＋epoch index纯导出。accepted Ck只进入每次dynamic grant，不改successor reservation root。

E0→E1固定替换test vector使用synthetic predecessor `sha1:1111111111111111111111111111111111111111`、target=`E1/1`、mode=`SUCCESSOR_PREPLAN`、namespace=`configs/audits/fin_0_1_3_product_wide_architecture_rebase/authority_epochs/epoch-1`、两个accepted-plan字段=`null_external_payload_v1`；替换后reservation SHA-256必须为`da05caf4c50646ce781783a74848701f9c47ad92ee535e4d210122e65ab18e3d`，再代入root template所得root SHA-256必须为`ced9904b196d0731fe5025b741835448337116c4474d61053aba607eab392d54`。漏token、额外替换、不同array/path/source、H0首次注入成员、same input导出另一digest全部invalid。任何字段、数组顺序或literal token变化都必须重做plan-only review。

runtime reservation只能由上述exact reservation template、seed/bundle/post-Z hashes和mode-bound token替换算法产生；它因此逐字节冻结target-epoch `PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01` stable subject/attempt template、compact paths、generic action subsets、`program_terminal_project_os_sync_write`三路径allowlist、non-self-referential `post_z_destination_contract_template_v2`、all-ready incident source/subject/intake、terminal-ready observation/source-projection与stale-sync closure schemas及deny surface，不能另从自然语言补值。`pre_activation_reservation_root_v1`只hash替换后的reservation digest以及exact seed/bundle中static subject/action/schema/roles/path-template/guard/deny digests，不含current head/tree、selector mode、request/review、future Z、actual future destination root或grant自身；同一reservation永远只有一个root。每次`pre_activation_transition_grant_v1`才绑定stable root、exact selector mode、remote canonical parent commit/tree、accepted Ck/H/F/S/T history、request/review/current `pre_g_transition_snapshot_v1`/empty-authority/runtime refs、fixed target/path、expected changed paths、expiry/one-shot。Cj/head/mode/source receipt high-watermark变化只使旧grant失效并重算新grant，不改变reservation/root/target/path。H0若存在只能核验并逐字节物化同一bundle，不能成为bundle唯一来源、选择source/path/action或重定义它。

固定pre-activation terminal subject=`E{t}-PREACT-Z0`、path=`configs/repository/fin_0_1_3_product_wide_architecture_rebase/authority_epochs/epoch-{t}/epoch_terminal.json`，每个reserved epoch最多一个Z。`ticket_applicability_receipt_materialization_write`增加bootstrap-root下不可与A/interrupt/post-Z/terminal-I互换的`transition_subject_kind=pre_g_governance_event` special variant；它不生成applicability verdict，只把exact seed中某source的signed/deterministic external payload嵌入`pre_g_event_receipt_v1`，写该source的`.../pre_g_intake/{source_id}/I{sequence}.json`单一路径，并通过`phase0_remote_ref_acceptance_v1`让receipt commit与H/T/F/S/Hnext/G/Z竞争同一expected remote parent。per-source sequence从0连续；global order只取remote canonical single-parent ancestry，不再另写global ordinal ledger。external payload的wall-clock/received_at只作证据，不是route boundary；remote receipt acceptance才是program-authority linearization。已知待物化payload必须出现在snapshot的`pending_external_payloads`并令normal successor false；遗漏源、伪造`pending=[]`、同parent sibling、本地orphan或未被remote接受的payload都不能授权。

每次pre-G control grant先纯生成`pre_g_transition_snapshot_v1`：绑定exact seed preimage/digest、accepted Ck/review full ref、current remote canonical parent commit/tree、逐source start sequence/current连续remote-accepted receipt high-watermark、按ancestry排序的完整event receipt refs/digests、signed `pending_external_payloads` attestation、sorted material/authorized-stop/unsupported/forged/integrity sets、recoverable missing sequence与`uncovered_governance_anomaly_set_v1`、duplicate/replay/completeness flags、evaluator raw payload/full digest、remote-ref readback receipt、observation UTC、expiry、one-shot generation与reducer input digest。`pending_external_payloads`只扫描exact seed的governance-event sources；当前exact Hn terminal-review payload属于专用`review_class`输入，明确不进入event registry/pending集合，并由T/F transition逐字节绑定。review payload若还夹带与exact Hn judgment无关的Owner新change/stop或其他独立治理事实，该独立部分必须由相应seed source形成event receipt，不能借review字段偷渡。只写空数组而漏seed source/receipt/known governance payload或缺连续性证明invalid；prepared/local-only objects不进入snapshot。grant消费前remote parent/tree或任一source accepted/pending high-watermark变化都使旧snapshot/grant失效；正常transition与event-intake都以相同expected remote parent CAS，故竞争只接受一个。

`pre_g_canonical_head_transition_table_v1`是以`(selector_mode,current_remote_logical_head,review_class,pre_g_transition_snapshot,event_class,event_identity,governance_stream_integrity_state)`为输入的total互斥reducer，优先级固定为irrecoverable integrity＞authorized stop＞material change＞normal successor：①无accepted Ck的plan-review finding→C(j+1)；②accepted Ck＋complete no-event→H0；③Hn exact terminal review PASS＋no独立governance event→Tn；④Hn exact terminal review的implementation/fixture/manifest finding＋no独立governance event→Fn，Fn＋no event→Sn，Sn＋no event→H(n+1)；⑤Hn exact terminal review自身material-plan finding→Fn(material)→positive Z且Sn forbidden；⑥accepted Ck或任一Hn/Fn/Sn/Tn current head收到独立`material_plan_change`，或Project-OS source的`mandatory_completeness_failure`，或valid signed但本epoch不支持的schema/class经作者分离material-governance review确认→positive Z并完整handoff原payload；⑦有bundle的任一pre-G head收到registry-authorized `program_stop`→negative Z stopped；⑧Tn＋complete no-event→prepared-G remote CAS；⑨material＋authorized stop→stop且无successor；⑩recoverable local sequence gap→`pre-g-integrity-intake`，只开放exact missing receipt；forged/stale/expired stopper→deterministic rejected-control-input I，证明未进入authorized-stop set后重算；repository source的`integrity_failure|source_registry_drift`、unknown source/class、duplicate/replay、registry drift或不可恢复corruption→deterministic `governance_stream_integrity_failure_v1` I＋作者分离review＋negative Z failed，无successor；⑪remote canonical ref已接受valid G→direct-Z false、仅PROGRAM-EPOCH-ROLLOVER。exact allowed-domain mapping固定为`material_plan_change→material`、`mandatory_completeness_failure→material`、`program_stop→authorized_stop`、`integrity_failure|source_registry_drift→irrecoverable_integrity`；current exact Hn review只进入review_class，不进入event_class。任何seed allowed class没有唯一normalized class、同一payload同时冒充review/event、或review主体不等于current Hn却走T/F都invalid。invalid状态不再是“all grants false”吸收态；它只关闭normal H/T/F/S/Hnext/G/positive successor，同时开放上述exact intake/rejection/failed-terminal special path。same-Fn/same-finding sibling Sn＋Z拒绝，但historical Sn→later distinct event/stop Z合法。H0/T/F/S/Hnext/G/Z/event-I dynamic grants必须逐字节消费相同version reducer的唯一输出，任一remote expected parent最多接受一个canonical successor。

`governance_stream_integrity_failure_v1`复用现有`ticket_applicability_receipt_materialization_write`的strict I variant与`authority_epoch_terminal_receipt_materialization_write`的failed-Z variant，不新增action ID。其canonical anomaly ID绑定stream kind=`pre_g|q_interrupt|positive_post_z`、source、expected/actual local sequence、remote ancestry/current head、raw evidence、first observation、seed/registry digest和recoverability classification。recoverable wait只开exact missing-I；不可恢复异常先物化唯一integrity I并由作者分离review确认，随后：pre-G直接从latest integrity control head写negative Z failed；Q允许以`integrity_anomaly_closure_complete=true`替代正常`pending_received_events=[]`并写Z failed；positive post-Z先写successor-intake integrity I，再由已预留的successor epoch从latest handoff head写H0-less negative Z failed。forged stopper只写rejected I，不建立stop latch且在proof完整后可恢复no-event；valid stop＋integrity同时出现时integrity failed优先。I/failed-Z只授权控制证据与terminal，不授H0/G/C0/successor/product/domain action。duplicate anomaly I、recovery replay、错误stream root/path/actor、无独立review或把failed分支改成positive均拒绝。

每个pre-activation Z只消费作者分离、只读`pre_activation_epoch_governance_review_payload_v1`，绑定exact remote logical/current canonical head/tree、current `pre_g_transition_snapshot_v1`与total reducer output、event/anomaly class/identity/full ref、exact seed/source/stopper authority、reservation/root/grant full preimage、target epoch、accepted Ck/review（适用时）、H/F/S/T历史、请求actor/scope/time、runtime baseline和完整empty-authority proof。normal positive/stopped Z要求stream complete；failed-Z要求unique deterministic integrity I、作者分离review及`integrity_anomaly_closure_complete=true`，不能要求原始异常从历史消失。validator证明remote canonical G(t)从未被接受、ordinary/substantive grant universe/action-start为空、writer/job/unknown side effect/unresolved delete为空、没有Q/ordinary ticket/产品route变化；authorized stop命中exact seed stopper。reviewer不同于event actor/stopper、plan/Hn author、snapshot evaluator、materializer和commit writer。Z也按`phase0_remote_ref_acceptance_v1`prepared并从current remote control head CAS：positive successor=E(t+1)，negative无successor。Z保存bundle/reservation、snapshot/reducer/event或anomaly/review、历史、empty-grant/runtime digests；不伪造Q、Phase0 PASS、G、ordinary terminal或产品动作。remote G已接受、无exact seed/bundle/empty proof、normal分支snapshot不complete、integrity分支未覆盖/未review、consume前remote head漂移、event/head组合不在表、root/target漂移、cross-epoch parent或negative Z带next均拒绝；prepared G orphan不等于G accepted，也不阻断合法Z。

每个activated epoch预注册conditional元票`PROGRAM-EPOCH-ROLLOVER`，stable ticket使用synthetic `phase_id=program-governance`，attempt=`E{e}-PROGRAM-EPOCH-ROLLOVER-C{a}`，不进入任何phase source/close denominator。其两个顶层trigger bit可同时为true而非互斥：

- `requested_change_trigger=true`：exact external governance request payload 的`request_class`为`plan_contract_change`或`program_stop`。前者还必须有`post_h0_plan_change_requested=true`且change class属于behavior/gate/permission/interface/security/material_plan_contract；后者必须绑定Owner或registry-authorized program stopper identity、reason/scope/time和current epoch/root，不直接产生program terminal；
- `completeness_failure_trigger = cleanup_index_post_terminal_completeness_failure OR phase_post_pass_completeness_failure`。前者要求same-epoch valid CLEANUP-INDEX terminal PASS、旧frontier/target/manifest digest、current canonical recomputation digest不等、exact evaluator payload及immutable refs；不要求所属Phase已PASS。后者要求same-epoch effective Phase PASS及其后exact denominator/completeness failure payload。无INDEX terminal、只有可变扫描文字、缺旧/新digest或未绑定canonical snapshot时两者均为false。

`program_governance_trigger_reducer_v1`在同一canonical snapshot分别输出`plan_change_requested`、`program_stop_requested`、`mandatory_completeness_failure`三个bit与一个不丢信息的class。bit顺序固定为`(plan,stop,mandatory)`，8行truth table恰为：`000→none`、`100→plan_change`、`010→program_stop`、`001→mandatory_completeness`、`110→plan_and_stop`、`101→plan_and_mandatory`、`011→mandatory_and_stop`、`111→plan_mandatory_and_stop`；任何unknown/第九种class都invalid。candidate/Q/Z/retry必须同时保存三个原始bit、完整R/I set和exact class，不能用class丢失正交证据。`current_rollover_required = plan_change_requested OR program_stop_requested OR mandatory_completeness_failure`。outcome优先级独立于class命名：任一unsafe/integrity→failed；否则任一stop bit→stopped；无stop且plan和/或mandatory clean closure→superseded。这里“mandatory优先”只表示完整性根因不能被普通plan/stop文字掩盖，不表示可以无视同一snapshot的停止意图并创建successor；`mandatory_and_stop|plan_mandatory_and_stop`必须同时保留修复责任与stop terminal intent。

raw trigger不能在A提交前只存在于内存。activated epoch复用`ticket_applicability_receipt_materialization_write`的`transition_subject_kind=program_governance_raw_trigger` special variant，把每个exact request/evaluator payload先物化为append-only `trigger/R{i}.json`；R不产生applicability verdict/candidate/action，只绑定source sequence、raw bits、input-set digest、previous R/I和G-root。R commit与ordinary lifecycle transition/substantive start-claim commit竞争同一expected remote parent并使用lease-guarded exact-expected-old remote fast-forward CAS：R先被remote接受后selector立即进入高优先级`program-governance-trigger-intake`，execution/ordinary/substantive=false，只开放exact A materialization；ordinary commit先接受则R必须基于新head重算，不能让一个parent接受两者。外部payload只有remote-accepted R才进入authority reducer；已知但尚未物化的payload列入pending并令ordinary/new substantive grant=false。只有remote-accepted start claim才算动作开始；因此raw trigger→A之间没有继续开产品动作的窗口，也不需要mutable pointer。

首个valid `PROGRAM-EPOCH-ROLLOVER` applicability A envelope把exact remote-accepted R、external evaluator payload、INDEX/phase refs、旧/新digests与request identity嵌入不可变receipt。`mandatory_completeness_latch_v1`与`program_stop_requested_latch_v1`都是epoch-scoped纯、单调reducer：只要任一valid R、initial/reevaluation/retry A或下述interrupt envelope证明对应bit曾为true即为true。二者不另写receipt、不增加action ID；撤回请求、旧validator恢复绿灯、后续raw bit=false、review FAIL、authority denied/held或新attempt都不能清除。只有Z(e)=superseded并进入新epoch，或negative Z形成terminal-ready并进入finalization，才结束该epoch latch作用域。每次candidate必须同时绑定current selector-selected applicable A、first-trigger R/A full refs、全部后续R/I refs、两个latch reducer version/input-set/digest，并分开记录`original_trigger_bits/current_trigger_bits/effective_trigger_class`，不得把latch冒充新的raw trigger。

`program_governance_entry_gate_v1`是pre-Q lifecycle入口，不是Phase1–7 phase-entry gate，并固定四种不可混用的transition mode：

1. `initial_applicability`：same-epoch G(e)已激活、program terminal/terminal-ready均null、无active rollover；selector mode=`program-governance-trigger-intake`且unique latest R/input-set未被A消费时可评估三类raw trigger；hold只接收program-stop或mandatory R，普通plan-change在hold中明确不适用。它唯一预分配新target的A0，`effective_rollover_required=current_rollover_required`；`active` mode本身不能直接写A0；
2. `reevaluate_applicability`：same target、candidate尚不存在、唯一valid A head存在、current remote-accepted R/input-set digest与head不同、previous A exact绑定且无sibling/parallel target时，才可追加A(a+1)。reevaluation期间selector仍为trigger-intake、所有ordinary/substantive grant=false。`effective_rollover_required=current_rollover_required OR mandatory_completeness_latch_v1 OR program_stop_requested_latch_v1`；无latch的普通plan request撤回可得到false，任一latch为true时head不得为false；
3. `continue_lifecycle`：只允许exact active target在selector-selected current applicable A后继续candidate→review→authority/Q，必须绑定同一target、first-trigger R/A、current R/A、interrupt/latch digest，且不得有未被A消费的新R/I或另一active rollover；
4. `retry_target`：只有前attempt已terminal才能预分配新target/A0；`effective_rollover_required=current_rollover_required OR mandatory_completeness_latch_v1 OR program_stop_requested_latch_v1`。A0绑定前terminal attempt、first-trigger A full ref和latch digest；无latch的普通plan-change已撤回时可not_applicable并回到active，不能强造retry candidate。

该gate加same-epoch governance root、prospective/current target、trigger stream、inputs与external evaluator payload后复用6.4.1八类generic lifecycle-control action schema；ordinary phase-entry gate不得冒充它。candidate还冻结旧root/plan、原假设/新证据/earliest layer、影响、contract version、plan path、grant universe、quiescence/rollback、runtime baseline交接与no-cross-epoch-authority。review FAIL、authority denied/held或pre-Q escalation走本attempt的immutable negative result→review→terminal；仅普通plan-change且无latch、所有R/A/I已闭合且current raw=false时旧epoch才可回到active，mandatory/stop latch存在时保持execution disabled并只能retry rollover治理。

candidate一旦存在，普通A-chain仍sealed。任何后到的plan-change修改/撤回、stop、mandatory或其input digest变化都走H0预注册的`program_governance_interrupt_gate_v1`，复用现有`ticket_applicability_receipt_materialization_write` action ID，不增加第17类action。I-chain是append-only evidence-set：首个新stop/mandatory可令对应bit`false→true`，plan-change可true→false/true但不得清除stop/mandatory latch；每张I都加入此前未出现的canonical `interrupt_event_id=SHA256(RFC8785(event_source_identity,event_source_sequence,received_at,evidence_sha256,trigger_bits,supersession_kind))`。event source sequence严格递增、previous-I连续、event set严格超集；duplicate/reorder/drop/shrink、同payload换ID或同ID换payload均拒绝。I使用`transition_subject_kind=program_governance_trigger_interrupt`和同一compact target namespace下连续`interrupt/I{i}.json`，绑定current target/candidate或Q full ref、previous R/I、exact evaluator payload、first-trigger R/A、root/epoch和deny surface，不属于A-chain、不改旧candidate、不授substantive action。candidate存在后的任一valid I立即进入`program-governance-candidate-supersession`，ordinary/substantive/Q/new-candidate=false；若candidate review尚无，由作者分离reviewer冻结`superseded_by_trigger` FAIL；review FAIL保持；review PASS/no-authority先写3.2 denial-only；authority denied/held消费原decision；authorized则按action-start撤销或真实收口。随后以exact review/decision＋完整R/I链、truthful action state形成negative/actual result→review→terminal stopped/failed；old terminal后`retry_target`才可消费全部event set。若仅plan-change已撤回且无latch，新attempt A0可exact not_applicable并回active；stop/mandatory latch仍强制applicable。不得从PASS review直接跳result、继续旧candidate签Q或把plan-change drift排除于interrupt。Q后Z前新event进入exact Q closure；Z后按terminal/successor intake归属。hold尚无target时由raw R触发initial applicability，不得用I凭空造target。

rollover candidate-review PASS 后，由与 author/reviewer/executor/request actor/applicability evaluator 分离的 issuer 签发 execution authority；其 commit 是 `quiescence_cutoff_ref=Q(e)`。该 authority 只能执行 `authority_epoch_quiesce_and_revoke`。Q receipt冻结complete grant universe并把它互斥分为：`started_primary_actions`、`revoked_unstarted_authorized_attempts`（已获authority但`primary_claim_state!=remote_accepted`）、尚未获authority的`candidate_or_review_only_attempts`、属于started attempt且已在其primary execution authority与remote-accepted primary start claim中原子预装的`prearmed_safety_suboperations`、以及必须frozen/revoked的其他grants。`started_primary_actions`只取Q前remote-accepted `substantive_action_start_claim_v5`；claim acceptance 本身已经恰好承诺一个 logical primary invocation，无论当前external effect仍无观测、in-flight、已知成功/失败或unknown都属于started，但这不自动授任何physical retry。execution authority存在但claim absent/prepared-unaccepted的attempt才可进入`revoked_unstarted_authorized_attempts`；prepared/local-only/timeout且readback未确认仍算unstarted。不得把未开始primary action、尚未获authority的candidate和started attempt的既有安全停止能力混为一类；前两组都不能开始动作，但只有已获authority组必须在Q内真实negative closure。仅有execution authority、candidate中的stop request或一个尚未remote接受的独立stop claim都不算prearmed member。

Q(e) full ref可用后，validator纯计算、不在Q对象内自指写入`q_closure_lifecycle_root_v1 = SHA256(RFC8785(Q full ref,current rollover candidate full ref,current rollover candidate-review full ref,current rollover execution-authority full ref,complete grant-universe digest,sorted q_closure_subject_set,per-subject allowed-action subsets,late-interrupt-gate/intake schema versions,deny-surface digest,whitelist version))`；不得引用Q时尚不存在的rollover result/review/terminal形成future-ref或自指。`q_closure_subject_set`恰为current exact rollover attempt、Q snapshot中的全部`started_primary_actions`所属ordinary attempts，以及全部`revoked_unstarted_authorized_attempts`；后续新增/漏项、candidate/review-only attempt、另一rollover均不得进入。现有generic action IDs使用Q-special authority-basis variant重算post-Q grant，因此action family总数仍为16。对started primary先按确定性priority分类：old terminal-sync attempt走`terminal_git_stale_no_retry_lifecycle_closure_v2`，同attempt只query/readback并non-PASS收口；IDX retirement family=`index_under_lock_recovery`；其余Q-started ordinary=`q_quiescence`。后两类消费各自physical safety member；terminal stale仍额外绑定Q root，但不得伪装为generic Q/IDX branch或双重命中。支持physical safety的分支只允许typed operation matrix中的query、具same-server-operation proof且赢得当前successor operation-intent的same-operation continuation/cancel，或exact prearmed compensation/recovery；`no_observation|effect_unknown`只query，`no_call_proven`不得重新primary。补偿member必须在claim body逐字节冻结action ID/adapter/target/fence/trigger/outcome/maximum-call及`safe_if_primary_not_invoked`，由独立safety-suboperation choice选中；实际compensation/recovery call还必须赢得自己的one-shot operation intent。它不是新action start、不创建第二claim、不补签authority。revoked-unstarted authorized attempt只可绑定`action_started=false`、exact Q revocation写negative result→result-review→terminal stopped；两组都不得retry、扩scope或downstream activate。

candidate/review-only attempt不获得Q closure action，而使用严格非自引用的两阶段pure reducer。Z前`candidate_nonterminal_freeze_completeness_v1`只消费Q full ref、latest Q closure head、Q时冻结的sorted exact candidate/review-only refs、每项stage、absence-of-authority/start proof、post-Q candidate/review/authority/start absence proof及set/count/order/schema digests；不含future Z。其valid preimage/digest是所有Z分支的共同前置与Z payload字段；漏项、加入Q后新对象、stage/authority/start分类翻转会使Z grant为空或形成explicit unsafe-integrity输入，绝不能靠future projection补齐。Z后`epoch_terminalized_nonterminal_projection_v1(freeze preimage/digest,Z full ref)`才逐项纯导出outcome：positive Z→`superseded_nonterminal`，stopped Z→`stopped_by_epoch_nonterminal`，failed Z→`blocked_by_failed_epoch_nonterminal`。它不另写receipt、不回写旧candidate、不伪造result/terminal，也绝不反向授权或阻止已经合法物化的Z；projection invalid只令current selector、next-plan或terminal-ready acceptance fail closed。current rollover只可物化自身result→result-review→terminal；Q后不再物化单独rollover downstream activation，`Z=superseded`自身是唯一next-plan activation。Q后的interrupt只作为当前rollover/Z closure-control input，不加入subject set；applicability/candidate/candidate-review/new execution-authority/new start、任何downstream activation、另一subject/rollover以及模型/网络/安装/迁移/删除/cutover/补签stop或rollback全部拒绝。Q后若存在accepted claim，physical operation仍严格服从六类typed matrix、当前非query operation-intent winner及exact-one safety branch；默认只能query，同一已选server operation的continue/complete/cancel除冻结capability和readback proof外还须各自的successor operation intent，补偿/恢复须exact prearmed member、独立safety-choice winner和实际调用自己的operation intent。它们都不能被解释为Q后new start，logical primary invocation总数仍严格等于1；lifecycle result一旦冻结，全部physical operation关闭。

`authority_epoch_grant_issuance_status=frozen`精确定义为冻结G-root的新ordinary/substantive/initiation grant，绝不禁止上述Q-root closure-control transition。每个Q-special preimage必须绑定Q commit/tree/blob/raw ref、cutoff grant-universe/subject-set digest、per-subject class、`action_started_at_Q`（只从Q前remote-accepted start claim投影，不能由日志/结果倒推）、revocation ref、fixed path、exact predecessor和whitelist version；started、revoked-unstarted、candidate-only三类互换，使用旧G-root、错误Q/subject或错误action subset一律fail closed。canonical branch一出现Q(e)即先fail closed，只有Q commit/push/local tracking aligned后才能使用该pure Q-root。

quiescence result必须逐项冻结complete grant universe、revoked-unstarted authorized negative closure、已开始terminal/safe-stop、`candidate_nonterminal_freeze_completeness_v1` preimage/digest、post-cutoff starts、in-flight/unknown completion、active writer/transaction/service/job、unresolved delete、实际current routes/data/dependencies/side effects。candidate-review或authority在Q(e)前失败时只终结当前rollover attempt：未latch的requested change可回到active，mandatory latch则保持active-invalidated并允许新rollover attempt；二者都不得凭pre-Q失败直接制造Z。Q closure维护`latest_q_closure_head_v1`，它是Q之后canonical single-parent branch上最后一个valid started/revoked-unstarted/current-rollover closure或I receipt commit。Z写入前冻结`interrupt_intake_snapshot_v1`：逐source最高连续sequence、sorted accepted event IDs/evidence digests、pending IDs、cutoff UTC、snapshot parent/tree，以及`governance_stream_integrity_state=complete|recoverable_wait|irrecoverable_open|integrity_failure_closed`。normal stopped/superseded要求`complete`且pending=[]；recoverable wait只开exact missing-I；irrecoverable_open只开deterministic integrity I/review；唯一I覆盖全部anomaly后成为`integrity_failure_closed`，即使原始pending/gap evidence继续不可变保留，也允许failed-Z而不允许stopped/superseded。`authority_epoch_terminal_receipt_materialization_write`只对已存在、已push且tracking aligned的Q closure及`complete|integrity_failure_closed` intake开放下列互斥分支，且一个epoch最多一个fixed Z path；Z commit必须是`latest_q_closure_head_v1`的direct single-parent child：

- 先算`z_failed = integrity_failure_closed OR unsafe_integrity_evidence OR unknown/in_flight/writer/job/post_cutoff_start/unresolved_delete非空 OR quiescence/result-review FAIL OR rollover terminal=failed OR 任一started subject未terminal/safe-stop OR 任一revoked-unstarted authorized subject未terminal stopped`。若输入是未闭合的incomplete/unknown而尚无deterministic I，Z仍不可写；只有anomaly closure完整时成立并只写negative `Z=failed`，activation/successor/next-plan=null/not_applicable；
- 再算`z_stopped = NOT z_failed AND (program_stop_requested_latch_v1=true OR rollover terminal=stopped)`。成立时必须先证明其余安全closure全部满足，再只写negative `Z=stopped`，activation/successor/next-plan=null/not_applicable；即便stop I在rollover result-review/terminal PASS之后到达，也直接由Z消费latest I/high-watermark，不覆盖旧terminal；
- 最后算`z_superseded = NOT z_failed AND NOT z_stopped AND governance_stream_integrity_state=complete AND pending=[] AND unknown/in_flight/writer/job/post_cutoff_start/unresolved_delete=[] AND grant universe complete AND全部unstarted primary grants revoked AND全部revoked-unstarted authorized attempts terminal stopped AND全部started actions terminal/safe-stop AND candidate_nonterminal_freeze_completeness.valid=true AND runtime baseline frozen AND quiescence result-review PASS AND rollover terminal PASS`。成立时Z本身同时是epoch terminal与唯一next-plan activation，`successor=E(e+1)`，才产生next-plan escape；不存在也不得补写单独的Q后rollover activation receipt。
