三谓词按上述顺序互斥且完备；同时有mandatory＋stop时class保留`mandatory_and_stop`及完整性修复证据，但safe closure只能`Z=stopped`、unsafe只能`Z=failed`，不得positive successor。mandatory-only clean closure才可superseded进入新epoch修复。stop I在rollover PASS后出现时历史PASS保留但不产生successor。positive/negative Z双写、任选分支、覆盖、多个Z、negative Z带successor、stop latch存在却supersede、quiescence/intake未闭合却写Z均fail closed。Z(e)记录旧epoch/root/plan/G、全部trigger/latch/interrupt refs、rollover terminal、latest Q head/intake snapshot、cutoff、grant-universe/三类attempt closure/revocation、candidate-nonterminal-freeze、runtime-baseline digests与no-cross-epoch-authority；旧receipt不回写。每个Z只固定`program_terminal_ready_projection`：`superseded→null`、`stopped→ready_stopped`、`failed→ready_failed`；任何其他映射、negative Z投影null/partial/complete/final terminal或superseded投影terminal-ready都拒绝。

Z cutoff后的事件由非自引用的`post_z_destination_contract_template_v2`和`post_z_interrupt_handoff_v1`机器化，不能只写“归入下一epoch/incident”。bootstrap/H0先冻结template的static subject/action/schema/path templates、role inequalities、guards与deny surface；Z payload逐source只固定sealed high-watermark、next expected sequence、cutoff、`destination_kind=successor_epoch_intake|terminal_ready_incident_origin`和`post_z_destination_contract_template_digest_v2`。该template preimage与Z body都明确排除current/future Z commit/tree/blob/raw full ref、actual successor bundle/reservation/root、terminal-ready observation/root及其digest；Z不得保存任何依赖自身full ref的future root。Z commit后才唯一导出：positive分支的`successor bundle/reservation/root=f(Z full ref,template digest)`；negative分支的terminal-ready incident origin/reservation则由`f(Z full ref,ready projection,template digest)`导出。同一Z＋template必须唯一，template/current-root/future-root互换或把future digest注入Z body均fail closed。

destination discriminant 是 closed-world：`Z=superseded` 只能选择 `successor_epoch_intake`，其materialized receipt subject=`program_governance_post_z_interrupt`；`Z=stopped|failed` 只能选择 `terminal_ready_incident_origin`，这个origin是 `pure_projection_no_intake_receipt`，不是lifecycle envelope、不是I receipt，也没有materializer action。negative-Z之后若再有事件，才由origin导出 intake profile，其materialized I subject恰为`program_governance_terminal_incident`。positive successor receipt、negative pure origin、negative后续incident I三者的subject kind、path、root member和receipt/origin kind不得互换；把negative origin伪装成post-Z handoff receipt或把positive event写到terminal-incident path一律fail closed。

positive Z后的每个event继续使用canonical event ID、strict sequence和append-only set，并复用`ticket_applicability_receipt_materialization_write`的`transition_subject_kind=program_governance_post_z_interrupt` special variant，不生成applicability verdict或substantive grant。destination是actual successor bundle下固定`post_z_intake/I{i}.json`；只在首个E(e+1)-C0或pre-activation terminal Z之前开放，`successor_pre_activation_control_head_v1`取Z（无I）或latest连续I，C0/Z必须为该head direct child并逐字节绑定lineage seed Z与完整handoff set，随后stream sealed。recoverable gap只开放exact missing I；unknown/duplicate/remap/不可恢复gap先写上述deterministic integrity I和作者分离review，再由reserved successor epoch从latest I写H0-less negative Z failed、无successor，不能卡在between-epochs或误建C0。缺template/path/root/subject/high-watermark、cutoff后回写旧Z、stale/foreign I、未review anomaly或handoff未被C0/failed-Z消费均fail closed。

negative Z和所有无Z的Phase7 `ready_complete|ready_partial|no_release→ready_stopped`统一使用`terminal_ready_incident_intake_v1`，不再把Z当普遍前提。每次`base_ready!=null`首次成立时，pure、immutable `terminal_ready_incident_origin_snapshot_v1`立即从exact origin refs导出并固定`origin_kind=negative_z|phase7_normal|phase7_partial|phase7_no_release`、finite event-source registry digest、逐source initial cutoff/initial received high-watermark/initial accepted-I high-watermark/next sequence、origin时sorted pending event IDs与static terminal-incident template digest；它不要求origin时pending先为空，后续high-watermark也绝不回写该origin。actual `terminal_ready_incident_reservation_v1`只在origin full refs已存在后纯导出，`terminal_ready_incident_intake_root_v1=SHA256(RFC8785(reservation digest,static subject/action/path-template/roles/guards/deny))`，明确排除current parent/tree、后续I-chain/stream snapshot与future sync，因此整个origin lineage中stable不变。

每个canonical head另纯生成可变观察而非可写pointer的`terminal_incident_stream_snapshot_v1`，绑定immutable origin full ref、current parent/tree、逐source current received/accepted high-watermarks、ordered accepted-I full refs、sorted pending IDs、完整observed anomaly records及observation digest。每个deterministic integrity/drift I必须列exact `covered_anomaly_ids`；`terminal_incident_uncovered_anomaly_set_v1 = observed_anomaly_ids - union(valid accepted deterministic-I.covered_anomaly_ids)`，同一anomaly只能被一张I覆盖，I不能覆盖未来/foreign anomaly。`terminal_incident_stream_state_v1`从该snapshot纯计算pre-seal状态：exact next event已接收未物化为`intake_pending`；可恢复missing sequence为`gap_waiting`；uncovered set非空为`invalid_corrupt`；uncovered=[]、pending=[]且至少一张accepted deterministic failure I存在为`integrity_failure_closed`；否则连续且pending=[]为`open_clean`。`sealed`不由本reducer读取program terminal反推，只由下述joint reducer输出。`intake_pending|gap_waiting|invalid_corrupt`不擦除last fully accepted incident snapshot所得effective ready，也不令selector退回active/empty；它们令固定`mode=program-finalizing-intake`，并把effective ready放入独立`program_terminal_ready_status_or_null`，`program_terminal_status_or_null`保持null；current sync candidate/review/authority/action/result/terminal、stale-closure transition、program terminal及全部product/domain grants均false，同时保留exact next-I intake。gap_waiting只在missing exact payload可验证时开放该I；invalid_corrupt只开放绑定uncovered anomaly set的deterministic integrity/drift I，不能接收自由文本。`integrity_failure_closed`保留全部原始异常及first-drift ancestry，不冒充clean，但其effect固定ready_failed并允许failed-only stale closure/sync/finalization；新anomaly到达会再次回到intake/invalid。

terminal incident envelope使用不可与post-Z handoff互换的`transition_subject_kind=program_governance_terminal_incident`及固定`terminal_incident/I{i}.json`，每次dynamic grant绑定origin、stable intake root、current parent/tree、previous I、strictly-growing event/anomaly set与one-shot path。任何finalizing期间的included source path/ref/denominator漂移都先立即撤销current sync grant，再由同一action物化deterministic `source_projection_integrity_drift` I；其event/anomaly ID与evidence逐字节绑定first drift commit/tree、exact changed included paths、old/new per-path blob/raw和source/denominator digests、absence-of-prior-covering-I proof，禁止通过改回旧blob抹去ancestry。`terminal_ready_incident_effect_v1`同时是bundle内`terminal_ready_incident_effect_reducer_profile`的exact machine dependency；它只折叠current canonical single-parent ancestry上按source从0连续、remote-accepted且principal/class命中registry的I，accepted-prefix digest排除received-only/pending IDs与whole current snapshot。它按完整连续accepted-I snapshot优先计算：accepted unsafe security/integrity、stream integrity failure、source-projection drift或mandatory completeness I→`ready_failed`；否则authorized stop→`ready_stopped`；仅benign evidence update保持base-ready但强制新observation。pending/gap/uncovered-corrupt阻断sync/final但不阻断唯一intake；I覆盖全部anomaly后重算为`integrity_failure_closed`、ready_failed、new observation/root。duplicate covering I、覆盖集合为空/越界或把closed failure降回complete/partial/stopped均拒绝。finalization只由下述joint reducer把`open_clean|integrity_failure_closed→sealed`与program terminal成对输出；其后事件才属于外部operations/security authority，不能回写已终结program。

`program_terminal_ready_reducer_v1`是唯一、纯函数、无mutable pointer的终局事实计算器；它先只读canonical branch上同epoch/phase/ticket的immutable receipts，按以下优先级计算`base_ready`，再强制应用上一段`terminal_ready_incident_effect_v1`，但不直接形成final program terminal：

1. current/final Z=`failed`，或已由同一Z绑定的fatal safety/integrity closure failure，`base_ready=ready_failed`；
2. current/final Z=`stopped`，`base_ready=ready_stopped`；normal Phase7中的no_release只有在active/nonterminal rollover target=null、Q=null、mandatory/stop latch=false、pending governance closure=[]，且`phase7_pre_release_decision_ready_v1=true`及gate digest被CLS-07全链绑定、Owner decision=`no_release`、result-review/terminal=`stopped`时也得到该base。standalone program-stop request/latch绝不是充分条件，pre-release gate不成立时的早期停止只能走PROGRAM-EPOCH-ROLLOVER/Q/Z或pre-activation Z；
3. active/nonterminal rollover target=null、Q=null、mandatory/stop latch=false、pending governance closure=[]，`PHASE-6-PARTIAL-CLOSEOUT-01` terminal/activation有效、Phase7 entry=`partial_closeout_only`、`phase7_partial_source_exit_ready=true`；其required set必须恰为`CLS-01/CLS-04/CLS-05/CLS-06(mode=partial)`且全部result-review/terminal PASS，partial CLS-06逐字节绑定`phase7_partial_review_denominator_v1`，excluded set必须恰为`CLS-02/CLS-03/CLS-07-OWNER-RELEASE-DECISION/RELEASE-EXECUTION-01`且每张恰有一条same-epoch、partial-mode-bound、selector-selected exact not-applicable A-chain/head。除该合法A receipt/selector projection外，任一excluded ticket出现第二/旧/stale/cross-mode A或candidate/review/authority/result/result-review/terminal/activation、任一不可恢复legacy deletion/publication/release side effect存在、或`effective_current_cutover_active_v1=true`都使reducer invalid；满足全部条件才得`base_ready=ready_partial`；
4. active/nonterminal rollover target=null、Q=null、mandatory/stop latch=false、pending governance closure=[]，Phase1–6 effective PASS、`phase7_pre_release_decision_ready_v1=true`且gate digest未漂移、`phase7_normal_source_exit_ready=true`、finite `pre_release_completion_gate_registry_v1`全部满足、CLS-07 decision=`release`且result-review/terminal passed，并且`RELEASE-EXECUTION-01`在Owner request=true时result-review/terminal PASS、false时有selector-selected exact not-applicable receipt，`base_ready=ready_complete`；
5. 其他情况`base_ready=null`。base-ready非null即建立origin；`program_terminal_ready_reducer_v1`只对last fully materialized、连续accepted-I snapshot应用`terminal_ready_incident_effect_v1`并输出effective ready：incident unsafe/mandatory/integrity覆盖为`ready_failed`，safe stop覆盖非failed base为`ready_stopped`，benign保持base但改变incident snapshot/source projection。received但未物化的event不被当作已生效事实，也不得令last effective ready或accepted-prefix digest消失/改变；两个finalizing mode必须消费同一profile key、profile digest与`last_fully_accepted_effective_ready_status_or_null`；只有`terminal_incident_stream_state IN {open_clean,integrity_failure_closed}`才可进入相应normal或failed-only sync，其他状态保持intake-only，直到I补齐或deterministic I覆盖全部anomaly。

这些条件是互斥优先级而不是可任选标签；higher-priority base/incident evidence存在时不得输出lower-priority状态。H0-backed epoch由H0 registry、H0-less stopped/failed pre-activation epoch由`pre_activation_epoch_bootstrap_bundle_v4`提前注册同一个stable `PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01`=`program_governance_control+not_in_frontier+not_in_phase_exit`合同，以及同一static terminal-incident intake/stale-sync-closure schemas；两者必须重算为相同schema/action/path/role/publication-profile/Git-adapter semantics，唯一差异是static source kind=`H0_REGISTRY|PRE_ACTIVATION_BUNDLE`，不能借旧epoch root。ready非null后，`terminal_ready_root_v1`只允许该票用既有generic lifecycle action IDs形成candidate→作者分离review→独立authority→remote-accepted exact start claim→initial primary choice→generation-0 publication operation intent→`terminal_project_os_three_path_publication_v3` exact publication/readback→result→作者分离review→terminal PASS；publication governed set恰为 capability ledger、current context pack、root-cause ledger 三条冻结路径及4/2/4 MiB caps，actual changed paths只可为其nonempty subset，三份resulting contents都须验证。`terminal_git_publication_adapter_effect_capability_profile_v3`只允许query/first initiate、其余operation=false、internal retry=0，unknown只query。不要求H0/G，不开放计划、source worklog、R14或任何第四条路径。另只允许bounded terminal-incident I intake及下述唯一stale sync closure。它不能产品发布、迁移、删除、重开普通票、改终局事实或获得任何domain authority，也不计为第17个control action。

terminal sync 与 terminal-incident I 以同一canonical parent作expected-old竞争：若I先被remote接受，publication claim/transport call count为0，old attempt使用stale root收口；若publication先被remote接受，后到I只能成为其child，old attempt按actual changed-path diff进入non-PASS stale closure并由新observation/new attempt纠正，不能删除或覆盖已发布diff。若primary start claim已经remote accepted后I才赢，old attempt必须记录一个已承诺logical invocation及真实physical observation，禁止伪装pre-execution negative。`no_observation|effect_unknown|in_flight_same_server_operation_proven`在terminal Git stale profile下都只能query/readback；`no_call_proven`永远不再primary；succeeded/failed只记录真实effect。该adapter不注册safety member或locator，stale同attempt全部非query operation=false；必须冻结truthful observation/actual diff并形成non-PASS result→独立review→old terminal，fresh exact remote-ref observation成立后才允许new attempt竞争。unknown可以终结为non-PASS obligation，但fresh ref仍unknown时new attempt physical call=0。

为消除“整个Git commit hash”与“排除sync paths”的矛盾，每个sync attempt只能在`terminal_incident_stream_state IN {open_clean,integrity_failure_closed}`时从其exact canonical parent纯生成`terminal_ready_observation_v1`：固定program/source epoch、effective ready status、pre-seal stream state、`observation_commit/tree`（仅作ancestry anchor）、sorted immutable source full refs/denominators、terminal-ready origin snapshot、current accepted-I/anomaly-coverage snapshot、`terminal_ready_incident_effect_v1` profile digest/accepted-prefix input digest/output status、`terminal_ready_included_path_set_v1`及逐path blob/raw digests、final-status output allowlist digest、compact sync namespace digest。`integrity_failure_closed`时ready必须恰为ready_failed、expected final-status diff只能failed。`terminal_ready_source_projection_digest_v1`只hashRFC8785 canonical的source refs/denominators/origin＋accepted-I/anomaly-coverage snapshots/included-path contents，明确不hashwhole commit ID，也排除全部`PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01` lifecycle/stale-closure paths和允许被sync改写的final-status output paths；observation commit/tree只进入每attempt transition grant。`terminal_ready_root_v1=SHA256(RFC8785(source_projection_digest,ready_status,pre-seal stream state,static PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01 subject/action subset,roles,path templates,guards,deny surface))`，不含future candidate/result/terminal或自身digest。

candidate绑定observation full preimage/digest、current attempt namespace empty proof和expected final-status diff；后续每个lifecycle grant分别绑定自己的materialization parent commit/tree。sync action还必须绑定已提交authority full ref、`sync_action_parent_commit/tree`、expected diff和unchanged source projection。terminal前每一步都重算incident received/accepted high-watermarks与uncovered anomaly set，并要求stream保持candidate冻结的`open_clean|integrity_failure_closed`；任一pending/gap/uncovered-corrupt或included source漂移立即使current sync write/start及后续grant失效，只保留exact incident-I intake。漂移先按上一段物化deterministic integrity I，之后old attempt才使用下述stale root真实收口；不允许无I借stale root。terminal后validator从current canonical head重算included source projection，验证observation→terminal为single-parent chain、每个control transition只改自身fixed compact path、domain write只改expected final-status allowlist且最终内容/Project OS一致；不得仅在历史ref上重算恒等式。无incident且source projection不变的普通失败retry可复用root但必须产生新observation/action-parent；valid I改变projection时旧attempt收口后，新attempt使用新projection/root。

终局采用唯一total pure `program_terminal_and_incident_seal_reducer_v1`，不再让program terminal与`sealed`互相作为输入。其preimage只含与observation/root相同的accepted-prefix input/output digest、exact pre-seal stream snapshot/state=`open_clean|integrity_failure_closed`、effective ready、passing sync terminal full ref、current source projection、post-sync Git/Project-OS verification、pending/uncovered anomaly=[]、无额外path/received-event证明和reducer version；不含future program status、future sealed值或pair digest自身。它一次输出canonical pair及digest：`ready_complete→(complete,sealed)`、`ready_partial→(partial,sealed)`、`ready_stopped→(stopped,sealed)`、`ready_failed→(failed,sealed)`；`integrity_failure_closed`只允许最后一组。`program_terminal_projection_v1`、selector、Phase7 effective reducer只消费该pair digest，不能另做固定点或独立重算program terminal。只有terminal无sealed、只有sealed无terminal、旧snapshot、sync FAIL、post-sync event、ready/state不匹配或两个pair均invalid。这样final Project OS不能预写future outcome，也不会由sync自身制造source drift、忽略已知漂移或选择任意“pre-sync base”。

若valid terminal incident I（包括deterministic source-projection/stream-integrity I）在某sync attempt terminal前使其observation/projection/root过期，bundle/H0预注册的`terminal_sync_superseded_attempt_closure_root_v1`提供唯一、narrow且非自引用的收口authority basis，不增加action family。为避免closure过程中又到incident造成root连锁失效，stable root preimage只绑定old attempt/candidate及首个supersession时已存在的review/authority full refs、old observation/projection/root、首个superseding terminal-incident I full ref、fixed stale-closure schemas/paths/roles、允许的generic stale-review、denial-only authority-decision、result/result-review/terminal action subsets与deny surface；明确排除可继续增长的later-I set、current stage/action-start、new projection/root、future closure objects/retry和root self-digest。每个one-shot closure transition grant再绑定current stage、latest完整连续incident set及old/new snapshots/projections、`action_started`分类、old sync grant consumption、expected/actual allowlist diff、current parent/tree、sorted exact predecessor refs与exact output path；later I只使旧dynamic grant失效并重算新grant，不改变stable closure root或覆盖已写closure object。selector的typed root set可同时开放stable incident intake、current-ready sync，以及至多一个old stale attempt的closure member；stale attempt terminal前new candidate grant=false。

stale closure stage matrix固定为：candidate-only先由作者分离reviewer在fixed stale-review path记录`superseded_by_terminal_incident`，再写pre-execution negative result/review/terminal；candidate-review FAIL沿普通negative路径；candidate-review PASS且authority decision尚无时，先由独立issuer使用stale root的denial-only variant物化`denied_due_to_registered_control_supersession`，再按authority-denied路径负向收口；authority已有denied/held decision时消费原decision；authority已authorized且`primary_claim_state!=remote_accepted`时先撤销old sync grant再负向收口；remote-accepted start claim存在时，一个logical invocation已经committed，必须冻结truthful observation、logical invocation count=1、全部operation intents/receipts、已消费action、实际allowlist diff及partial/full write，禁止pre-execution shortcut或第二claim。后续同attempt只可按terminal Git profile query/readback；continue/complete/cancel/compensation与safety choice/locator均为false。必须按exact-new/exact-old/valid-competitor/foreign/unknown总分类冻结真实intent/call/observation/actual diff，再形成non-PASS result与独立result review。任何unknown blind replay、第二次push、原dispatch winner授权后继操作或伪造no-effect都拒绝。result或result-review已存在时旧票physical operation全部false，不覆盖历史；必要时追加唯一fixed incident-supersession result-review后形成non-PASS terminal并把未关闭义务交给新registered recovery。old attempt terminal后才允许new observation/retry。该root绝不授权candidate重绑、authorized execution authority、新sync/domain write、activation或任何product action；denial-only receipt永远`granted=false`。old/new ready root、intake/stale root、stage或denial/authorized互换均fail closed。passing sync terminal被`program_terminal_and_incident_seal_reducer_v1`与同一pre-seal snapshot消费并原子输出terminal＋sealed pair之后才到达的事件属于外部incident，不再创建stale closure。

`program_hold_latched_v1`是current CLS-07 sovereign-decision lineage的pure reducer：只有绑定valid `phase7_pre_release_decision_ready_v1` digest的unique latest Owner-decision terminal=`held`才建立anchor。其resolution lineage必须same epoch、same normal mode、same stable ticket、single-parent且一次只有一个active attempt；下一attempt predecessor可为anchor held terminal或上一resolution attempt的`held|failed|stopped` terminal。pre-decision candidate/review/authority-negative失败不清除hold；只有该lineage最终产生valid sovereign result与Owner decision=`release|no_release` terminal才变false。hold=true时ready/program terminal均null、publication/release grant=false；它不能冒充partial/stopped/complete。新的release terminal先清除hold再评估RELEASE，新的no_release terminal由上项得到ready_stopped。缺receipt、cross-epoch/mode混用、sibling resolution、同一evidence导出多个ready/terminal、negative Z与complete/partial并存、partial-mode excluded ticket缺唯一合法NA A-chain/head或除该A/selector外出现任何downstream lifecycle object、release request=true却把RELEASE票排除均令reducer invalid且execution disabled，不能择优掩盖冲突。

Phase7没有也不得临时发明`PHASE-7-CLOSE-*` ticket。`phase7_effective_status_reducer_v1`与ready/program reducers使用同一canonical snapshot：ready非null但terminal-sync尚未PASS时Phase7保持`in_progress`且scope已冻结、全部产品动作false；program=`complete`时Phase7 effective status=`passed`；program=`partial`时Phase7 effective status=`stopped`且`phase7_closeout_scope=partial`；program=`stopped|failed`时Phase7同名；program/ready均null时Phase7只能保持真实planned/candidate/in_progress/blocked状态，不能passed。program complete但Phase7非passed、program partial却把Phase7写passed、ready时提前写final status、伪造Phase7 close receipt或reducers snapshot/digest不同都invalid。

`Z(e)=superseded` 预授权两个 bounded escape actions，而不是恢复旧 root：`next_epoch_initial_plan_revision_materialization_write` 只能创建 `E(e+1)-C0`，其parent必须是`successor_pre_activation_control_head_v1`（无post-Z I时即Z(e)，有I时为latest连续handoff I）、single-parent/non-merge、changed path恰为本计划、one-shot，并逐字节绑定完整handoff event set；`next_epoch_plan_revision_successor_materialization_write`只在同epoch、尚无accepted Ck且exact Cj review有P0/P1/P2时创建C(j+1)，同样只改计划路径。一旦Ck accepted，本action grant永久false，material change改走Z。两者绑定author/materializer、expected raw payload digest、fixed plan path并禁止其他receipt/config/source/action。新epoch unique Ck exact review PASS后才可创建H0；旧PASS/grant/activation/phase-entry不carry，旧evidence与post-Z handoff只能通过新H0的`cross_epoch_evidence_continuity_matrix`标为`compatible/needs_revalidation/invalidated`后作只读输入。

`current_authority_epoch_selector_v1` 是唯一 reducer，不写 mutable pointer。它扫描remote canonical branch、经exact fast-forward reconciliation的local/origin-tracking、fixed epoch receipt namespace与single-parent/non-merge chain，并以唯一解析的`terminal_ready_incident_effect_reducer_profile`计算last accepted prefix，再在同一snapshot计算joint program-terminal/seal pair、base/effective-ready、incident/pre-G/Q/post-Z stream-integrity、unresolved Q/Z topology、两个latch、hold与trigger R/A/I reducer，再输出完整selector tuple；至少含`current_epoch_id/terminal_epoch_id/mode/program_terminal_ready_status_or_null/program_terminal_status_or_null/terminal_incident_stream_state/execution_enabled/ordinary_new_ticket_allowed/substantive_new_grant_allowed/governance_control_roots_digest/governance_control_whitelist`。`mode`是无参数固定enum，ready与final terminal status绝不编码进mode；非finalization/terminal modes两个status字段都必须null。`governance_control_roots_digest`不是含义不明的单一root字符串，而是RFC8785 canonical `governance_control_roots_v1` typed map的SHA-256；每个mode只允许registry列出的member keys，每个grant同时绑定whole-map digest、自己的member kind和exact member root。duplicate/foreign/missing member、member/whitelist不对称或intake/sync/stale互换均invalid。`execution_enabled`只表示substantive/product action是否可以进入逐票grant评估，不控制治理receipt写入；whitelist只能例外开放control transition。`program-finalizing-intake`只开exact next terminal-I；`program-finalizing`才可另外开放current terminal sync lifecycle与至多一个stale sync attempt closure；`program-terminal`只读final pair且无whitelist。trigger/intake、stream-integrity和finalizing modes都不能获得product/domain authority。每个mode的完整selector tuple必须按下表全量出现，漏字段、suffix mode或unknown mode即invalid：

| 优先级与 mode | current / terminal epoch | execution | ordinary new ticket | substantive new grant | typed governance-control root map | 唯一 control whitelist |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---|
| reducer invalid → `invalid` | null / null | false | false | false | `{}` / empty-map digest | empty |
| terminal=`complete|partial|stopped|failed` → fixed `mode=program-terminal`；ready=null；`program_terminal_status_or_null={status}` | null / exact terminal epoch | false | false | false | `{terminal_evidence_readonly}` | empty |
| unresolved Q存在 → `quiescing` | exact E(e) / prior terminal epoch或null | false | false | false | `{q_closure, stream_integrity=optional}` | frozen started ordinary actual closure、revoked-unstarted authorized negative closure、current rollover result/review/terminal、late trigger I；recoverable gap exact missing-I，irrecoverable anomaly I/review/failed-Z；normal或integrity-closed Z；无activation |
| base-ready origin已建立且incident stream=`intake_pending|gap_waiting|invalid_corrupt` → fixed `mode=program-finalizing-intake`；`program_terminal_ready_status_or_null={last_fully_accepted_effective_ready_status_or_null}`；terminal status=null | exact source epoch / prior或null | false | false | false | `{terminal_incident_intake, terminal_sync:null, stale_sync_closure:null}` | 仅exact next terminal-incident I或deterministic integrity-failure/drift I；pending payload本身不改变effective ready，也不得退回base；sync/stale closure/new candidate/final terminal全部false |
| last fully accepted effective ready非null且incident stream=`open_clean|integrity_failure_closed` → fixed `mode=program-finalizing`；`program_terminal_ready_status_or_null={last_fully_accepted_effective_ready_status_or_null}`；terminal status=null | exact source epoch / prior或null | false | false | false | `{terminal_incident_intake, terminal_sync=current terminal_ready_root, stale_sync_closure=optional unique root}` | 与intake mode消费同一effective-ready reducer；bounded terminal-incident I intake；open-clean走对应status sync，integrity-closed只走ready_failed/stale-closure/failed-sync；无stale时exact current PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01 lifecycle/Project-OS sync/result/review/terminal；有stale时先stage-bound review/denial-only-authority/result/result-review/non-PASS terminal；joint reducer才成对输出final+sealed |
| pre-G、Q外或positive-post-Z stream=`recoverable_wait|irrecoverable_open` → `governance-stream-integrity-intake` | exact current/reserved E / prior或null | false | false | false | `{stream_integrity}` | recoverable只补exact missing I；irrecoverable只写deterministic integrity I→作者分离review→negative failed-Z；forged stopper只写rejected I；无normal successor/positive Z |
| active G且candidate已存在、出现未消费新trigger R/I或A-input drift → `program-governance-candidate-supersession` | exact E(e) / prior或null | false | false | false | `{active_epoch=G root, trigger_supersession}` | old rollover attempt按review/denial-only/action-start stage真实non-PASS收口；old terminal后才retry；无Q/new candidate/product action |
| active G且known pending raw trigger、accepted R尚无current A或A head stale → `program-governance-trigger-intake` | exact E(e) / prior或null | false | false | false | `{active_epoch=G root, trigger_intake}` | exact raw-trigger R receipt或initial/reevaluate/retry A；不得ordinary/substantive/candidate/Q；A current后进入continue或latch mode |
| mandatory＋stop latch → `program-stop-and-invalidated` | exact E(e) / prior或null | false | false | false | `{active_epoch=G root}` | current/new rollover lifecycle；stop terminal intent与mandatory evidence同时绑定 |
| mandatory latch only → `active-invalidated` | exact E(e) / prior或null | false | false | false | `{active_epoch=G root}` | current/new rollover lifecycle |
| stop latch only → `program-stop-requested` | exact E(e) / prior或null | false | false | false | `{active_epoch=G root}` | current/new rollover lifecycle |
| hold only → `program-hold` | exact E(e) / prior或null | false | false | false | `{active_epoch=G root}` | exact serial CLS-07 hold-resolution lifecycle；以及仅为stop/mandatory的raw-trigger R intake；普通plan-change明确禁止 |
| positive Z后尚无next C0 → `between_epochs` | null / superseded E(e) | false | false | false | `{successor_bundle, Z_escape, pre_activation, stream_integrity=optional}` | bounded post-Z handoff I；recoverable gap补I，irrecoverable anomaly→integrity I/review→reserved successor failed-Z；否则E(e+1)-C0或pre-activation Z是latest successor-control head direct child |
| next epoch Cj已存在、H0前 → `plan_revisioning` | E(e+1) / E(e) | false | false | false | `{plan_revision_bootstrap, pre_activation}` | accepted Ck前的same-epoch plan-only C successor；accepted Ck后H0或material-change Z；任一head可authorized stop Z |
| accepted Ck/Hn/Tn/Fn/Sn/G pending → `phase0_governance` | exact next E / prior E或null | false | false | false | `{phase0_bootstrap, pre_activation}` | total pre-G reducer指定的event receipt、H/F/S/T、prepared-remote-CAS G、material-change/stop/failed Z；remote accepted G才切active |
| valid remote-accepted且local/tracking-aligned G、以上均不成立 → `active` | exact E(e) / prior或null | true | true | true | `{active_epoch=G root}` | phase/ticket entry-gated lifecycle、raw-trigger R receipt，以及current program-governance continue/retry/interrupt gates；initial A只在trigger-intake |

统一授权公式固定为：`control_transition_effective = selector.valid AND exact root-map digest/member binding AND ((mode=active AND ordinary_new_ticket_allowed AND exact phase/ticket entry gate) OR (exact transition IN governance_control_whitelist AND valid mode-specific derived control grant))`。`ordinary_substantive_grant_effective = selector.valid AND execution_enabled AND substantive_new_grant_allowed AND exact ticket candidate-review PASS AND independent execution-authority authorized AND action/path/budget/expiry/one-shot checks`。`finalization_sync_action_grant_effective = selector.valid AND mode=program-finalizing AND program_terminal_ready_status_or_null IN {ready_complete,ready_partial,ready_stopped,ready_failed} AND program_terminal_status_or_null=null AND stream IN {open_clean,integrity_failure_closed} AND pending_received_events=[] AND uncovered_anomaly_set=[] AND no_unclosed_stale_attempt AND exact PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01 candidate-review/independent-authority AND observation/source_projection/incident_snapshot stable AND exact terminal-sync root member AND action_id=program_terminal_project_os_sync_write AND exact terminal_project_os_three_path_publication_v3 surface/path/budget/expiry/one-shot checks AND exact terminal_git_publication_adapter_effect_capability_profile_v3 binding AND (stream=open_clean OR program_terminal_ready_status_or_null=ready_failed)`；这是唯一不依赖`execution_enabled/substantive_new_grant_allowed`的finalization-specific primary action basis，不能泛化到其他action。`substantive_grant_effective = ordinary_substantive_grant_effective XOR finalization_sync_action_grant_effective`，且tagged authority basis必须与所选root一致。

`substantive_start_claim_effective = substantive_grant_effective AND primary_claim_state IN {absent,prepared_unaccepted} AND no_remote_accepted_claim_for_same_attempt_action_target AND exact remote-identity/ref/expected-old/direct-parent/action-key/idempotency/fence/rollback/prearmed-safety-member-set checks`；它只允许第一张prepared primary claim参加lease竞争，不允许physical effect。`primary_invocation_committed = primary_claim_state=remote_accepted AND claim_was_accepted_while_substantive_start_claim_effective AND accepted_claim_count_for_attempt_action_target=1`。initial `primary_effect_choice_cas_v2`只选择`dispatch_primary|close_no_call|select_safe_if_primary_not_invoked_compensation`。`dispatch_primary` winner把route置为primary-effecting，但在真实adapter call前physical state仍为`pre_dispatch_no_call_proven`，不得直接投影in-flight。

任何physical operation先过共同门：同claim/full-ref/fence/invocation/action/target/adapter、candidate-review与authority仍绑定、lifecycle仍在execution window、result/result-review/terminal均不存在、current selector/root/member有效、adapter capability允许、call cap未耗尽。query只生成readback receipt，不选effect；每个非query call还必须让`physical_operation_intent_cas_v1`在generation-0 null predecessor或successor exact predecessor intent/observation上赢得该operation generation的唯一winner。primary first intent绑定initial dispatch winner与pre-call state；safe-if-no-call compensation O0绑定accepted safety choice且两个operation predecessor refs均为null；其真实receipt才投影`no_observation|in_flight_same_server_operation_proven|primary_effect_complete|primary_effect_absent_proven|effect_unknown`。后继continue/complete/cancel各自需要新generation与same-server-operation proof，不能共用原dispatch winner；`cancel_before_effect`属于effecting operation，只有qualified readback明确证明无effect才到absent，timeout/lost response仍unknown。`no_observation|effect_unknown`只query；`no_call_proven`只可query、negative result或safe-if-no-call member，primary dispatch/resume/complete永久false。同一idempotency key本身绝不是intent winner、same-operation或exactly-once证明。

实际call许可必须在同一current snapshot上同时满足：physical-state×operation cell、route-state×operation cell、current-state next-operation allowlist、adapter capability、全部named/global gates、call cap和current exact predecessor；任一项deny即call=0。route=`compensation_selected`、member=`selected_not_invoked`或旧compensation intent都不能覆盖后来accepted observation投影出的`no_observation|effect_unknown`；此时旧intent因predecessor不再current而不可消费，也不得新建可执行compensation intent，只能query。qualified query先产生新的、允许态的latest exact observation之后，支持该能力的ordinary/Q/IDX adapter才可重新竞争对应safety/operation协议；`no_call_proven`的typed safe-if-no-call sentinel正路保持独立可达。

adapter支持的safe-if-no-call或post-dispatch补偿/恢复由`prearmed_safety_suboperation_choice_cas_v3`使用exact-one tagged authority basis选出单个预装member：IDX interlock/delete用`index_under_lock_recovery`；Q-started ordinary用`q_quiescence`；active ordinary failure/partial用`ordinary_same_claim_recovery`。terminal-sync旧attempt不在该enum，固定走Git stale lifecycle。choice preimage绑定initial primary winner、typed predecessor discriminant、null-or-exact observation、current narrow root、branch/member/target/adapter/fence/trigger/outcome/max-call；winner前suboperation call=0，winner接受后才令member selected、route compensation-selected并永久关闭primary continuation。实际invoke仍须自己的one-shot operation intent。IDX即使处于Q也仍归IDX分支并额外绑定Q root，不得双重命中。`substantive_physical_operation_effective`是共同门、typed matrix、exact operation-intent winner及适用时exact safety-choice/basis的合取，不是宽泛OR。result frozen/consumed或attempt terminal后全部physical operation（包括query）false。任何新claim/new scope/second invocation、同generation双winner、generation skip/replay、`unknown`换operation/member、未预装恢复或result后回旧票均false；无法安全收口时写non-PASS并转新registered recovery/cleanup ticket。

`finalization_incident_intake_effective = selector.valid AND mode IN {program-finalizing-intake,program-finalizing} AND exact terminal-ready origin/intake-root member/event/anomaly/sequence/high-watermark/path checks`。`stale_sync_closure_effective = selector.valid AND mode=program-finalizing AND stream IN {open_clean,integrity_failure_closed} AND unique stale attempt AND exact stage/action-start/superseding-I/closure-root member/action-subset/path checks`。final terminal只由joint pair reducer产生。trigger-intake/supersession、stream-integrity与finalization都不开放ordinary/general substantive或product grant，所有domain-authority bits仍false；finalization仅能按上述专用basis启动一次exact Project-OS sync。active的true不自动授action，known trigger R/pending input会先切intake而不是继续active；hold/Q/latch模式的whitelist也不能绕过substantive=false。unresolved Q优先于任何Q内新满足的Phase7 ready predicate；只有Z闭合或Q根本不存在时Phase7 evidence才可建立base-ready origin。Q与hold/latch同时存在时Q优先；Q前hold中形成mandatory/stop R即切相应intake/invalidated/stop mode并冻结CLS-07 retry；普通plan-change不得借hold白名单。

tracking drift、merge、多个successor/nonterminal epoch、duplicate remote-accepted G/Z、epoch gap、selector disagreement、cross-epoch predecessor、terminal/ready/hold/stop/latch与topology冲突，以及root map漏/重/foreign/member-whitelist不对称均令selector invalid/empty、all grants false；但已注册的trigger pending、recoverable/irrecoverable stream anomaly或terminal incident intake必须进入各自narrow control mode，不能误归ordinary invalid或永久all-false。Q-path Z必须是`latest_q_closure_head_v1` direct child；pre-activation Z必须是reservation指定target/root、total reducer接受的current remote control head之direct child；E(e+1)-C0只可为`successor_pre_activation_control_head_v1` direct child且另绑lineage seed Z。Q后的nonterminal freeze必须在Z前valid，outcome projection只在Z后约束selector/next-plan/ready。mutation逐mode断言完整selector tuple exact equality并拒绝所有status-suffix mode，同时覆盖四类origin×pending/benign/stop/mandatory/unsafe、I gap补齐、drift anomaly→唯一I coverage→integrity_failure_closed→failed-only stale/sync→joint `(failed,sealed)`、duplicate/foreign coverage拒绝、新anomaly重回intake、root-map漏换多foreign/scalar composite、later-I stable members不变而map/dynamic grant更新、valid G＋ready/final complete/partial/CLS-07 no_release/negative Z、raw trigger R与ordinary remote CAS竞争、R→A窗口execution=false、A stale、candidate后plan-change修改/撤回逐stagesupersession、active Q内后到Phase7-ready仍quiescing、hold串行C0-held→C1-failed→C2、hold→mandatory/stop、mandatory＋stop combined mode、Q/post-Z integrity closure、pre-activation exact seed/head-table/change/stop、stale sync逐stageclosure/retry、joint terminal/seal单一pair与自引用/双固定点拒绝、任一final terminal下新ticket/grant、whitelist扩大为substantive以及invalid evidence不得被priority/topology掩盖。

rollover 固定角色不等式至少为：candidate author≠candidate reviewer；authority issuer≠author/reviewer/quiescence executor/external request actor/program stopper/applicability evaluator；quiescence result reviewer≠executor/change requester；next-plan reviewer≠next-plan author/materializer。只有变更涉及产品范围、发布含义、付费规模、安全或不可逆动作时必须有 Owner decision；普通合同修正也只能由 registry 预设 governance issuer 签发，不能由计划作者自签。`PROGRAM-EPOCH-ROLLOVER`的applicability/candidate/candidate-review/execution-authority/result/result-review/terminal/activation全部以synthetic `phase_id=program-governance`、exact target attempt和full-64 `target_attempt_path_key`进入6.4.1 compact lifecycle namespace；synthetic phase仍完整绑定在target-key preimage/envelope中，但不再重复成为path segment。interrupt也只进入同target compact namespace，禁止在descriptive root复制任一control/lifecycle envelope。`configs/audits/fin_0_1_3_product_wide_architecture_rebase/authority_epochs/epoch-{e}/rollover/...`只保存result所引用的非lifecycle Q/quiescence/grant-universe/runtime-baseline domain artifacts；`epoch_terminal.json`只保存special Z envelope。epoch machine manifest/root/registry固定到`configs/repository/fin_0_1_3_product_wide_architecture_rebase/authority_epochs/epoch-{e}/...`，不得覆盖前epoch；导航`current.json`永不进入authority reducer。

### 6.4 权限状态机：允许“做什么”与允许“把什么当真”分开

程序采用 deny-by-default。章节标题、计划顺序、代码已存在或候选安装成功都不构成权限。机器合同必须同时维护：

1. `phase_state`：3.2 的 `phase_status`、`phase_review_status` 与证据绑定；
2. `ticket_state`：predecessor、`ticket_lifecycle_stage`、`ticket_terminal_status` 及 candidate/candidate-review/execution-authority/result/result-review/terminal receipts；
3. `phase_entry_eligible`：上一 phase downstream activation 后能否提出本 phase 的逐票 candidate；它不是 action grant；
4. `downstream_activation_state`：terminal PASS 后的独立 activation status/receipt；
5. `action_grants`：3.2 的 `granted:boolean`＋`action_grant_status`，说明允许执行的动作；
6. `domain_authority`：Candidate、Evidence、NumericFact、S2、S3、report、human、product/release 的权威；
7. `allowed_roots_and_surfaces`：动作能读写的精确路径、服务、API、数据类型与消费者。

每个 `action_grant` 至少包含：

- stable action ID 和 `default=false`；
- grant phase/ticket；
- exact authority commit/receipt；control plane总计固定16类：8类generic ticket-lifecycle actions（ordinary、pre-Q rollover、Q closure、terminal-ready sync、pre-Q/Q interrupt、positive post-Z handoff、all-ready terminal incident与stale-sync closure按strict root/action subsets复用）＋4类Phase0-specific bootstrap actions＋4类authority-epoch quiesce/terminal/next-plan actions。pre-activation Z复用terminal action的bundle/root variant，terminal sync复用generic actions并另消费一个registered non-authoritative domain write；均不增加control action ID，也不允许互换entry gate/root/subject；只有相应组可使用其明示root/predecessor重算`derived_transition_grant_id`，任何product/domain substantive action不得使用该例外；
- predecessor gate；
- allowed roots/surfaces 和显式 deny roots；
- input manifest/digest；
- budget/TokenBudgetBasis（适用时）；
- repository artifact输出时的ticket-bound `substantive_repository_artifact_publication_v1` profile、exact path/content-class/digest/bytes/ref；无repo输出时显式not_applicable；
- start/expiry/consumption；
- stop、rollback 和 reviewer receipt。

`substantive_action_start_linearization_v1`是所有action-bearing ticket与最低动作矩阵中每个primary substantive Action ID的统一强制子协议；它不是第九个generic lifecycle action、不是新的control authority，也不增加16类control action ID。start claim是该既有primary Action ID获独立execution authority后允许执行的第一个、且在任何其他读写/网络/进程/删除/cutover/publication副作用之前的bounded write；claim本身不创建authority、candidate、result或domain truth，只消耗既有authority并给“逻辑调用已经承诺”提供durable线性化点。真正的physical effect还依次受initial primary choice、逐call one-shot operation intent与真实observation约束；adapter支持的safe-if-no-call或post-dispatch补偿/恢复另须独立safety-suboperation choice。补偿/恢复不是无claim执行：同attempt若要在Q、IDX under-lock或ordinary partial/failure路径使用，必须在primary authority和claim中预装exact tagged member，并沿同一accepted claim/fence及其narrow root调用；terminal Git stale只使用既有stale lifecycle root/readback/non-PASS链，不预装physical member；active态独立stop/cleanup则自己是primary action，仍须独立start claim。registry若缺start协议、adapter capability、primary-choice/operation-intent/safety-choice primitive或需要的预装member，H0 validator必须拒绝。

每个exact primary action先计算`action_start_claim_key=SHA256(RFC8785(authority_epoch_id,phase_id,stable_ticket_id,ticket_attempt_id,action_id,target_identity))`，并只允许一个compact action-start path。三种key都必须是完整lowercase 64-hex；`phase_id`、完整ticket/attempt/action/target只存在于key preimage与envelope，不再重复为path segment。canonical primary-start-claim schema至少绑定：program/contract/epoch/G-or-Q/current-ready root；phase/ticket/attempt/action/target；candidate-review与execution-authority full refs；完整selector tuple/root-map digest；exact remote identity/ref/materialization parent；executor/materializer/commit-writer；action/surface/budget/TokenBudgetBasis/expiry/one-shot；idempotency与`primary_invocation_key`；logical/claim count上限；冻结adapter capability profile；六类`physical_operation_kind` allowlist；initial primary-choice key/backend/readback；per-operation intent key/generation/predecessor/locator；physical/observation/route schema；result boundary；deny/changed-path/publication profile。需要安全收口的primary还必须含sorted `prearmed_safety_suboperation_set_v1`，每项逐字节冻结suboperation kind、三选一physical authority basis、action ID、trigger、target/surface、adapter、executor/recovery actor、same-claim/fence/idempotency/invocation/safety-choice-member binding、maximum calls=1、`safe_if_primary_not_invoked`、permitted observations/outcomes、expected receipts与deny，并冻结safety-choice key/backend/locator；集合遗漏或claim接受后扩写均令grant=false。claim使用与`lifecycle_envelope_v1`相同的non-self-referential integrity规则；post-materialization refs只由same-endpoint readback记录，绝不回写body。

claim先在detached object中准备，不移动local/tracking ref，再与raw R、candidate后I、Q cutoff及同票其他primary start claim在同一canonical remote/ref用`lease_guarded_fast_forward_expected_old_v1`竞争。R/I/Q先赢时old grant立即stale、claim lease失败，logical invocation、physical operation及suboperation call均为0；claim先被remote接受时`primary_claim_state=remote_accepted`且恰好一个logical invocation已committed，prearmed set进入armed-not-selected。initial primary choice由同一remote/ref或qualified durable backend做expected-old CAS；winner=`dispatch_primary`只选route，不改变pre-call physical state。第一次adapter call还要取得generation-0 operation intent；call receipt后才推进physical state。后继每个effectful call都从上一observation receipt生成successor intent，same-generation competitor只能一个accepted；`no_observation|effect_unknown`只query，`in_flight_same_server_operation_proven`只沿同一server operation继续，`no_call_proven`永久关闭primary。需要safe-if-no-call或post-dispatch补偿时，先由safety-choice CAS用typed sentinel或exact observation选exact预装member，再由其operation intent调用；terminal Git stale不进入该路径；不得把它当initial primary的alternate loser。prepared/local-only、timeout/readback未接受claim/choice/intent都不得调用。任一choice/intent接受后在adapter前崩溃、effect后observation/result前崩溃、response lost或tracking失败，都只按same winner/operation readback；不得创建第二claim、同generation第二winner、跳generation或换member。Q落在authority与claim acceptance之间时调用数为0；Q落在accepted claim之后时attempt属started，但仍不产生第二invocation。

每个provider/model/source/service/install/benchmark/file-write/delete/cutover/publication adapter必须把exact claim full ref、fence、idempotency、invocation、initial primary-choice、current operation-intent generation/winner、physical operation kind、适用时的safety-choice/branch/member和action/target传到wrapper；wrapper逐次验证冻结remote identity、唯一accepted claim、未冻结result、typed generation-0 predecessor或successor exact observation、current root/member和adapter capability。支持服务端idempotency/fencing/query/resume/cancel的能力必须由qualification和receipt逐项证明，且记录作用域与保留窗口；同key不能替代choice、operation-intent或same-server-operation proof。没有durable intent primitive时仅可使用等价的qualified server fence/Git expected-old/IDX lock backend；既不可查询/围栏、又无事前补偿/恢复的action不得获得authority。suboperation wrapper另验证exact-one basis、same claim/fence、member/trigger/outcome/target/adapter/max-call与safety winner；ordinary/Q/IDX不可互换；terminal Git stale不得借用三者。result必须绑定全部primary/safety choices、operation-intent generations/winners、operation/observation、server-operation/readback及suboperation receipts；result冻结后旧票所有physical operations为false。mutation覆盖claim/primary-choice/operation-intent/call/observation/result各崩溃窗口、continue-vs-complete-vs-cancel竞争、cancel response lost、两个safety members竞争、generation replay/skip、no-call primary restart、unknown换member、result后旧票恢复、Q/IDX/ordinary branch互换或terminal Git stale借用physical recovery，并对model/service/index-delete/current-cutover/publication各给正反fixture。

#### 6.4.1 Ordinary、pre-Q program-governance 与 Q-special closure 的非自引用 bootstrap

每个Hn candidate必须携带`lifecycle_governance_root_v1`、16类actions、actors/paths/guards/hash、phase-entry gates与`program_governance_entry_gate_v1`；Hn FAIL时H(n+1)仅按Sn allowlist修正。accepted Hn/Tn冻结root，G(e)只激活control-plane transition engine并开放Phase1/2 candidate eligibility与受触发条件约束的program-governance入口，不激活审计、网络、安装、模型、迁移或领域动作。这样可合法物化首批P1/RSH candidates及必要时的pre-Q rollover lifecycle，而无bootstrap receipt循环。

`lifecycle_governance_root_preimage_v1`具有唯一、非自引用的RFC 8785 schema。它只含静态schema/action IDs、closed-world actor-role slots/action map/inequalities、guards、deny surfaces，以及完整`compact_lifecycle_path_schema_v3`；明确排除root/integrity、rendered paths、future Hn/Tn/G(e) refs和任何由root派生字段。static lifecycle root仍按`R=SHA256(RFC8785(root_preimage))`计算，但compact namespace不得再从R、H0或future governance root推导。对每个已合法预留epoch，先物化`pre_activation_reservation_root_digest`，再唯一计算`authority_epoch_key=SHA256(RFC8785({authority_epoch_id,compact_lifecycle_path_schema_sha256,pre_activation_reservation_root_digest,program_id}))`并编码为full lowercase 64-hex；`static_contract_source_kind=H0_REGISTRY|PRE_ACTIVATION_BUNDLE`只作provenance evidence，不进入key。H0-backed与H0-less对同一program/epoch/reservation-root/path-schema必须得到同key，H0只能copy/verify，不得re-key。随后才计算`target_attempt_path_key`、适用的`action_start_claim_key`、primary-choice/operation-intent/observation/safety-choice keys并按frozen leaf map作typed substitution。future H0/G/Q/Z/ready/sync/self、rendered path和source-kind都不得回流key；同epoch双root/key/namespace、旧root-derived alias、双写或兼容读取均invalid。

这套G-root authority 的初始basis是exact epoch-scoped accepted Ck plan/review、accepted Hn immutable manifest、accepted Tn与remote-accepted/aligned G(e)；pre-G bundle/reservation/event+integrity intake、Q、terminal-ready、terminal-incident intake、stale-sync closure及positive post-Z intake各自只使用6.3明示的narrow special root，不得冒充G-root。它们都没有Evidence/NumericFact/S2/S3/report/human/release权威。每次 materialization 都必须从 RFC 8785 canonical preimage 生成 one-shot `derived_transition_grant_id`，preimage 至少绑定 `schema_version/program_id/authority_epoch_id/root_digest/lifecycle_control_subject_family/transition_subject_kind/transition_subject_id/transition_action_id/materialization_parent_commit/tree/expected_remote_ref+parent/sorted_exact_predecessor_refs/external_payload_sha256_or_null_sentinel/payload_actor_identity/role/materializer_identity/role/commit_writer_identity/role/normalized_fixed_output_path/expected_changed_paths/authorized_at/expires_at/one_shot/deny_surface_digest`。这里的`root_digest`是已计算的static root，不是grant自摘要；current head/tree只进入grant。generic ticket lifecycle transition使用`transition_subject_kind=ticket_attempt`；applicability、raw trigger、pre-Q/Q interrupt、pre-G event/integrity、positive post-Z handoff与terminal-ready incident使用各自不可互换的subject kind。Q前validator从已提交G-root重算；Q后仅对frozen closure subset使用`q_closure_lifecycle_root_v1`；terminal-ready current sync、all-ready incident intake与unique stale attempt closure分别使用其专用root/action subset；positive Z后只用由Z full ref＋non-self-referential template导出的successor bundle/handoff/escape roots。grant不作为另一个待预先写入仓库的receipt。preimage不得包含future output commit/tree/blob/raw digest、future candidate object identity或grant自身digest。路径、actor、epoch、subject family、entry/root variant、predecessor、payload、local/remote parent任一不符即fail closed；control-plane grant只能写指定对象，永远不能执行ticket申请的substantive action。

所有 lifecycle object 使用 `lifecycle_envelope_v1`，不得在对象内部保存自身完整文件/Git blob digest。envelope 固定包含：schema/action、`authority_epoch_id`、`transition_subject_kind/id`、适用时的 ticket-attempt、规范化 predecessor refs、`payload_actor_identity/role`、`materializer_identity/role`、`commit_writer_identity/role`、`external_payload_media_type`、external payload 原始字节的 base64、`external_payload_sha256`、canonical grant preimage/digest、derived grant、UTC RFC3339 `authorized_at/consumed_at`、normalized output path，以及 `integrity.canonical_body_sha256`。`canonical_body_sha256` 只对排除整个 `integrity` 字段后的 RFC 8785 canonical JSON body 计算；完整 raw-file/Git-blob digest 由下一 transition 的 predecessor ref 或独立 post-materialization verification 记录，不能自指。external payload 原始字节必须可由 base64 逐字节还原；materializer 物化的是 envelope，并非声称 envelope 与 external payload 本身逐字节相等。无 external payload 的 terminal/activation transition 使用固定 UTF-8 sentinel `null_external_payload_v1` 及其 SHA-256。predecessor refs 按 `(object_type,authority_epoch_id,transition_subject_id,normalized_path,raw_sha256)` 排序；repo path 使用 `/`、不得含 `..`，时间统一 UTC `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ`。H0 schema test 必须有 canonical serialization、field-order、path normalization、actor/epoch swap、predecessor reorder、null sentinel、root-template projection和 self-digest rejection fixtures。Phase 0 的 H/T/F/S/G receipts 复用该 envelope 的 serialization/integrity 规则，但在 G(e) 之前绝不能把尚未激活的 `lifecycle_governance_root_v1` 当作 authority basis；它们的 grant 只来自 6.4 已注册的四类 Phase0-specific action及各自 current total pre-G snapshot/reducer output、accepted Ck review、Fn/Sn或accepted Tn predecessor；pre-activation Z只可使用上一段严格special root。Phase1–7 ordinary tickets与pre-Q `PROGRAM-EPOCH-ROLLOVER`都从same-epoch G-root重算八类generic lifecycle grants，但前者必须使用`phase_entry_gate_registry_v1`，后者必须使用`program_governance_entry_gate_v1`/interrupt gate；root/entry/subject交叉即拒绝。Q(e)一旦建立，八类G-root issuance立即frozen；同一action ID只有绑定Q full ref、frozen subject set和action subset的Q-special closure variant可继续，另有terminal-ready current-sync、terminal-incident intake、stale-sync closure、Z与next-plan special roots。pre-Q interrupt与stale-sync variants是仅有的registered control-supersession contexts：可以让existing generic authority-decision action写strict denial-only receipt，但不得重绑old candidate、签authorized decision或授权sync/other write；Q-root与任一ready root都不得授权applicability/new candidate/new authorized authority/start、downstream activation或scope expansion。

ticket owner、applicability evaluator、candidate author、candidate reviewer、execution-authority issuer、executor、result reviewer、lifecycle materializer与commit writer都是 registry 中的显式角色。applicability/candidate/review/authority decision 可以先作为不可变 external payload 存在；“external”这里只表示尚未写入 Git 的结构化 payload，不表示网络或第三方系统。evaluator/reviewer始终只读，只返回payload；materializer只能把未经修改的payload bytes嵌入canonical envelope，不能改finding。凡`payload_actor_role`属于`plan_reviewer|phase0_hn_reviewer|applicability_evaluator|candidate_reviewer|packet_reviewer|result_reviewer|next_plan_reviewer|pre_activation_governance_reviewer`，都必须同时满足`payload_actor_identity != lifecycle_materializer_identity`且`payload_actor_identity != commit_writer_identity`。所有 action-bearing ticket 还必须满足 `candidate_reviewer_identity != candidate_author_identity`、`execution_authority_issuer_identity != candidate_author_identity`、`execution_authority_issuer_identity != candidate_reviewer_identity`、`execution_authority_issuer_identity != executor_identity`、`result_reviewer_identity != executor_identity`。conditional ticket 还必须满足 `applicability_evaluator_identity != ticket_owner_identity`、`applicability_evaluator_identity != candidate_author_identity`；evaluator不能修改required-if inputs。candidate author 是否可以兼任 executor 不是自由解释项：只有该 stable ticket registry 显式写 `candidate_author_may_execute=true` 时才允许，默认 false；即使允许，也不能削弱前述 reviewer/issuer/commit-writer 分离。角色不足、identity 不可验证、pairwise constraint 不满足或 payload 未绑定 exact object 时停止，不得合并角色来抢进度。

pre-H0 bundle 对 special flows 的角色合同是closed-world，而不是只列几条不等式的开放集合：stable slots必须恰好覆盖 commit writer、event actor/intake materializer、plan/Hn author、stopper、snapshot evaluator、pre-activation reviewer/terminal materializer、post-Z handoff materializer、terminal-incident materializer、terminal-sync ticket owner/candidate author/candidate reviewer/authority issuer/executor/lifecycle materializer/result reviewer、stale-closure reviewer；每个special action都必须在`special_action_profile_map`中绑定logical actor role、materializer role、path-template key和root variant。slot缺失、多余、action无映射、映射引用未知slot、grant actor binding与map不一致、或任一冻结inequality失败都使grant为空。minimum separation不是“所有人必须彼此不同”：未列安全/作者分离关系的slots可以复用actual identity，positive fixture必须至少证明一组允许相等的角色可达。

registry 对每张ordinary ticket及conditional `PROGRAM-EPOCH-ROLLOVER`必须固定`lifecycle_control_subject_family=ordinary_phase_ticket|program_governance_rollover_ticket`、`applicability_mode=always|conditional`、canonical raw/effective `required_if_expression`、ordered input refs/digests、适用时的`required_for_phase_exit_when_applicable:boolean`、evaluation scope/time、evaluator/materializer identities和receipt path template。`always` ticket由registry静态重算为`applicable`，applicability attempt/payload/receipt/selector binding均为null/not_applicable，禁止伪造A-attempt。`conditional` ticket初始为`pending_evaluation`；对应phase gate或program-governance `initial_applicability/reevaluate_applicability/retry_target`先唯一选择target/A，字符串本身不创建candidate、不推进lifecycle、不授action；gate得到零个或多个target时grant为空。

同一 `(authority_epoch_id,phase_id,stable_ticket_id,target_ticket_attempt_id)` scope 使用独立 append-only identity `applicability_evaluation_attempt_id={target_ticket_attempt_id}-APP-A{a}`。`a` 从 0 开始、无前导零、按数值排序；A0 predecessor=null。只有 candidate 尚不存在、当前已有唯一 valid head Aa、且重新冻结的 `required_if_input_set_sha256` 与 Aa 不同，才允许 A(a+1) exact 绑定 Aa；同 input重复评估、gap、duplicate/sibling、跨 epoch/phase/ticket/target replay均非法。input X→Y→X 必须形成 A2，不能复用 A0。candidate一旦物化，普通A-chain sealed；后续input drift使本票stop/phase PASS blocked，PROGRAM-EPOCH-ROLLOVER的plan-change修改/撤回、stop、mandatory全部走interrupt/supersession gate。前attempt terminal且retry gate唯一选出新target后，新target从A0开始；program retry/reevaluation effective required-if必须消费latch，不能出现latch=true但selected head=false的accepted状态。

evaluator payload schema 固定为 `ticket_applicability_evaluation_payload_v1`、media type=`application/vnd.fin.ticket-applicability-evaluation+json;version=1`，至少包含 program/contract/epoch/root/phase/stable-ticket/target-attempt/A-id/index、evaluation snapshot commit/tree、exact registry ref、raw/effective required-if expression schema/digest、ordered immutable input refs 与 `required_if_input_set_sha256=SHA256(RFC8785(...))`、previous A receipt full ref、candidate fixed path不存在证明、scope/time/evaluator identity、boolean result、唯一 `true→applicable/false→not_applicable` verdict和expected envelope path；program-governance payload还必须分开保存current raw bits、first-trigger/latch refs和effective result。payload 不得写 future receipt/candidate identity或 self/grant digest。`authority_epoch_key`与`target_attempt_path_key`均是完整lowercase 64-hex、不得截断。所有conditional ticket（含cleanup obligation）的唯一applicability路径为`configs/l/{authority_epoch_key}/{target_attempt_path_key}/a/A{a}.json`；同target interrupt唯一为`.../i/I{i}.json`；raw trigger/generic indexed receipt唯一为`.../g/R{r}.json`。`a|i|r`必须使用canonical ASCII uint64 decimal `0|[1-9][0-9]{0,19}`、范围0..18446744073709551615；overflow、负号、`+`、前导零、空串或Unicode digit均拒绝。H0固定canonical repo root=`D:/FIN_Insight_Agent`与`resolved_absolute_path_budget_utf16_code_units=240`：三个full-64 key的action-start最坏绝对路径=232；A/I/R uint64-max=189；pre-G/post-Z special path最坏=196。Phase2冻结actual candidate registry时还须逐项用同一reducer重算。envelope仍保存完整phase/stable/target/A/I/R identities；key mismatch、多logical target同key、collision alias、old `configs/audits/lifecycle/...`、new/old双路径、case variant、`..`、trailing dot/space、backslash输入或任一resolved absolute path>240均fail closed。raw payload bytes只嵌入该envelope，不另写临时raw文件。

同一target attempt的leaf map恰为：candidate=`c.json`、candidate-review=`v.json`、execution-authority=`u.json`、primary action start=`s/{action_start_claim_key}.json`、result=`r.json`、result-review=`w.json`、terminal=`t.json`、downstream activation=`d.json`、stale review/authority/result/result-review/terminal依次为`x/v.json|x/u.json|x/r.json|x/w.json|x/t.json`。完整schema/action/phase/ticket/attempt/A/I/R identities只在envelope/claim内保存，不得再次拼入目录或文件名。leaf与object kind互换、旧长leaf别名、同一对象同时出现在compact与descriptive路径、同一logical target映射两个keys、两个logical targets映射同一key、或同一action出现两个accepted claims，均为identity failure。

applicability materialization 使用 `transition_subject_kind=applicability_evaluation_attempt`、subject ID=A-attempt，并在 canonical grant preimage 额外绑定 stable/target ID、registry/expression/input-set digests、previous A、candidate namespace empty-set、fixed path与 exact expected changed path。materializer 写前重算 snapshot→parent 无漂移、candidate仍不存在、A-chain唯一连续、A(a>0) inputs确已变化；output commit必须是 parent direct single-parent child且只改该 envelope path。raw trigger variant使用`program_governance_raw_trigger`并绑定source sequence/raw bits/input-set/previous R；pre-Q/Q supersession使用`program_governance_trigger_interrupt`并绑定sealed candidate或Q、previous R/I、strictly-growing event-set、new event/evidence/supersession kind、first-trigger/latch及fixed path；pre-G event/integrity分别使用`pre_g_governance_event|governance_stream_integrity_failure`并绑定exact seed/source/sequence/anomaly/current remote parent。positive post-Z handoff和terminal incident各自绑定Z successor或ready origin/intake root。stop/mandatory的first true与novel true→true、plan-change true/false mutation、integrity coverage各有独立schema；所有subject/root/path不可互换。R/I不得产生applicability verdict、target、candidate或substantive action grant。

`applicability_attempt_selector_v1` 只扫描 governance root冻结的 canonical branch/registry/namespace，返回唯一连续 `A0→...→Aa` chain head；head input-set digest必须等于 selector base tree当前重算值且 candidate尚不存在。空 namespace、gap/duplicate/sibling、invalid newer object、head stale、cross-epoch ref、path overwrite、多个 latest或 candidate已存在都返回 empty，不能回退旧 A，也不能按 mtime/lexicographic/current pointer选择。selector preimage绑定 epoch/phase/ticket/target/base commit/tree/registry/namespace、按数值排序的完整 receipt refs、current input digest与 selected head，其 SHA-256 由 candidate/reducer重算，不另增 selector receipt。

conditional candidate 必须绑定 selector-selected、verdict=applicable 的 exact A receipt，包括 selector version/preimage digest、A ID/index、receipt commit/tree/path/blob/raw SHA-256/bytes/canonical-body、external-payload SHA、registry/expression/input-set digests与 verdict；candidate derived grant把该 full ref作为 predecessor。只写 boolean `applicable`、只写 path、绑定旧/not-applicable/stale/sibling/cross-epoch receipt或 materialization parent重算 selector为空时 fail closed。candidate 后只认其 frozen binding，不动态改绑。`not_applicable` 合法组合固定为 lifecycle=`planned`、terminal=null、execution authority=null/not_applicable、downstream activation=`not_applicable`、无 candidate/action/result，且必须由 selector-selected exact receipt证明 required-if=false。

八类固定generic lifecycle action family如下；Phase1–7 ordinary ticket与pre-Q program-governance rollover ticket均可按各自registry/entry gate复用，每个ticket attempt独立实例化、fixed path、one-shot、消费后`granted=false`：

| Lifecycle action family | 精确 predecessor / external payload | 唯一可写对象 | 显式禁止 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ticket_applicability_receipt_materialization_write` | exact phase/program A gate；active raw-trigger/interrupt；bootstrap pre-G event/integrity；Q integrity；positive post-Z successor；或all-ready terminal-incident intake root，subject/root/path必须匹配 | fixed applicability A、raw-trigger R、rollover I、pre-G/Q/post-Z/terminal incident或integrity I；A只算applicability，R/I只冻结事件/anomaly | candidate、review、authority、result、terminal、activation、substantive output、覆盖旧A/R/I、entry/root/kind互换、R/I改candidate/applicability |
| `ticket_candidate_contract_materialization_write` | G-root ordinary/pre-Q：always static applicable或selector-selected exact A＋entry/retry；terminal-ready：exact observation/source projection/incident snapshot＋`PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01` gate；全部再绑定prerequisite与author payload。重试只允许前attempt failure/stopped/held，terminal PASS后不得same-epoch retry | 当前 attempt candidate contract/manifest fixed path；冻结root/gate/applicability或observation与derived grant consumption | applicability、review、execution authority、result、terminal、activation、substantive output、动态改绑 A/observation、PASS后重试、root/gate互换 |
| `ticket_candidate_review_receipt_materialization_write` | exact immutable candidate＋作者分离只读 candidate-review payload；通常继承candidate root；stale-sync candidate-only时只允许预注册closure root下的`superseded_by_terminal_incident` review并同时绑定old root | fixed candidate-review receipt，或唯一fixed stale-review path | candidate 修改、execution authority、动作执行、任意root rebind |
| `ticket_execution_authority_receipt_materialization_write` | same subject/root candidate-review PASS＋独立 authority-issuer decision payload＋budget/allowlist/expiry/rollback；sync另绑observation/source projection/incident snapshot；pre-Q late-I或stale-sync review-PASS/no-decision只可绑定exact supersession root/evidence走denial-only variant | fixed execution-authority decision receipt；authorized才授registry actions；rollover authorized receipt commit=Q且只授quiesce/revoke；terminal-sync authorized只授bounded Project OS/source-doc sync；supersession denial固定`not_granted/granted=false` | 被授权动作本身、result、terminal、activation、自签authority、denial变authorized、denial用于ordinary ticket、program ticket授ordinary/product action |
| `ticket_result_receipt_materialization_write` | 成功/执行后exact authority＋已消费actions＋outputs；pre-execution negative；no-action aggregate；CLS-07 sovereign branch；Q后改绑Q-root subject；terminal-sync current attempt改绑ready root、observation/action-parent与exact sync diff/evidence；stale-sync改绑closure root并按remote-accepted start claim分类 | fixed frozen result/failure receipt；普通negative写action_started=false及claim=null；CLS-07只嵌入Owner payload；current sync只记录bounded write；started stale sync必须记录actual/unknown与actual diff | candidate/review/authority回写、terminal/activation、started伪装未开始、把sovereign/Q/ready/stale closure泛化 |
| `ticket_result_review_receipt_materialization_write` | exact frozen result＋作者分离只读 result-review payload；继承G/Q/current-ready root；stale-sync只允许closure root及必要时唯一incident-supersession review | fixed result-review receipt或fixed stale supersession-review path | result repair、terminal/activation、old/new root任意交换 |
| `ticket_terminal_receipt_materialization_write` | exact result review；PASS只在review PASS；stale-sync必须消费其stage-bound closure review且只能FAIL/STOP；其余按registry规则 | exactly one fixed terminal receipt；此时才允许non-null `ticket_terminal_status` | downstream activation、result/candidate 修改、stale PASS |
| `ticket_downstream_activation_receipt_materialization_write` | terminal PASS＋registry downstream policy/consumer/rollback；仅G-root ordinary/pre-Q路径可用，Q-root无此variant | fixed downstream-activation receipt | execution authority、额外 consumer、后续 substantive action、任何Q后activation；rollover successor只由Z=superseded产生 |

`phase_entry_eligible=true`只能成为ordinary applicability evaluation或candidate materialization的一个predecessor；它不生成audit/network/model/implementation grant，也不能用于PROGRAM-EPOCH-ROLLOVER。program-governance entry/interrupt gates只允许该meta-ticket的pre-Q lifecycle、late escalation及Q-special closure，不授quiescence以外的substantive action。每个phase/ticket/program registry必须提前固定target selector、A/I schema/media type/path/preimage/selector/binding、candidate request schema、八种generic action family的G-root、Q-root、terminal-ready current-sync、positive post-Z、all-ready terminal-incident与stale-sync closure适用variant、supersession denial-only authority-decision variant、actor identities、path、expiry/consumption、predecessor和deny surface；pre-activation五对象及expected hashes/E0→E1 vector、remote identity与authority-neutral exact-ref mechanism/control profile、bundle/root/grant、pre-G event/integrity receipt、exact-Hn-review exclusion、total class/transition reducer、terminal-ready origin/stream/root-map/observation/source-projection/incident/stale-closure schemas同样必须先冻结。每个primary substantive Action ID还必须逐项注册`substantive_action_start_claim_v5`的claim-key/path/authority-basis/root/adapter/fence/idempotency/result binding、default-deny adapter capability、initial primary-choice schema/key/backend/locator/readback、`physical_operation_intent_cas_v1`的key/backend/locator/generation/predecessor-observation/result-boundary、observation receipt、`prearmed_safety_suboperation_choice_cas_v3`的key/backend/locator/branch/member/root/target/adapter/fence binding、total physical-operation/state matrix、readback/same-server-operation proof，以及按`ordinary_same_claim_recovery|q_quiescence|index_under_lock_recovery`三类互斥basis事前预装的exact physical recovery members，以及terminal Git专用`terminal_git_stale_no_retry_lifecycle_closure_v2`；query不选effect，非query缺current intent winner时call count=0，recovery缺safety winner或其自身operation intent时call count=0。repo输出适用时另须ticket-bound `substantive_repository_artifact_publication_v1`。`program_terminal_project_os_sync_write`明确使用finalization-specific basis而不是ordinary execution bits。Phase4–7无substantive action的票可把execution-authority写null/not_applicable，但其适用control lifecycle仍不可跳。新增action family、改变path/actor/predecessor/root variant或把control grant扩成substantive grant，都必须走6.3新authority epoch；不能临时发明。

正常负路径不得死锁。candidate-review FAIL 直接经 negative result→作者分离 result review→terminal failed/stopped；authority denied/held 经 decision receipt→negative result→result review→terminal failed/held；authority expired/revoked且remote-accepted start claim absent、`action_started=false`经negative result→review→terminal stopped；sealed rollover或terminal-sync candidate在review PASS、authority尚无时被valid control I supersede，必须先生成strict denial-only authority decision，再以exact I与该decision形成`action_started=false` negative result→review→terminal stopped/failed；不得result-before-decision。只有terminal后才可创建新attempt。remote-accepted claim已存在的started/started_unknown不得走pre-execution shortcut：ordinary/Q/IDX先按typed matrix收集query/operation receipt并消费对应事前预装member；terminal sync stale只query/readback并如实冻结non-PASS result、独立review和显式新attempt obligation；且result后旧ticket不再query/resume/cancel/compensate。Q后只能用`q_quiescence` closure；terminal-ready incident后只能用6.3逐stage `stale_sync_closure` lifecycle root；IDX只能用under-lock recovery，不能互借或把started sync伪成negative shortcut。mutation覆盖四类普通负路径、rollover/stale的review-PASS/no-authority denial-only、denial→authorized篡改、issuer身份错误、stale-sync各stage、started误走shortcut、缺exact evidence、terminal前retry、interrupt改旧candidate或直接建sibling，全部fail closed。

最低动作矩阵如下；未来 machine registry 可以增加更细动作，不能合并这些行。前16个control Action ID的remote-accepted receipt commit本身就是其唯一控制状态变更，并统一使用冻结remote identity＋exact expected-old lease；它们不再套一层substantive claim。自`phase1_audit_read_and_bounded_write`起至表末的每个primary substantive Action ID，无论名称是否含read/stop/sync，都强制继承`substantive_action_start_linearization_v1`、六类typed operation matrix、initial primary route choice、每次非query调用的one-shot operation intent、适用时独立safety-suboperation choice和result boundary；其允许范围自动包含且只包含本attempt exact start-claim/primary-choice/operation-intent/observation/safety-choice paths，不能借子协议扩大其他surface。stop/disable/rollback/recovery若要在started primary后执行，支持physical recovery的adapter必须事前预装exact tagged member：IDX=`index_under_lock_recovery`、generic Q=`q_quiescence`、active ordinary=`ordinary_same_claim_recovery`，三分支互斥；member由对应safety choice选中后，实际调用仍须自己的operation intent。old terminal sync固定走`terminal_git_stale_no_retry_lifecycle_closure_v2`，同attempt无safety member/call。active态独立动作则自己是primary并须独立claim、primary choice和first operation intent。凡输出repository artifact的行还必须消费ticket-bound publication profile；control transport不能代发。未来新增substantive action若未注册claim、adapter capability、primary choice/fence/query、per-call operation intent、typed safety recovery与publication profile（适用时）即grant=false：

| Action ID | 最早可能 grant | 允许范围 | 不随之获得的权威 |
|---|---|---|---|
| ticket_applicability_receipt_materialization_write | phase/program A；active raw-trigger；pre-Q/Q interrupt；bootstrap pre-G event/integrity；positive post-Z successor；或terminal-incident intake root，均需subject/root/path/sequence exact | one A-attempt、raw-trigger R、pre-Q/Q interrupt-I、pre-G/Q/post-Z/terminal incident或integrity-I fixed path | candidate/review/authority/result/terminal/activation/substantive action、旧A/R/I overwrite、entry/root/kind互换、R/I改applicability/candidate |
| ticket_candidate_contract_materialization_write | G-root ordinary/pre-Q entry，或terminal-ready root下exact observation/source projection/incident snapshot＋`PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01` gate＋external request | one current-attempt candidate fixed path | applicability/review/authority/result/terminal/activation/product action、A/observation rebind、root/subject互换 |
| ticket_candidate_review_receipt_materialization_write | exact candidate＋external read-only review；通常继承exact root，stale candidate-only只走预注册closure-root supersession review | one fixed candidate-review或stale-review receipt | candidate repair/authority/action、任意root rebind |
| ticket_execution_authority_receipt_materialization_write | candidate-review PASS＋independent authority decision；root/action subset exact；pre-Q late-I或stale-sync review-PASS/no-decision只可用exact supersession member写denial-only | one fixed authority-decision receipt；rollover authorized commit=Q且只授quiesce/revoke；terminal-sync authorized只授bounded sync；denial-only=`not_granted/granted=false` | 被授权动作/result/terminal/activation、denial变authorized或用于ordinary、rollover/sync授ordinary product action |
| ticket_result_receipt_materialization_write | G-root execution/negative/no-action；Q-root frozen closure；current-ready exact terminal sync；stage-bound stale-sync closure | one frozen result/failure receipt；started stale必须truthful actual/unknown | candidate/review/terminal/activation、新subject/scope、started→pre-exec shortcut |
| ticket_result_review_receipt_materialization_write | frozen result＋external read-only result review；绑定同一G/Q/current-ready root或exact stale closure root | one fixed result-review或唯一stale supersession-review receipt | repair/terminal/activation、root swap |
| ticket_terminal_receipt_materialization_write | exact result review；绑定同一G/Q/current-ready root；stale只消费closure review且non-PASS | one PASS/FAIL/STOP/HOLD terminal receipt | activation、execution authority、stale PASS |
| ticket_downstream_activation_receipt_materialization_write | terminal PASS＋exact downstream policy；Q后全部false | one fixed ordinary/pre-Q activation receipt | substantive execution、额外 consumer、任何Q后activation；rollover successor只由Z=superseded产生 |
| phase0_governance_candidate_materialization_write | per-attempt one-shot；H0需exact accepted Ck review/seed＋current complete pre-G snapshot/reducer=`accepted_Ck_no_event_to_H0`；H(n+1)需Sn＋`Sn_no_event_to_Hnext`；均用remote expected-parent acceptance | accepted Ck review receipt、C0..Ck revision/review registry、exact seed copy、source supersession、Project OS、worklog、governance config/test exact allowlist；successor仅Sn allowlist；plan path deny | pending/unsupported/uncovered anomaly/invalid stopper或remote-head drift时H/Hnext、local ref先行、terminal/review/activation receipt、plan mutation、Phase1、component adoption、产品行为 |
| phase0_review_terminal_receipt_materialization_write | exact external Hn review payload＋current total pre-G reducer选择T/F后per-attempt one-shot | 二选一 fixed Tn PASS 或 Fn FAIL receipt；external payload bytes 未经修改地嵌入 canonical envelope，aggregate review/result-review/terminal 语义与完整对象 digest 按 6.4.1 分离 | post-head event时T/F、candidate repair、successor、downstream activation、Phase 1 action |
| phase0_successor_authority_receipt_materialization_write | exact implementation-class Fn terminal FAIL＋current reducer=`Fn_implementation_no_event_to_Sn` | fixed Sn receipt，绑定 failed Hn/review/findings、snapshot与 exact H(n+1) allowlist | event时Sn、Hn overwrite、plan mutation、downstream activation、Phase 1 action |
| phase0_downstream_activation_receipt_materialization_write | current recovery lineage exact Tn terminal PASS＋logical head=Tn＋exact-seed complete no-event snapshot＋reducer=`Tn_no_event_to_G`＋expected remote parent=`pre_g_control_head_v1.materialization_parent_ref` | 只创建不移动canonical local ref的prepared G commit；G logical parent=Tn、Git parent=current materialization parent，并以lease-guarded exact-expected-old remote fast-forward CAS让fixed G(e) receipt成为唯一accepted child；remote G full ref为route boundary，本地aligned后才令Phase1/2 candidate eligible | pending/unsupported/uncovered-integrity/source-sequence drift或remote-parent CAS conflict时G、local ref先行、reset/unconstrained-force/ref-delete-or-recreate、prepared orphan冒充G、I漏绑/换序、同parent G/Z双accept、Phase0 PASS签发、P1 audit/RSH network或任何action grant |
| authority_epoch_quiesce_and_revoke | rollover candidate-review PASS＋issuer与author/reviewer/executor/requester/evaluator分离＋aligned Q cutoff | freeze G-root issuance；分类started、revoked-unstarted authorized、candidate/review-only与事前contingency；冻结Q subject set/root/intake inputs | 新 ordinary grant/start、plan write、任何activation、补签 stop/rollback、证据删除 |
| authority_epoch_terminal_receipt_materialization_write | Q-path：aligned Q＋attempt closure＋pre-Z freeze＋interrupt stream=`complete|integrity_failure_closed`；pre-activation/successor：exact seed/bundle/root/grant＋head/event/integrity table＋G absent＋独立review | one fixed Z(e)：Q parent=latest Q head；pre-activation/successor parent=current accepted integrity/control head。normal complete可superseded/stopped；integrity-closed只failed。Z只冻结destination template；Z后导出roots/projection | 绕过Q/head table、未覆盖anomaly写Z、integrity分支positive/stopped、双Z/任选、target/root重定义、future root写入Z、stop＋positive、negative带next、产品PASS/rollback虚构 |
| next_epoch_initial_plan_revision_materialization_write | exact positive Z＋由Z/template导出的successor bundle＋完整post-Z handoff set | direct child of `successor_pre_activation_control_head_v1` 的E(e+1)-C0；payload另绑lineage seed Z；only plan path | 漏event/seed、已有I仍以Z为parent、receipt/config/source/business write、H0/action grant |
| next_epoch_plan_revision_successor_materialization_write | same-epoch、accepted Ck尚不存在且exact Cj review P0/P1/P2>0 | logical Cj successor/direct child of current allowed plan-control head；only plan path | cross-epoch parent、PASS后追加Cj、receipt/business write |
| phase1_audit_read_and_bounded_write | G(e) candidate eligibility后exact P1-xx review＋该票execution-authority | 该票exact repo/data metadata只读及bounded Phase1 artifacts | 其他P1、src/product data mutation、component execution |
| phase2_network_research | G(e) candidate eligibility＋exact RSH review/execution authority＋registry-mapped relevant P1 constraint terminal/activation | 官方 docs/repo/spec/paper 调研与来源快照 | 未绑定P1 constraint、external source product call、package download |
| phase3_qualification_harness_and_execution_evidence_write | exact QL candidate-review PASS＋execution-authority receipt | 物化 reviewed external harness payload到 exact scripts/tests/configs qualification paths，并写 bounded execution evidence；lifecycle receipts 由上面的 control actions单独写 | candidate/review/authority/terminal/activation、production src、pyproject/current config/data、component promotion、domain authority |
| phase4_adr_and_migration_registry_write | Phase 1/2/3 required terminal/activation＋exact ADR ticket execution-authority receipt | 该 ADR ticket 的 exact architecture/migration registry paths | production implementation、consumer cutover、其他 ADR ticket |

| index_retirement_preflight_and_recovery_evidence_write | `IDX-RETIRE-PREP-01` candidate-review PASS＋独立 execution-authority receipt | D current index metadata/content 的只读 digest、Z/scratch snapshot/export/restore drill 或 isolated rebuild，以及 exact retirement evidence paths；不改 D current index | delete、retrieval suspension、current route、其他数据 |
| index_retirement_execution_interlock | exact `IDX-RETIRE-01 authority`＋PREP terminal/frozen consumer registry/qualified atomic durable-lock adapter；claim预装normal release与`index_under_lock_recovery` actor/member，另冻结initial primary choice、acquire operation-intent和IDX safety-choice | claim accepted后pending-acquire/accepting；primary choice只选acquire route，backend atomic acquire必须由generation-0 operation intent授权。commit原子得到lock＋draining；qualified atomic abort须同时命中正式14字段：`index_retirement_interlock_status=failed`、`interlock_acquire_observation_state=atomic_not_acquired`、`old_index_consumer_admission_state=accepting`、logical/acquire counts=1、generation/fence=null、lock absent、drain/delete未开始、delete counts=0、release=false、same-claim reacquire=false；随后才freeze abort result→independent result review→terminal failed，terminal outcome不是tuple前置字段。unknown/timeout/inconsistent readback保持blocked recovery/release义务；接管须独立IDX safety choice，实际recovery/release仍须新operation intent。acquired后持续监督到under-lock outcome→independent release-safety review→explicit release→lifecycle result | claim或primary choice冒充lock/acquire intent、atomic write-set拆分、abort同claim重试、unknown按abort收口、自动expiry解锁、owner-death后accepting、无review或无operation-intent release、delete、未登记consumer、无锁继续 |
| old_index_delete | exact `IDX-RETIRE-01 authority`绑定PREP terminal、frozen target/recovery、Owner通知；interlock仍`acquired+atomic_acquired`后另建delete claim，预装`index_under_lock_recovery` restore/recovery member并冻结primary/operation/safety choice locators | claim accepted只commit一个logical invocation；initial winner=`dispatch_primary`只选择delete route，generation-0 delete operation intent才授权第一次physical delete；同一lock generation持续held且final manifest逐target no-follow/identity复核。partial/unknown先query/readback，只有旧generation durable fenced、admission blocked、独立IDX safety choice选中exact member且recovery operation intent赢得CAS时才可恢复，且全部发生在lifecycle result前 | 第二claim/primary winner/new-key、primary choice冒充delete intent、同predecessor intent双winner、unknown盲重放或换member、新consumer、lock/manifest drift、result后旧票恢复、root本身、范围外数据、未预装IDX member |
| legacy_source_retire_delete | Phase 7 CLS-03 execution-authority receipt | active imports/registry/consumers=0 的 exact paths | historical evidence deletion |
| phase7_dependency_cleanup_write | Phase 7 CLS-02 execution-authority receipt | exact unconsumed dependency/lock/config/service/image targets，且 no-current-reference proof | active/shared dependency、历史证据、legacy source |
| phase7_closeout_documentation_and_registry_write | Phase 7 exact CLS-01/CLS-04/CLS-05 execution-authority receipt | 各票 exact feasibility/operations/Project-OS/docs/registry allowlist | release、source delete、其他 CLS ticket |
| program_terminal_project_os_sync_write | `program_terminal_ready_reducer_v1`非null＋exact `terminal_ready_observation_v1`/source-projection/incident snapshot＋`PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01` candidate-review PASS＋独立execution-authority＋finalization-specific grant；action parent必须与observation/retry grant一致，副作用前另完成本action exact start claim、initial primary choice和generation-0 publication operation intent，并逐字节匹配`terminal_git_publication_adapter_effect_capability_profile_v3` | 恰好三条：`docs/project_os/capability_status_ledger.jsonl`、`docs/project_os/current_context_pack.zh-CN.md`、`docs/project_os/root_cause_issue_ledger.jsonl`，resulting-size caps=4/2/4 MiB、total≤10 MiB；Git adapter只允许query与一次expected-old direct-child initiate，continue/complete/cancel/compensation=false、internal retry=0；readback精确分类exact-new/exact-old/valid-competitor/foreign/unknown；只同步ready及immutable evidence，post-action验证source projection未漂移 | 缺capability或choice/intent/observation locator、control transport冒充publication adapter、unknown后再次push、product/release/migration/delete、计划/source worklog/R14、终局事实预写或改写、普通ticket、domain authority、第四路径、whole-commit摘要冒充source projection、旧observation/grant/claim重放 |
| evidence_admission | Phase 6 `ACC-S1 execution_authority_receipt` | qualified Candidate→Evidence | NumericFact/S2/S3 |
| numeric_fact_and_s2_authority | Phase 6 `ACC-S2 execution_authority_receipt` | qualified FactObservation→NumericFact/bridge | S3/report/release |
