| report_candidate_generation | Phase 6 exact `ACC-S4-CANDIDATE-Cnn execution_authority_receipt` | non-released report candidate | current reader deliverable、qualified-human approval、publication/release |
| qualified_human_approval | Phase 6 `ACC-S4 execution_authority_receipt`；其 predecessor 同时绑定 `ACC-S4-CANDIDATE terminal PASS` 与独立 downstream-activation receipt | exact S4 case/report/review packet 与 denominator | product acceptance、publication/release |
| qualified_human_product_acceptance | Phase 6 `ACC-S5 execution_authority_receipt`；其 predecessor 绑定 `ACC-S4 terminal PASS` digest＋downstream activation | exact end-user workflow/product acceptance packet | publication/release |
| product_publication_release | conditional ordinary ticket `RELEASE-EXECUTION-01` 的candidate-review PASS＋independent execution-authority receipt；其candidate predecessor逐字节绑定CLS-07 release result/review/terminal与exact publication request | 仅该ticket冻结的exact product/version/deployment/consumer/publication scope、operator、start/expiry、one-shot action、rollback与post-action result | 任何未列 scope；Owner decision、CLS-07 receipt、RELEASE candidate/result/terminal本身都不是执行授权 |

Phase 3 的 candidate contract 先由 `ticket_candidate_contract_materialization_write` 单独物化；candidate request payload 必须冻结待写 harness/config/test 的完整 external payload digest、exact changed-path allowlist 与 expected output paths。作者分离 candidate reviewer 在只读 external payload 与 committed manifest 都通过后，authority issuer 才可签 execution decision；随后还必须让该exact primary start claim在冻结remote/ref赢得R/I/Q expected-old竞争，才允许`phase3_qualification_harness_and_execution_evidence_write`物化reviewed harness并执行有界证据写入。有repo输出时由claim-bound artifact profile发布，control transport不能代发。candidate、candidate review、execution authority、result、result review、terminal、activation分别由6.4.1对应control action写入，qualification execution action不能写这些lifecycle receipts。失败append-only，不能运行后补写candidate来反向授权既有动作。qualification allowlist只能包含`scripts/qualification`、`tests/qualification`、`configs/qualification`、`configs/audits`、qualification research/worklog等candidate-specific路径；不得修改production `src`、`pyproject.toml`、正式依赖锁、current product config/data或domain authority。

停止/卸载和清理不是 install/write 的隐含反向动作。candidate只能预声明/request，所有 substantive grants 在candidate中为false。active态独立stop/disable/cleanup须自己的authority、claim、initial primary choice和每次非query operation intent；若收口started primary，则必须在parent claim接受前预装exact tagged member，并按对象类型由独立safety choice唯一选择ordinary/Q/IDX physical basis，实际调用仍须自己的operation intent；terminal Git stale只走query/readback与non-PASS lifecycle closure。failure、stop、expiry只能触发消费事前authority/member；lifecycle result/terminal一旦存在，旧票不得再做任何physical stop/compensation/recovery，必须用新registered obligation ticket。candidate/shadow/scaffold delete同样须在创建前预授权exact roots；缺原授权、已过期或target漂移时，由selected `cleanup_obligation_expansion_v1`为exact source attempt×target生成content-addressed ticket，完整经过applicability→candidate→review→authority→claim→primary choice→delete operation intent→call才可删除。family/source ID不授权且禁止batch。candidate绑定source attempt/terminal/result、obligation/target、原授权不可用理由、manifest、retention与no-current/shared/admitted/import/consumer proof；只能授对应cleanup action。消费时仍绑定exact process/service/container/env/image、route/surface/root、shared-reference proof、failure evidence retention和post-action receipt。目标出现shared/promoted/admitted/current引用、D current indexes、冻结failure/corpus/receipt、legacy/current source、其他slice或identity不确定时保持false并停止。candidate/result/terminal/trigger冒充grant、未预装或错branch member、缺operation intent、result后旧票恢复、Q后独立stop claim、family/cross-obligation借权均fail closed。

Phase 5 最多取得 production integration code/test/config/lock 写入、qualification-only data、shadow 和 dual-read 权限；它不能签发 Evidence、NumericFact、S2/S3 judgment、reader deliverable、human approval 或 release。Phase 6 的 ACC downstream activation 只表示相邻内部 ticket 或 CUTOVER applicability 可以评估，不执行 current product side effect。实际 `current_consumer_cutover` 必须另走稳定 `CUTOVER-Sn` ticket：same-epoch/target 的 selector-selected exact applicability receipt绑定对应 ACC-Sn terminal PASS＋activation、exact consumer scope和`current_consumer_cutover_requested`；`required_for_phase_exit_when_applicable=true`，只有 verdict=applicable 才能创建精确绑定该 A receipt的 candidate request，后者再绑定 exact consumer/feature switch、operator、last-active qualified route、rollback/post-action fixed paths；只读 candidate review后，再由独立 execution-authority授权 one-shot switch。result/post-action、只读 result review与terminal随后分开。rollback只回到已验证旧 route，不能扩新 consumer。若 frozen request=false，selected exact receipt令整票not_applicable且不得出现candidate/grant；不得把downstream activation的not_applicable或通用activation冒充ticket applicability/cutover authority。

Phase 6 也不是一次性打开全部下游。`ACC-S1`、`ACC-S2`、`ACC-S3` 分别先通过 candidate review 获得 execution authority，产生并审查结果，物化 terminal PASS，最后以独立 downstream activation 允许下一阶段；后序 PASS 不能补偿前序 FAIL。S4/S5 固定为：

~~~text
ACC-S3 terminal PASS + downstream activation
  -> ACC-S4-CANDIDATE-Cnn candidate review + execution authority
  -> non-released report result frozen
  -> author-separated read-only report-result review
       PASS -> ACC-S4-CANDIDATE-Cnn terminal PASS
            -> independent ACC-S4-CANDIDATE downstream activation
       FAIL -> immutable ACC-S4-CANDIDATE-Fnn terminal failure receipt
            -> new ACC-S4-CANDIDATE-C(n+1) candidate/review/authority/result
  -> ACC-S4 candidate review + execution authority
  -> exact qualified-human decisions
  -> immutable human result + denominator/binding/authority review
  -> ACC-S4 terminal PASS
  -> independent ACC-S4 downstream activation
  -> ACC-S5 candidate review + execution authority
  -> operations/security/rollback/end-user + qualified-human product-acceptance results
  -> ACC-S5 terminal PASS
  -> independent ACC-S5 downstream activation
  -> Phase 7 CLS-07 Owner release decision candidate/review/result/review/terminal
  -> if decision=release: RELEASE-EXECUTION-01 applicability evaluation
       request=false -> selected exact not-applicable receipt
       request=true  -> candidate/review/execution authority/publication/result/review/terminal
~~~

`ACC-S4-CANDIDATE-Cnn` 是 stable `ACC-S4-CANDIDATE` 的不可变 attempt；其 execution authority 只能授予 `report_candidate_generation`。result 一旦 frozen，作者不得在同一 attempt “repair”；reviewer 只读，FAIL 必须先形成 immutable failure receipt，再用新 `C(n+1)` candidate/review/authority/result 修正。只有 result-review PASS、terminal PASS 和独立 `ACC-S4-CANDIDATE downstream_activation_receipt` 都存在，才能提出 `ACC-S4` candidate。`ACC-S4` execution authority 只能授予 exact S4 `qualified_human_approval`；`ACC-S5` execution authority 可以授予 exact `qualified_human_product_acceptance`，三者都不能授予 publication/release。所有 execution-authority 阶段产生的 Evidence/NumericFact/judgment/report/human/product artifacts 在本票 terminal PASS＋downstream activation 前只能存在于 exact ticket-scoped、不可被 current consumer 读取的 namespace。

`ACC-S4.predecessor` 必须同时逐字节绑定 `ACC-S4-CANDIDATE-Cnn` terminal result digest 与该 stable ticket downstream-activation receipt identity；`ACC-S4 execution_authority_receipt` 绝不能兼任上一票激活。它还必须在 human action 前绑定 exact case/report/review packet、required-item denominator、author/reviewer/human 身份和角色分离、admit/reject/rebind/defer/reopen vocabulary、allowed action、expiry/consumption、no-current/no-release deny，以及 result/review/terminal/downstream receipt fixed paths。`ACC-S4 terminal_receipt` 必须逐字节绑定 report-review digest、immutable human-result digest、完整 denominator 与 authority review；所有 required items 必须有 terminal disposition，deny/defer/timeout 不得当 PASS。`ACC-S5.predecessor` 又必须逐字节绑定该 terminal receipt result digest 和 downstream activation receipt identity。Phase 7 只允许在 consumer/rollback/root-cause/release 条件分别满足后收口，不因“项目结束”获得广义删除权。

machine manifest 必须在`candidate_state`中存3.2的可写base轴、transition rules及pure reducer schema/version，不能预写program ready/terminal或Phase7 reducer结果；允许额外输出只读derived booleans，如`phase0_candidate_awaiting_fresh_review`、`phase6_s4_report_candidate_authorized`、`phase6_s4_human_authorized`、`phase6_s4_terminal_pass`、`phase6_s5_terminal_pass`。`effective_state`、program ready/terminal、Phase7 effective status与这些derived flags必须由validator从不可变manifest＋receipts在同一snapshot重算，不得作为transition、PASS或grant的自由输入。并行只通过各phase/ticket独立base状态表达；不得用一个粗粒度`current_phase`或长状态字符串覆盖事实。

### 6.5 可机检 ticket/slice registry

Phase 0 必须创建 machine-readable `program_authority_epoch_registry_v1` 与该 epoch 的 `program_ticket_registry_v1`。epoch registry固定E(e) predecessor/lineage seed Z(e-1)、initial materialization parent selector、accepted Ck逐字节绑定的五个pre-H0 canonical对象及expected hashes/E0→E1 vector、canonical remote identity/config projection、`git_exact_ref_lease_transport_mechanism_v1`、control profile与`lease_guarded_fast_forward_expected_old_v1`、`pre_activation_epoch_bootstrap_bundle_v4`及reservation/root/grant/subject/path、finite pre-G event/stopper principals与per-source receipt sequences、exact Hn review排除规则、`pre_g_transition_snapshot_v1`/total class/head-transition+stream-integrity reducers、plan/C/H/F/S/T/G identities与互斥remote acceptance、root/activation、raw epoch states、rollover synthetic `phase_id=program-governance`、entry/interrupt/pre-activation gates、requested/completeness subpredicates、全部8态trigger/hold/mandatory/stop reducers、G/Q/terminal-ready/pre-activation/Z typed roots与selectors、candidate-nonterminal freeze/post-Z outcome projection、positive post-Z handoff、`terminal_ready_incident_origin_snapshot_v1`/reservation/intake-root/stream/effect、`governance_control_roots_v1` map/digest、terminal-ready observation/source-projection、stale-sync closure和denial-only supersession decision、observed runtime baseline和cross-epoch continuity，并逐项列齐4类Phase0 bootstrap和4类epoch actions。ticket registry中每个ordinary ID/attempt/receipt都带exact epoch，且提前注册独立`RELEASE-EXECUTION-01`与`PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01`；每票至少包含stable ID、attempt template、capability family、全部actors、applicability mode/target selector/A-attempt schema/path/preimage/selector/binding、required-if、phase-exit flag、inputs/outputs/predecessors、authority、budget/TokenBudgetBasis、acceptance/stop/rollback、lifecycle/terminal/activation，以及8类generic receipt paths。registry逐票冻结8类generic actions及其G/Q/current-ready/positive-post-Z/terminal-incident/stale-sync/pre-G-event+integrity root variants、control-supersession denial-only subset与ordinary/program subject-family gates；每个primary substantive Action ID另逐项冻结start-claim schema/key/path、authority/root basis、remote lease/fence/idempotency、default-deny adapter capability、initial primary-choice CAS、per-call operation-intent generation/CAS、observation receipt、independent safety-choice CAS、8×6 physical-operation/6×6 route-state matrices、same-server-operation proof、typed no-call predecessor、result boundary、三类互斥prearmed physical recovery members、terminal Git stale lifecycle及repo-output publication profile，terminal sync另冻结具体Git publication adapter capability，IDX另冻结使用正式typed axes的exact14字段atomic-abort projection、durable consumer admission、crash-fail-closed interlock、under-lock outcome/release-safety review/final-manifest第1–16门。machine validator证明16类control总清单无缺项/重复/组间ID或entry-root互换，同时不把start claim、primary choice、operation intent、safety choice、recovery member或artifact profile误计为第17类control。`candidate_author_may_execute`显式boolean、缺省false；no-action authority与no-downstream activation按null/not_applicable，不能替代ticket applicability。

ticket registry还必须使用三个正交、不可由candidate选择的分类轴。`registry_entry_kind`只允许`phase_source|cleanup_expansion_control|cleanup_obligation_instance|phase_close_control|program_governance_control`；`cleanup_frontier_role`只允许`target_producer|non_target_source|not_in_frontier`；`phase_exit_semantics`只允许`must_pass|terminal_evidence_for_aggregate|not_in_phase_exit`。

H0另冻结closed-world `cleanup_capable_surface_to_target_kind_registry_v1`与完整`action_cleanup_target_capability_registry_v1`，不能只列开放式示例。前者的v1初始persistent surface/target-kind枚举恰为：downloaded source、package、model、image、environment、process、service、container、run artifact、index、database、table、data namespace、shadow artifact/namespace、migration scaffold、snapshot、export、restore-drill artifact、scratch index、scratch data；任何新增kind都先改registry并走新authority epoch。Git内受retention保护的immutable receipt或bounded source evidence单独标`retained_evidence`，不因可写就自动成为cleanup target。每个现有action ID必须完整列出`allowed_output_surface_classes`；pure reducer计算`possible_cleanup_target_kinds=union(surface→kind mapping)`及`cleanup_target_capability=possible IFF possible_cleanup_target_kinds!=[]`，字段不可由registry作者独立手填。未知action/surface/kind、允许任一persistent cleanup-capable surface却宣称empty/never、或同时翻转surface/capability/role都fail closed；新增action或改变surface映射必须新authority epoch。

在现行action set中，`package_model_image_download`、`qualification_service_install_start`、`local_fixture_benchmark`、`external_source_call`、`provider_model_call`、`qualification_index_or_data_write`、`shadow_artifact_write`、`production_integration_code_test_config_lock_write`、`dual_read_compare`和`index_retirement_preflight_and_recovery_evidence_write`按现行允许面都必须派生possible；其中PREP明确派生snapshot/export/scratch-index/scratch-data kinds。其余action仍逐一列入registry并由surface映射重算，而不是因未出现在本句便缺省never。任何benchmark/provider/harness若要派生never，其action schema必须机器性禁止持久run/artifact/data surface，只允许retained bounded evidence。`action_allowlist`中任一action派生possible IFF `cleanup_frontier_role=target_producer`；`non_target_source`只允许no-action aggregate/decision或全部actions派生never且authority明确`cleanup_capable_target_creation_allowed=false`。普通QL component candidate是`phase_source+target_producer或non_target_source+terminal_evidence_for_aggregate`：适用时必须真实terminal/result-review，但failed/stopped/held可由完整family DECISION吸收为EXCLUDE/HOLD，不要求伪PASS；QL DECISION、CLEANUP-INDEX aggregate和一般required source是`must_pass`。selected obligation按applicability进入must-pass或exact NA；close/rollover不进phase exit。分类由pure oracle从exact action allowlist冻结，candidate只能绑定exact ref/digest，不能按ID前缀、运行结果或自身需要改成员。

所有action-bearing `phase_source` result都必须携带`cleanup_target_manifest_v1`和actual action/resource inventory digest。target producer允许non-empty或canonical empty，但二者都须与inventory重算一致；non-target必须canonical empty。若non-target result观察到任一cleanup-capable target，truthful result保留并terminal failed，`source_exit_ready=false`，pure completeness/registry-role violation锁存mandatory rollover；新epoch通过continuity matrix将旧target纳入reclassified producer/expansion，不得靠拒绝manifest遗忘已创建资源。Phase4 migration registry对任何可创建scaffold/shadow/route/artifact的MIG slice使用同一等价规则。role/action不兼容、实际target漏manifest、failed candidate被DECISION吸收后target从frontier消失均fail closed。

Phase 0 不把 P0-01..P0-10 冒充十张 ordinary tickets。它注册一个特殊 `phase0_governance_aggregate_v1`，attempt=`H{n}`，采用第 6.2 节专用 lifecycle：Hn aggregate candidate → external read-only Hn review → exactly one Tn/Fn aggregate terminal → PASS 时 G0 aggregate activation；FAIL 时，implementation finding走Fn→Sn→H(n+1)，material-plan finding走Fn→pre-activation Z，二者互斥。这个 aggregate 没有 Phase 1–7 execution-authority/result object 序列，不能套用 6.4.1 的 ordinary-ticket substantive lifecycle；其写权限只来自 6.4 的四个 Phase0-specific one-shot actions，pre-activation Z另复用epoch-terminal action special variant。

稳定 ID 命名空间：

- Phase 0：`phase0_governance_aggregate_v1`；另有 `phase0_requirement_transition_registry_v1` 的 `P0-01` 至 `P0-10` entries，以及非 ticket 的 `phase0_failed_attempt_recovery_v1` action-instance family，key=`H{n}`，固定映射 `Fn`、`Sn`、`H(n+1)` identities/paths/allowlist/consumption；
- Program governance：conditional `PROGRAM-EPOCH-ROLLOVER`与registry-static applicable但candidate gate=`terminal-ready`的`PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01`；二者均为`program_governance_control+not_in_frontier+not_in_phase_exit`，不进入任何phase denominator，root/action subset不可互换；
- Phase 1：`P1-01` 至 `P1-15`；
- Phase 2 families：`RSH-01` 至 `RSH-15`，对应 9.3 的十五个技术面；
- Phase 3 qualification：`QL-01` 至 `QL-13` 是capability family IDs，不是把全部组件压进一张stable ticket。Phase2冻结有限`qualification_candidate_registry_v1`；每个组件/managed profile有collision-free canonical `qualification_candidate_key`（lowercase ASCII `[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{0,63}`，vendor长名/版本另存metadata）、stable ticket=`QL-{nn}-{candidate_key}`、attempt=`E{e}-QL-{nn}-{candidate_key}-C{a}`，C(a)只表示该candidate lineage的retry。candidate ticket固定`phase_source+terminal_evidence_for_aggregate`，并由`action_cleanup_target_capability_registry_v1`从exact action allowlist强制派生`target_producer|non_target_source`；每family另注册always/no-action `QL-{nn}-DECISION-01`=`phase_source+non_target_source+must_pass`，其candidate commit先形成member-lineage cutoff，再在完整lineages后冻结roles/disposition。条件式 index retirement另注册`IDX-RETIRE-PREP-01`与`IDX-RETIRE-01`；always/no-action aggregate为`QL-CLEANUP-INDEX-01`，result schema=`cleanup_obligation_expansion_v1`；fallback grammar与obligation identity保持下述规则；
- Phase 4：`ADR-01` 至 `ADR-15`（exact count=15）；`ADR-15`绑定前14票并拥有 `migration_slice_registry_v1`；
- Phase 5：`MIG-{capability}-{nn}`，每个 slice 单变量、单 feature switch、单 rollback；always/no-action aggregate为`MIG-CLEANUP-INDEX-01`，result schema=`cleanup_obligation_expansion_v1`；fallback family grammar为`MIG-CLEANUP-{source_slice_id}`，真实 instance=`{family}-CO-{full_cleanup_obligation_sha256}`、attempt=`{instance}-Cnn`；
- Phase 6：`ACC-S1`、`ACC-S2`、`ACC-S3`、`ACC-S4-CANDIDATE`（attempt=`ACC-S4-CANDIDATE-Cnn`）、`ACC-S4`、`ACC-S5`，另有 `DELL-03B-S1-R14-REPLACEMENT-CLOSURE`；current-product route 另注册条件式 `CUTOVER-S1` 至 `CUTOVER-S5`，其 applicability required-if 绑定对应 ACC terminal＋activation、exact consumer scope 和 `current_consumer_cutover_requested`；
- Phase 7：`CLS-01` feasibility、conditional `CLS-02` dependency cleanup、conditional `CLS-03` legacy retirement、`CLS-04` operations/rollback、`CLS-05` Project OS、`CLS-06` final independent review/Owner recommendation、normal-mode conditional `CLS-07-OWNER-RELEASE-DECISION`，以及conditional ordinary ticket `RELEASE-EXECUTION-01`。partial mode对02/03/07/RELEASE只能产生exact mode-bound not-applicable receipts。

H0冻结有限`pre_release_completion_gate_registry_v1`，禁止validator把“§14.2全部条件”或其他自然语言章节当输入。registry stable IDs恰为：`PRG-01-R14-STRATEGIC-CLOSE`、`PRG-02-DELL-REPLACEMENT-RC-OWNER`、`PRG-03-SUPERSESSION-ADR`、`PRG-04-CAPABILITY-DISPOSITIONS`、`PRG-05-RESEARCH-SATURATION`、`PRG-06-COMPONENT-QUALIFICATION-LOCK-SBOM-LICENSE`、`PRG-07-Z-LAB-REPRODUCIBILITY`、`PRG-08-TARGET-ARCHITECTURE-INTEGRATION`、`PRG-09-DOMAIN-KERNEL-ISOLATION`、`PRG-10-LEGACY-COMPAT-REGRESSION-ROLLBACK`、`PRG-11-REAL-CASE-MODEL-VALIDATION`、`PRG-12-CRITICAL-FINANCIAL-ERROR-DISPOSITION`、`PRG-13-STAGE-HUMAN-REPORT-PRODUCT-OPS-SECURITY-VERDICTS`、`PRG-14-NO-HIDDEN-FAILURE-VALIDATOR-INTEGRITY`、`PRG-15-GIT-PRE-RELEASE-CLEAN-RECOVERABLE`。每项固定exact receipt/reducer schema、required status、producer ticket/path、known-boundary和digest算法；漏项、额外项或自由文本替代都invalid。registry显式排除`phase7_pre_release_decision_ready_v1`自身/output digest、CLS-06 future result、CLS-07、RELEASE、`PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01`、program ready/terminal、Phase7 effective/final status与future Owner/close output。

CLS-06使用mode-discriminated `phase7_final_review_denominator_v1(mode=normal|partial)`，attempt identity、candidate/result/review/terminal都逐字节绑定mode与denominator digest，normal/partial不得互换、重放或在同一epoch双开。`mode=normal`的稳定alias为`phase7_pre_release_review_denominator_v1`，只由normal-mode CLS-01..05 applicability/terminal/required activation/cleanup refs和上述15项已实现PASS gate refs/digests构成。`mode=partial`的稳定alias为`phase7_partial_review_denominator_v1`，恰好绑定`PHASE-6-PARTIAL-CLOSEOUT-01` terminal/activation、Phase6 truthful failed/stopped/held source universe、CLS-01/04/05 partial-mode refs、CLS-02/03/07/RELEASE四张partial-mode selected exact NA、15项PRG逐项actual PASS/FAIL/NA状态及known boundaries（不要求也不得伪造全PASS）、全部cutover/rollback/current-route revalidation、quiescence/grants/in-flight/unknown/irreversible-side-effect集合、remaining root causes和operations/recovery scope。两种denominator都显式排除CLS-06自身future candidate/result/review/terminal及ready/final outputs；CLS-06 terminal PASS只证明对应mode review packet完整和review结论被忠实物化，不把partial underlying failure改成PASS。

`phase7_pre_release_decision_ready_v1`是不写receipt的pure reducer，只消费normal denominator、normal-mode CLS-06 immutable result/result-review/terminal及same-snapshot quiescence/grants/scope。它只在Phase1–6 effective PASS、Phase7 normal entry valid、CLS-01..05 applicability闭合且applicable票PASS/required activation与cleanup满足、excluded conditional票有normal-mode selected exact not-applicable、15项gate全满足、normal CLS-06 result-review/terminal PASS、pending/in_flight/unknown/writer/job/unresolved-delete=[]、全部此前action grants consumed/expired/revoked、active/nonterminal rollover=null、Q=null、mandatory/stop latch=false、pending governance closure=[]、exact product/version/deployment/consumer/publication/rollback scope frozen时返回true并输出canonical denominator/gate/CLS-06/scope digest。任何成员、mode、applicability、terminal、grant、scope或snapshot drift都返回false/invalid；注入、删除或翻转reducer自身output不得改变其input digest。

CLS-07 candidate/packet review/Owner payload/result/result-review/terminal必须逐字节绑定上述gate digest；gate=false时Owner若要早停，只能走program-stop/Q/Z，不能用no_release/hold绕过quiescence。`RELEASE-EXECUTION-01`不是CLS-07附属receipt，而是独立stable ticket。`publication_execution_requested`只能由Owner sovereign payload显式给出：release时必须显式true/false，no_release/hold时必须false。CLS-07全链绑定该field、Owner identity、scope与payload digest；其他actor不得新设或翻转。`RELEASE-EXECUTION-01.required_if_expression`仅在Phase7 normal、same-epoch pre-release gate仍为true且digest未漂移、CLS-07 release result-review/terminal passed、request=true时为true；release＋false只能selected exact not-applicable，no_release/hold不得创建candidate。applicable时完整走applicability→candidate→review→execution-authority→`product_publication_release` one-shot→result/post-action→result review→terminal；candidate和action-start都重新绑定/验证gate、Owner payload与exact scope，issuer与operator分离。publication在`PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01`前发生时program terminal理应仍null；真正invalid的是缺RELEASE authority/one-shot、gate false/drift后发布，或任一CLS-07/RELEASE/program reducer冒充execution authority，必须事前阻断或事后真实failure/rollback处置。

另外注册always/no-substantive-action的`PHASE-1-CLOSE-01`至`PHASE-6-CLOSE-01`，以及conditional `PHASE-6-PARTIAL-CLOSEOUT-01`；明确不存在且禁止临时创造`PHASE-7-CLOSE-*`，Phase7只由program/Phase7 joint reducers收口。这些已注册close条目固定`registry_entry_kind=phase_close_control`。`phase_close_control`永远排除于`phase_source_ticket_universe_digest`、required/optional/excluded source sets、source/cleanup frontier与其自身冻结的denominator，不能引用自己的future result/review/terminal/activation。Phase1–6每张normal close只在`source_exit_ready=true`后，绑定exact source-universe digest、denominator和全部result/review/terminal/cleanup refs，走candidate→review→no-action result→result review→terminal PASS→activation。Phase1–6 effective phase PASS当且仅当source_exit_ready=true且normal close result-review/terminal PASS；activation是另一步，仅设置下一phase candidate eligibility，不等于本phase PASS、不授action。

`effective_current_cutover_active_v1`是pure reducer：某CUTOVER成功后，只在exact preauthorized rollback已消费、post-action result、独立result-review/terminal、current consumer/route revalidation全部PASS，candidate/new route current consumers=0、route恢复到frozen last-active-qualified route、cutover/rollback grants consumed/expired/revoked且无in-flight/unknown/writer/job时才返回false；未cutover也返回false。成功cutover且没有这组完整rollback closure时返回true/invalid。历史CUTOVER receipts永远保留，不能为通过partial删除。

Phase6 partial close同为`phase_close_control`且不进入自己的source set：仅在Phase6 exit universe中每张applicable source票都有truthful terminal disposition、每张excluded conditional票有selector-selected exact not-applicable receipt、无pending applicability/in-flight/unknown/writer、normal source_exit_ready=false、independent review确认无法normal PASS且`partial_closeout_requested=true`时适用；不得要求not_applicable票伪造terminal。其terminal PASS只证明partial packet完整，绝不令Phase6 passed；activation仅令Phase7=`partial_closeout_only`并保持release/retire/cutover=false。Phase7 registry固定双模式denominator：normal沿用全部Phase7 source票；partial-only required恰为`CLS-01/CLS-04/CLS-05/CLS-06(mode=partial)`，excluded恰为`CLS-02/CLS-03/CLS-07-OWNER-RELEASE-DECISION/RELEASE-EXECUTION-01`。因此CLS-02/03/07/RELEASE均在registry中具有mode-bound conditional applicability；每张excluded票都必须由same-epoch、partial-mode-bound、selector-selected exact not-applicable receipt排除，且不得存在candidate/review/authority/result/result-review/terminal/activation；terminal disposition永不替代not-applicable。partial CLS-06必须绑定上一段exact partial denominator，不能消费normal pre-release denominator或要求15项全PASS。partial要求`effective_current_cutover_active_v1=false`并绑定全部历史cutover/rollback/revalidation refs；已完整回滚的历史CUTOVER object合法保留，未回滚/证据不完整/current consumer仍指向新route则invalid。任一不可恢复legacy deletion、publication/release side effect仍使partial fail closed。normal close与partial close activation互斥；close进自身denominator、source ready却无normal close仍报passed、close PASS但source未ready、partial将Phase6标passed、为partial删除历史cutover receipt、normal/partial CLS-06 digest swap或双terminal均fail closed。

`phase_entry_gate_registry_v1` 逐phase冻结唯一setter、exact predecessor refs、fixed activation receipt/path、candidate-only scope与substantive-action deny：

- Phase 1：same-epoch valid G(e)；只可提出P1 candidates；
- Phase 2：same-epoch valid G(e)也可提出RSH candidates，以保留Phase1/2受控并行；但每张RSH execution authority必须绑定其registry映射的relevant P1 capability-constraint terminal及required activation，`PHASE-2-CLOSE-01`还必须绑定P1-15与`PHASE-1-CLOSE-01` terminal/activation和全部relevant P1 constraints；
- Phase 3：`PHASE-2-CLOSE-01` terminal/activation，且该close result已绑定P1 close、全部required RSH terminals/activations、frozen benchmark/gold/security fixture refs；
- Phase 4：`PHASE-3-CLOSE-01` terminal/activation，result绑定Phase1/2/3 exact exit reducers与reviews；
- Phase 5：`PHASE-4-CLOSE-01` terminal/activation，result绑定fresh-reviewed exact 15-member `ADR-01..15` set、ticket-universe digest与`migration_slice_registry_v1`；
- Phase 6：`PHASE-5-CLOSE-01` terminal/activation，result绑定required MIG slices、rollback/stop与selected MIG CLEANUP-INDEX closure；
- Phase 7 normal：`PHASE-6-CLOSE-01` terminal/activation，result绑定全部ACC/product/root-cause gates；Phase 7 partial-only：上面的`PHASE-6-PARTIAL-CLOSEOUT-01` activation，二者互斥。

缺一个predecessor、跳phase、用derived boolean/text代替receipt、Phase2执行未绑定relevant P1 constraint、normal/partial双activation或cross-epoch entry replay都使`phase_entry_eligible=false`。每个phase ordinary candidate仍须其exact phase entry receipt；execution authority还须本票全部依赖，entry eligibility从不授action。Phase 7 partial-only只能输出feasibility/operations/root-cause/rollback/Project-OS closeout，不得执行legacy retirement、current cutover、publication或release。

conditional universe 必须有限且有 digest。Phase 3 entry绑定Phase2 frozen `qualification_candidate_registry_v1`：13个family、每family完整candidate keys/required-or-conditional、exact action allowlist→frontier role、phase-exit semantics/member retry policy、13张DECISION/cutoff tickets、两个IDX tickets、`QL-CLEANUP-INDEX-01`与QL cleanup family grammar；漏shortlist candidate、role/action不兼容或Phase3临时新增key必须回Phase2/epoch治理，不能动态扩票。Phase 5开始前从fresh-reviewed migration registry冻结MIG source IDs、action allowlists/derived frontier roles、`MIG-CLEANUP-INDEX-01`与family grammar；Phase6 CUTOVER固定五个。A-attempt只评估已注册stable target。cleanup obligation实例不在Phase开始时假展开，而在producer frontier关闭后，由唯一selected expansion逐`source attempt × exact target`派生；family pattern不是ticket，未在selected expansion中的实例不存在。

`QL-CLEANUP-INDEX-01`/`MIG-CLEANUP-INDEX-01` 是 always、required、无 substantive action 的 aggregation tickets，完整走 candidate→作者分离 review→no-action frozen result→作者分离 result review→terminal PASS→downstream activation；activation只允许 expansion内 obligations开始 applicability evaluation，不授cleanup。它们固定`registry_entry_kind=cleanup_expansion_control`、`cleanup_frontier_role=not_in_frontier`，绝不能进入自己的producer frontier。frozen result只写入6.4.1 compact namespace的`r.json`，`cleanup_obligation_expansion_v1`作为external payload原始字节嵌入该唯一envelope，不得另写result副本。candidate只能绑定由`cleanup_source_attempt_frontier_v1`计算的source-registry/frontier digest与target enumeration算法，不能自选成员；result payload至少绑定 program/contract/epoch/phase、expansion ticket/attempt/parent、producer registry ref/digest、`source_attempt_creation_closed=true`、open attempts/unconsumed retry grants=0、全部selected producer attempts terminal/result/`cleanup_target_manifest_v1` refs（无target也须empty digest）、合法not-applicable producer exclusions、frontier set digest/counts、逐target obligation entries、canonical order与obligation-set digest。

每个source result必须完整列出其所有cleanup-capable targets，不能只列`cleanup_needed=true`。target identity=`SHA256(RFC8785(target_kind,producer_attempt_id,registered_containment_root_id,normalized_relative_path_or_namespace,creation_manifest_raw_sha256,target_entry_id))`；其中`creation_manifest_raw_sha256`只能引用target创建前已存在的producer candidate/MIG manifest raw digest，绝不能引用正在包含这些target identities的`cleanup_target_manifest_v1`。所有target identities算完后，target manifest才可物化并取得raw digest；该digest只进入后续`cleanup_obligation_id=SHA256(RFC8785(program_id,authority_epoch_id,phase_id,cleanup_family_id,source_stable_id,source_attempt_id,source_terminal/result raw digests,target_manifest raw digest,target_entry_id,target identity))`，不回流target identity。required-if真假/authority expiry不进identity。每个target entry恰对应一个full-64-hex obligation和一个ticket instance；zero/multiple mapping、creation/target-manifest digest互换、自指、truncated-only identity、path alias/collision均拒绝。所有lifecycle envelopes只走6.4.1 compact target-attempt namespace；descriptive`.../cleanup_obligations/...`路径只可保存由result引用的target/post-action domain artifacts。一票只处理一个target，禁止family-level/batch grant。

`cleanup_source_attempt_frontier_v1`只取exact registry中`registry_entry_kind=phase_source AND cleanup_frontier_role=target_producer`的stable candidate lineages。对每个potential producer必须恰有一种闭合：其selected exact applicability receipt为not_applicable且无attempt，或source-attempt creation已closed、所有已创建attempt terminal/result并携完整或空target manifest、所有可建新attempt的grants consumed/expired/revoked。Phase3的creation-closed由对应family valid DECISION candidate cutoff＋完整member refs纯派生，并最终要求13张DECISION terminal/activation PASS；Phase5由fresh-reviewed migration registry的slice-closure reducer提供，不能靠“暂无retry”文字。`cleanup_expansion_control`、decision aggregate、derived obligation、phase close、program governance和合法`non_target_source`不进入frontier；但role/action incompatibility或non-target actual target不是排除理由，而是mandatory failure。frontier membership/digest由H0/Phase2/Phase4 registry reducer生成，candidate漏一producer、把aggregate/obligation塞回、按ID猜成员或要求no-action票伪target manifest均fail closed。

selected expansion必须same epoch/phase唯一latest、Cnn连续single-parent、candidate/result reviews与terminal/activation均PASS，且source-attempt-set digest等于validator从当前canonical producer frontier重算值。gap/duplicate/multiple latest/parent/path drift、新attempt/target、manifest digest漂移或漏项使selector empty并阻止cleanup applicability及phase PASS。cleanup obligation lifecycle只使用compact `target_attempt_path_key`；descriptive cleanup paths只承载被result引用的domain target/post-action artifacts，不存lifecycle envelope。

任一 CLEANUP-INDEX terminal PASS后发现frontier drift、target omission、manifest completeness failure或新source/target——无论所属Phase是否已PASS——都不得在same epoch生成INDEX C(n+1)、修改旧source result/manifest或让INDEX自行制造frontier。validator必须立即把exact INDEX terminal、旧frontier/manifest digests、current canonical recomputation与drift details作为program-governance evaluator payload，按entry gate物化A envelope；在该A尚未valid commit前相关cleanup/phase action一律fail closed，A一旦证明completeness trigger即由pure reducer锁存mandatory rollover，使旧cleanup eligibility和unstarted grants effective revoked。rollover quiescence必须证明所有旧expansion grants consumed/expired/revoked、active/unknown cleanup actions=0并保留旧INDEX/source/results/reviews/activation。新epoch H0把受影响Phase3/5标为invalidated/needs_revalidation，重新建立source frontier与INDEX；ordinary PASS retry、旧activation授权新target、只换digest无producer或same-epoch refresh全部fail closed。

`phase0_requirement_transition_registry_v1` 每项必须有 `entry_kind`，只允许 `precondition_requirement / candidate_requirement / review_transition / terminal_transition / activation_transition`。P0-09 在每个 Hn attempt 上是 Tn/Fn terminal transition，P0-10 的 predecessor 是当前 accepted lineage 的 Tn aggregate terminal PASS。`phase0_failed_attempt_recovery_v1` 是 action-instance family，不是 required-PASS phase ticket；旧 Fn/Sn attempts 保留，但不阻止后来 H(n+1)→T(n+1) 成为 accepted lineage。material-plan Fn改走pre-activation Z且不得对same finding再有sibling Sn；historical Sn后distinct external event可由current head合法写Z。namespace test 必须接受 aggregate、P0-10、合法 recovery instance、pre-activation terminal variant和historical Sn→distinct-event Z，拒绝 P0 entry 被当普通 ticket、P0-11、裸 `P0-Hn-FAIL-SUCCESSOR`、same-Fn/same-finding Sn/Z双分支、非法/跳号 attempt 与未注册对象。

Phase0 transition只由epoch-scoped Hn/Tn/Fn/G(e)与P0 entries计算。`phase_source_ticket_universe_v1`与cleanup frontier分轴：每phase base universe由全部`registry_entry_kind=phase_source`和适用的`cleanup_expansion_control`组成，排除phase-close/program-governance；Phase3/5 exit universe再以`base_universe_digest + selected cleanup expansion digest + 全部cleanup_obligation_instance O`确定性扩展，不能由candidate删减。只对该exit universe计算effective applicability、required/optional/excluded、`phase_exit_semantics`、terminal/result-review/cleanup；close、rollover与next-phase entry不进入。对Phase1–6，`source_exit_ready=true`仅当无pending/drift，全部applicable `phase_exit_semantics=must_pass`票terminal/result-review PASS，全部applicable `terminal_evidence_for_aggregate`票都有truthful result-review/terminal disposition且被registry点名的must-pass aggregate以完整member denominator绑定，excluded conditional票有selected exact not-applicable，source-required activation/cleanup满足且universe digest未漂移；被aggregate真实记录为failed/stopped/held的component candidate不能伪造PASS，也不能脱离aggregate单独令phase ready。它本身不等于phase passed，`effective_phase_status=passed IFF source_exit_ready=true AND registered normal_close_result_review=PASS AND registered normal_close_terminal=passed`。normal close activation只设置下一phase entry，partial close不产生Phase6 passed。

Phase7不使用normal-close公式。normal mode从其exact source universe计算`phase7_normal_source_exit_ready`；partial-only mode从上文exact four-required/four-excluded集合计算`phase7_partial_source_exit_ready`：每张excluded票必须恰有一条same-epoch、partial-mode-bound、selector-selected exact not-applicable A-chain/head；该合法A receipt和selector projection是required exclusion evidence，不是禁止对象。漏/双/旧/stale/cross-mode A，或除该A/selector外出现candidate/review/authority/result/result-review/terminal/activation，才使partial invalid。二者与`program_terminal_ready_reducer_v1`、`program_terminal_and_incident_seal_reducer_v1` pair projection、`phase7_effective_status_reducer_v1`在同一canonical snapshot联合计算，禁止不存在的Phase7 close receipt。可选票开始后仍须诚实terminal；authority只授本票动作，自由文本无效。

Phase 3/5 还必须满足cleanup obligation layer：selected expansion存在且aggregate terminal=passed/activation=activated；令O为其全部obligations，`required_cleanup={o|effective_applicability=applicable}`、`excluded_cleanup={o|not_applicable且selected exact receipt证明required_if=false}`、`pending_cleanup=O-required-excluded`。必须`pending_cleanup=∅`、required+excluded count=obligation_count、每个required obligation的exact-target result-review/terminal/post-action proof PASS、每个excluded有valid false receipt、selected frontier digest与当前一致且expansion外不存在cleanup-capable target。family名不进denominator；同一source两个attempt、或一个attempt两个targets必须分别产生两个obligations，只完成一个不得phase PASS。Phase5仍不得在Phase4 registry fresh review及slice authority/rollback/stop contract前开始。

每个 expansion-listed QL/MIG cleanup obligation固定`required_for_phase_exit_when_applicable=true`。required-if只有 source terminal/result valid、`cleanup_needed=true`、exact target unconsumed、原exact-target cleanup authority absent/expired/revoked/target-mismatch，且target不属于current/shared/admitted/other-slice并有containment/recoverability proof时为true。false合法排除只限：source result可重算`cleanup_needed=false`，或原authority已consumed且accepted post-action证明exact target安全清除；原authority仍valid/unconsumed、identity/containment/reference不确定、source未terminal、selected expansion缺失/漂移时必须pending，不能not_applicable。cleanup source可passed/failed/stopped/held，但cleanup不是普通retry且不能取得原implementation action。跨source/obligation target、裸family名、family-level candidate、普通retry冒充cleanup、cleanup authority授install/write/cutover或protected target全部fail closed。

## 7. Phase 0：R14 终止与新架构治理重基线

### 7.1 目标

把 Owner 决定变成仓库可恢复、机器可检查的唯一当前权威；明确 R14、旧计划、新程序和所有下游权限。

### 7.2 非目标

- 不修改任何 R14 implementation/test/frozen corpus/failure receipt；
- 不安装候选；
- 不调用产品、研究、package、模型、Provider、外源、embedding或reranker网络；唯一例外是seed-bound、exact endpoint/ref、expected-old lease的`git_control_transport_v1`，只发布/readback本程序plan/governance/lifecycle/start-claim receipts，不能泛化为Phase2 network authority；
- 不改 Evidence、S2、S3、报告或产品行为；
- 不删除 D:\FIN_Insight_Agent\data\indexes。
