失败时只修 Phase 0，不进入 Phase 1。

## 8. Phase 1：全产品能力与旧代码迁移审计

### 8.1 目标

从真实 import、consumer、data、artifact 和运行入口出发，建立每个能力的 retain/wrap/replace/regression/retire 决策；识别真正的 FIN domain kernel 和通用轮子。

### 8.2 固定输入

- Phase 0 exact authority；
- current active baseline manifest；
- current code map 和 strict mainline gates；
- pyproject/requirements/lockfiles；
- src、apps、scripts、configs、tests、data contracts；
- Runtime registry、Workbench composition root；
- `dell_03b_s1_internal_chain` R14、其他明确命名空间的历史 R-attempt、Project OS frozen evidence；
- current local data/artifact roots，仅做路径、schema、consumer 和容量审计，不读取 hidden outcome。

### 8.3 执行顺序

Phase 1不是并行填写十五张表。G(e)之后仍planned、只有candidate eligibility；P1-01至P1-15每张action-bearing ticket先candidate/review，再以独立authority授权该票metadata read与bounded write。没有本票receipt不得借另一票或G(e)。Phase2虽可同时提出RSH candidate，但其execution authority必须等待并绑定对应P1 constraint，不能用“并行”绕过Phase1事实输入。

在逐票授权内，先执行 P1-01、P1-02、P1-04、P1-10，冻结活动入口、package cycle、runtime resource compatibility spine 与历史 R-attempt reachability；这四项未通过前，禁止创建目标目录、移动模块或把某个旧模块宣布为 retired。随后执行 P1-03、P1-05 至 P1-13，最后由 P1-14 汇总迁移矩阵、P1-15 冻结 ports 和验收合同。每张票的 bounded audit 完成后都按 frozen result → read-only result review → terminal receipt 前进；只有确有下游消费者的票才另建 downstream-activation receipt。

### 8.4 需求票

| Ticket | 工作内容 | 主要输出 |
|---|---|---|
| P1-01 baseline and active consumers | Git/baseline、backend/frontend composition roots、CLI/API/worker、runtime entrypoints；先以exact G(e)为input同步Project OS/navigation为“derived Phase0 passed、Phase1/2 candidate eligible、substantive grants仍false” | active_consumer_graph_v1＋post_g_navigation_sync_v1 |
| P1-02 package graph and cycle plan | Python/TypeScript imports、dynamic imports、service composition、domain inversion | package_dependency_and_cycle_break_plan_v1 |
| P1-03 domain-kernel extraction | identity/source/Evidence/Numeric/PIT/bridge/Gap/WWC/release | fin_domain_kernel_map_v1 |
| P1-04 runtime-resource compatibility spine | runtime registry、exact digest、resource path、replay/app consumers | runtime_resource_compatibility_map_v1 |
| P1-05 artifact/data/index map | raw/source/object/index/SQL/model/trace/eval/report/private/public | artifact_lineage_and_rebuild_map_v1 |
| P1-06 S1 capability map | capture/document/XBRL/index/retrieval/ranking/semantics/Evidence | s1_capability_and_authority_map_v1 |
| P1-07 S2 capability map | financial fact/normalization/bridge/derivation/forecast inputs | s2_capability_and_authority_map_v1 |
| P1-08 S3 capability map | causal/counter/WWC/gap/research method/model nodes | s3_capability_and_authority_map_v1 |
| P1-09 S4/S5 and product map | writer/citation/render/review/release/workbench/operations | s4_s5_product_capability_map_v1 |
| P1-10 legacy R-chain reachability | 按 capability/责任命名空间识别的 R-attempt files、tests、fixtures、current consumers、historical-only paths；禁止把 S3 Writer/VS2/DELL 03B 等轨道混成一个全局序列 | r_chain_legacy_disposition_v1 |
| P1-11 config and runner taxonomy | 1,000+ configs、formal/one-off/current/result/policy、live/zero-call runners | config_and_runner_taxonomy_v1 |
| P1-12 test/eval spine | declared markers、逐测试分类、gold/blind/replay/integration/full-chain、default pytest safety | test_and_eval_spine_v1 |
| P1-13 dependency/deploy/SBOM | direct/transitive deps、duplicate capability、platform/privacy/license constraints | dependency_deployment_and_license_baseline_v1 |
| P1-14 unified migration matrix | retain/wrap/replace/regression/retire、owner、consumer、risk、wave、rollback | capability_migration_matrix_v1 |
| P1-15 ports and acceptance contracts | canonical schema、IDs、failure codes、receipts、API/UI、fixtures、shadow parity | target_ports_and_qualification_input_v1 |

### 8.5 每个模块必须回答

- 谁 import/调用/展示它；
- 输入、输出、schema、side effect 和 data root；
- 是否持有 FIN 权威；
- 是否可由成熟组件替代；
- replacement port 是什么；
- 哪些 tests 是 truth、regression、implementation-coupled 或已污染；
- shadow/dual-read 怎么做；
- rollback 怎么做；
- retire 前最后一个 consumer 是谁；
- 删除后如何恢复；
- 许可证、隐私、部署和资源限制；
- 是否有服务端 idempotency/fence，其作用域、保留窗口和冲突语义是什么；
- 是否能 query/status、same-operation resume、cancel-before-effect；
- initial primary route choice 的durable backend/locator/readback在哪里；每次非query physical call的one-shot operation-intent backend、generation、typed generation-0 predecessor或successor exact observation与readback在哪里；safe-if-no-call/post-dispatch recovery的independent safety-choice backend/locator与sentinel discriminant在哪里；cancel是否明确按effecting operation处理且response lost/timeout保持unknown；
- unknown/partial effect如何恢复，何时必须终止旧票并新建 recovery/cleanup ticket。

这四项只是 inventory 事实，不授权任何调用；vendor 宣称“exactly once”必须拆到协议、窗口、scope 和 crash fixture验证，不能原样写成FIN结论。

### 8.6 Phase 1 输出路径

- docs/architecture/repository/FIN_0_1_3_PRODUCT_CAPABILITY_AND_LEGACY_MIGRATION_INVENTORY_20260830.zh-CN.md；
- configs/repository/fin_0_1_3_product_capability_migration_matrix_v1_0.json；
- configs/repository/fin_0_1_3_active_consumer_and_artifact_rebuild_map_v1_0.json；
- configs/repository/fin_0_1_3_runtime_resource_and_r_chain_compatibility_map_v1_0.json；
- configs/repository/fin_0_1_3_test_eval_and_runner_taxonomy_v1_0.json；
- 对现有 code map 的增量 supersession note；
- Phase 1 worklog 和 fresh read-only review receipt。

### 8.7 退出门与停止条件

通过要求：

- S1–S5 每个产品能力都有 owner 和 decision；
- 所有活动 import/consumer 都有归属；
- package cycle、domain inversion 与 runtime exact-digest 绑定均有可回滚拆解顺序；
- 所有大 artifact 都有 producer、input、rebuild、consumer、retention；
- 每条 R-attempt 都绑定 capability/责任命名空间，不再被误标为多个活动产品版本，也不因 DELL 03B R14 disposition 删除其他轨道历史；
- 所有 test 都按已声明 marker vocabulary 分类，默认测试路径不会意外触发 local-data、network 或 paid-model；
- domain kernel 不依赖具体 vendor schema；
- migration matrix 可以逐模块执行和回滚。
- P1-01至P1-15每张action-bearing票均完整terminal；`PHASE-1-CLOSE-01`冻结exact denominator/result review并terminal PASS/activation。G(e)没有直接授任何票动作；该activation不直接开放Phase3，而供Phase2 close绑定。

停止：

- 无法证明某模块是否被活动消费者使用；
- hidden/holdout 边界不清；
- data artifact 没有可重建输入；
- 计划要求先删除再理解；
- 为迁移方便需要放宽 Evidence/Numeric/release 权威。

## 9. Phase 2：成熟技术栈广泛调研与 shortlist 冻结

### 9.1 目标

在每个 ADOPT_MATURE 能力面建立足够广、足够新、又有现实采用证据的 longlist；形成可测试 shortlist，而不是直接把知名度当答案。

### 9.2 调研工作流

每个 capability family 必须执行：

1. 定义问题、FIN-specific constraints 和非目标；
2. 发现 longlist；
3. 只用官方 docs/repo/spec/paper 核实能力、版本、许可和限制；
4. 用 release cadence、issue/security posture、维护者、现实部署案例和生态兼容性判断成熟度；
5. 记录 native Windows、WSL2、Docker Linux、remote Linux、managed 画像；
6. 记录 data region、retention、training/use-of-data、pricing 和 exit；
7. 明确排除理由；
8. 选 default candidate、challenger、managed ceiling；
9. 冻结 exact version/tag/commit/model revision 候选；
10. 对每个会产生副作用的candidate核实其idempotency/fence作用域与保留窗口、server-side query/status/resume/cancel、crash readback、primary route-choice primitive、per-call operation-intent primitive、independent safety-choice primitive、SDK retry默认值、cancel的真实副作用/ambiguous outcome以及无法证明outcome时的query-only/exclude路径；
11. 由未参与调研结论编写者做 evidence review。

### 9.3 必须覆盖的技术面

1. regulator/issuer official APIs 与 XBRL；
2. HTTP/crawl/browser/WARC/source capture；
3. untrusted-content intake security；
4. PDF/OCR/layout/table/chunk/document intelligence；
5. canonical metadata、transaction、object/artifact、Parquet/analytics；
6. lexical/vector/hybrid search；
7. embedding 与 reranker；
8. source-grounded LLM semantic extraction、span alignment、abstain/human；
9. Agent state/checkpoint/HITL 与 durable jobs；
10. Provider SDK、structured output、gateway、MCP；
11. OTel/trace/eval/experiment/artifact lineage；
12. schema/preflight/policy/IAM/secrets；
13. human annotation/review/citation/report rendering；
14. GraphRAG 与 whole-RAG platform，只作触发式/benchmark 判断；
15. Workbench product UX 与 operator UX 的职责分离。

### 9.4 调研饱和标准

不能用“列了几个 famous 项目”收口。每个 family 至少满足：

- 已覆盖一条成熟本地/OSS 路线；
- 已覆盖一个不同架构的现实 challenger；
- 有商业价值时覆盖一个 managed ceiling；
- 对明显相关的近期/广泛采用候选有 include/exclude 记录；
- 连续两轮发现检索没有出现新的能力类别或会改变 shortlist 的候选；
- 所有排除项都对应许可、能力、维护、部署、隐私、成本、锁定或 FIN gate；
- 仍存在信息时效不确定时明确标记并进入 Phase 3 exact snapshot，不假装已固定。

若某 family 现实上不足三个可信候选，可以少于三个，但必须说明市场结构和为何继续搜索不会改变决策。

### 9.5 shortlist 不是 winner

当前 landscape packet 中的 SEC+Arelle、Scrapy+Playwright+warcio、Docling、MinerU、PostgreSQL+Parquet+DuckDB、pgvector/OpenSearch、BGE/Cohere、LangExtract+DeepSeek、LangGraph、official OpenAI SDK、Pydantic、OTel/OpenInference、MLflow、Quarto/Pandoc/CSL 只是 Phase 2 seed hypothesis。任何一项未经 Phase 3 exact qualification 都不能写成正式已采用组件。

### 9.6 输出与退出门

输出：

- product-wide landscape v2；
- finite `qualification_candidate_registry_v1`（family、canonical candidate key、required/conditional、profile、exact action allowlist、derived frontier role、phase-exit semantics、member retry policy与DECISION cutoff schema）；
- exact source/version/license snapshot；
- shortlist/exclusion matrix；
- qualification hypotheses、metrics 和 resource estimates；
- research saturation receipt。

退出要求：

- 每个 ADOPT 能力有至少一个可运行 shortlist；
- 每个 shortlist member都有唯一canonical candidate key与exact qualification profile；
- 没有许可/隐私/部署 blocker 被藏在“后续再看”；
- Phase 3 所需下载、磁盘、GPU、API、服务和 fixture 预算可计算；
- fresh reviewer 对事实引用与 recommendation 分离给 PASS。
- `PHASE-2-CLOSE-01`冻结全部RSH denominator与`qualification_candidate_registry_v1` complete-member digest，并绑定P1-15、PHASE-1-CLOSE activation、每张RSH对应P1 constraints及benchmark/gold/security fixtures；result review、terminal PASS与activation完成后才令Phase3 candidate eligible。

## 10. Phase 3：Z 盘 qualification lab 与组件实测

进入任何 candidate 的下载、安装、启动或运行前，必须先从Phase2 frozen registry选出exact `(QL family,qualification_candidate_key)`，再通过6.4.1创建该candidate lineage的单一`E{e}-QL-{nn}-{candidate_key}-C{a}` pre-run contract/manifest，接受review，并由独立execution-authority授权exact harness/evidence及下载/安装/运行。C(a)仅是同一candidate失败/停止/held后的retry；candidate A terminal PASS只禁止A自身same-epoch retry，不禁止registry中candidate B独立建C0。external request可含reviewed harness/config/test patch与digest，但candidate commit只物化本candidate pre-run manifest；执行后才按逐路径allowlist物化`scripts/qualification/`、`tests/qualification/`、`configs/qualification/`、`configs/audits/`及worklog payload，production `src/`、`pyproject.toml`、正式lock/current config/data显式deny。candidate/review/authority/result/review/terminal/activation互不覆盖，任何后件不能回写manifest。

### 10.1 实验室根与隔离

建议主根：

Z:\FIN_Insight_Agent_qualification_lab\20260830_product_wide_architecture_rebase_v1

建议结构：

~~~text
downloads/
sources/
envs/
models/
services/
images/
fixtures/
corpora/
indexes/
runs/
artifacts/
licenses/
sbom/
logs/
quarantine/
~~~

大文件、虚拟环境、模型、容器层、候选源码和运行产物不进 Git。仓库只保存：

- qualification manifest；
- lockfile/revision/digest；
- fixture manifest；
- commands；
- bounded metrics/result；
- license/SBOM conclusion；
- failure/stop receipt；
- winner/challenger decision。

### 10.2 pre-run candidate、frozen result 与 terminal receipt 分离

每个`E{e}-QL-{nn}-{candidate_key}-C{a}` pre-run manifest只能包含运行前已知或明确标为planned/estimated的字段，并先绑定完整`qualification_candidate_registry_v1` ref、family、candidate key与本lineage predecessor：

- candidate family/name/proposed role；
- exact intended version/tag/commit；官方已发布 package hash 时写 expected hash，否则为 null 并冻结 post-download hash verification rule；
- source URL、access time、source/license digest；
- Python/Java/Node/runtime/OS；
- native Windows/WSL2/Docker/remote/managed profile；
- proposed container image/model repo/revision/weights/tokenizer digest；
- CUDA/driver/hardware compatibility assumption；
- proposed config/schema/prompt/harness payload digest；
- fixture/corpus/query/gold digest；
- planned network domains、ports、egress；
- secrets and privacy class；
- estimated disk/RAM/VRAM；
- planned run-ID namespace 与 expected artifact paths，不得伪造 actual run ID/digest；
- planned start/stop/recovery/backup/export/uninstall；
- frozen adapter capability profile：idempotency/fence scope/window、query/status、same-operation resume、cancel-before-effect、initial primary-choice backend/locator/CAS/readback、per-call operation-intent backend/locator/CAS/readback、independent safety-choice backend/locator/CAS/readback、SDK retry=0；cancel为effecting且lost/timeout保持unknown；
- frozen `physical_operation_kind` allowlist、primary-choice key、operation-intent key/generation/predecessor、observation receipt、safety-choice key/generation/predecessor-discriminant/null-or-exact-observation/branch/member、exact prearmed ordinary/Q/IDX physical member set、terminal Git stale no-retry profile及result boundary；
- known/preliminary transitive SBOM/license inputs；
- hypothesis、baseline、ceiling、metrics、stop、rollback；
- `ticket_lifecycle_stage="candidate"`、`ticket_terminal_status=null`、actual resource/run/artifact/result fields absent 或 null；
- proposed 3.3 `capability_disposition` 与 component role 仍只是候选，不是 terminal/winner 事实。

运行后由独立 `ticket_result_receipt_materialization_write` 冻结本candidate result/failure，才允许记录actual resources/run/artifacts/digests/metrics/failure/cleanup/license/SBOM。每个action-bearing QL candidate result都必须携带`cleanup_target_manifest_v1`与actual action/resource inventory digest；target producer可为完整non-empty或canonical empty，non-target必须canonical empty并证明authority未允许、实际也未创建cleanup-capable target。它绑定已消费authority/actions，不能回写candidate、漏target或把sibling candidate target混入。non-target发现target时如实failed并触发mandatory rollover，不能只丢弃manifest。

最后只有result-review存在时才可产生candidate terminal。candidate terminal只陈述该组件/profile资格结果，不直接决定family role。每个`QL-{nn}-DECISION-01`是always/no-action aggregate，其candidate commit同时是预注册`family_member_lineage_cutoff_v1`。cutoff前先由pure `family_member_cutoff_eligibility_v1`对Phase2 frozen denominator中的每个member逐项只允许二选一：①`selected_not_applicable`：仅conditional，selector-selected exact verdict=`not_applicable`且candidate/attempt set恰为空；②`terminal_lineage_closed`：always或selected applicable，attempt count>=1、C0..Ca连续、unique latest terminal、全部attempt有result/result-review/terminal、active/start/unknown/in-flight=[]、全部initial-creation/retry grants consumed/expired/revoked，并且frozen retry-policy计算为`pass_no_retry|nonretryable|retry_exhausted|independently_reviewed_waiver`之一。`pending_evaluation`、selector empty/stale、applicable但zero attempt、always未执行、terminal缺失、retry仍required、NA却已有candidate均使DECISION candidate grant为空。

DECISION candidate derived grant绑定完整member eligibility preimages/digests、`active_attempts=[]`、started/unknown actions=[]、exact retry-policy digest和canonical parent/tree。member C0或retry若先提交会令旧decision grant/snapshot失效；valid decision candidate若先提交，则所有member initial-creation与C(a+1) retry grants立即false，不只是关闭retry。`candidate_lineage_creation_closed`只可由valid cutoff＋上述逐member eligibility＋无active attempt＋完整terminal/NA refs纯派生，不能首次由decision result自报。

cutoff后才可由完整member denominator生成decision review/result；result逐member绑定derived creation-closed、last terminal ref与no-active proof，并冻结唯一default、至多一个challenger/managed ceiling及KEEP/HOLD/EXCLUDE理由。decision review/result/terminal失败时member retry仍frozen，只允许新的DECISION attempt绑定前decision failure和同一/新审过aggregate payload；若review证明member执行证据本身不完整，必须走family-registry revision/mandatory authority-epoch治理后才能在新epoch重开member，不能静默解除cutoff。decision terminal PASS后的activation最终sealed disposition/frontier；漏member、绑定旧terminal、cutoff后sibling retry、把candidate PASS当winner、或用一个candidate cleanup authority处理sibling均fail closed。13张decision activation后producer frontier才可关闭；candidate tickets固定`phase_source+terminal_evidence_for_aggregate`，decision tickets固定`phase_source+non_target_source+must_pass`，前者的真实非PASS结果只有被后者完整吸收后才可满足Phase3 source-exit。

mutation tests必须拒绝candidate中terminal/actual字段、result早于authority、terminal早于review、后件改变candidate；并接受A PASS后B-C0、A FAIL后cutoff前A-C1，拒绝A PASS后A-C1；接受A真实FAIL→DECISION cutoff→A-C1拒绝、A EXCLUDE＋B PASS且完整DECISION PASS后Phase3继续；A-C1先于cutoff出现时旧decision grant必须失效。还要拒绝conditional applicable但无C0、always/required从未执行、retry policy仍required、selected NA却存在candidate、cutoff后initial C0或sibling retry、decision candidate漏member/绑定旧terminal、decision review FAIL后重开member、把A FAIL伪改PASS、漏A仍做DECISION、DECISION未PASS却source-exit、漏shortlist member仍做Phase3 close、跨candidate target/authority与candidate-key/path collision；并接受terminal lineage完整或exact NA后的合法cutoff，证明decision candidate到activation denominator逐字节不漂移。

### 10.3 先零调用，再真实调用

qualification candidate的adapter capability必须声明支持的query binding kinds、exact classification→normalized projection map、有限read cap、same-path observation revision规则及intentless query是否允许；未显式opt-in时`accepted_claim_pre_choice|accepted_choice_pre_intent`均false。首轮真实调用前先用zero-call fixtures证明query union不会生成choice/intent/effect/safety/result authority，且unknown compensation两格为deny。

顺序固定：

1. install/import/version/license smoke；
2. saved/synthetic fixture compatibility；
3. failure injection、timeout、malformed、security；至少覆盖claim accepted前后、claim后/primary choice前、primary choice accepted后/operation intent前、intent accepted后/adapter call前、effect后/observation receipt前、observation后/successor intent前、safe-if-no-call sentinel safety choice accepted后/safety operation intent前、post-dispatch safety choice accepted后/safety operation intent前、safety intent accepted后/recovery call前、continue/complete/cancel同predecessor intent竞争、cancel response lost、两个safety members竞争、effect后/result前及result后旧票恢复拒绝；
4. frozen local corpus benchmark；
5. 只有前四项通过，才进入允许的外部/managed/model shadow；
6. live run 后必须重新做 deterministic post-validation 和 author-separated review。

### 10.4 首轮资格切片

每个表项是capability family；表中每个实际组件/profile是registry内独立stable candidate ticket，family decision另成aggregate，不做多候选塞进一张manifest的大爆炸：

| Slice | default/challenger | 核心 gate |
|---|---|---|
| QL-01 contracts | Pydantic strict canonical source | old/new schema parity、unknown field、cross-field FIN invariant |
| QL-02 intake security | MIME/magic + malware + sandbox + egress | zip bomb、SSRF、active content、prompt/tool/path/query injection |
| QL-03 source/XBRL | SEC API + Arelle | accession/period/unit/dimension/amendment/raw lineage |
| QL-04 capture/WARC | Scrapy + Playwright + warcio | redirect/resume/rate/raw round-trip/as-of |
| QL-05 document | Docling vs MinerU + one managed ceiling if approved | page/bbox/cell/span/footnote/cross-page table |
| QL-06 storage | PostgreSQL + Parquet + DuckDB | transaction/PITR/snapshot/rebuild/locking |
| QL-07 embedding | BGE-M3 vs Qwen3-Embedding bounded local candidates + managed ceiling if approved | fixed qrels、identifier zero-miss、target-in-pool、Recall@k、critical issuer/period/unit/source-role slices、p95、RAM/VRAM、index bytes、rebuild |
| QL-08 retrieval engine | fixed qualified embedder；PostgreSQL+pgvector vs OpenSearch | 单一 embedding input 下的 PIT/Recall/MRR/nDCG/p95/rebuild/operations |
| QL-09 rerank | qualified candidate pool；BGE v2-m3 vs managed ceiling | target-in-pool first、material slice、latency/resource |
| QL-10 semantic | LangExtract pattern + DeepSeek shadow | exact span/schema/hard validator/abstain/human gold |
| QL-11 workflow/provider | LangGraph + official SDK | max_retries=0、checkpoint、HITL、duplicate-risk、crash recovery |
| QL-12 trace/eval | OTel/OpenInference + MLflow | passive import、privacy、export、authority independence |
| QL-13 rendering | Quarto/Pandoc/CSL | claim/citation precheck、PDF/DOCX/HTML visual parity |

每个QL-07 candidate独立冻结model/revision/weights/tokenizer、pooling、normalization、dimension、precision/quantization、max length/truncation、dense/sparse/multi-vector mode、batch/device。只有`QL-07-DECISION-01`基于完整candidate denominator冻结winner或有证据KEEP/HOLD，QL-08才能在固定embedder上比较engine；只有`QL-08-DECISION-01`确认target-in-pool/candidate pool，QL-09才比较reranker。沿用current embedding也必须作为registry candidate有独立KEEP/HOLD decision，不能隐含在retrieval结果里。

### 10.5 DeepSeek API 使用合同

Owner 已允许真实 case 中使用 DeepSeek API，但每个节点仍须在调用前创建 TokenBudgetBasis，至少包含：

- node purpose；
- input scale 和 source/privacy class；
- required outputs；
- schema burden；
- materiality/quality risk；
- comparable run evidence；
- model/deployment/profile/reasoning mode；
- max input/output/reasoning budget；
- stop/truncation behavior；
- expected cost/latency；
- retry policy，默认 max_retries=0；
- request hash、idempotency key、wire ordinal、start/terminal receipt；
- API key 仅来自环境/secret store，不进日志或 artifact。

Semantic shadow 只能产生 SemanticCandidate。模型不能写 Evidence、NumericFact、Gap closure、S2 bridge 或 release verdict。旧 R14 parser output 不作 truth；case-correct human-adjudicated gold 才是主判定。

### 10.6 D 盘 data\indexes 条件式清理授权

Owner 已明确授权：如果成熟技术栈测试确需 D 盘空间，可以严格移除 D:\FIN_Insight_Agent\data\indexes 下的文件，并接受旧 retrieval 暂停、之后按新架构重建。

该授权不是立即删除。两个 conditional ticket 都固定 `required_for_phase_exit_when_applicable=true`，并先按 6.4.1 A-attempt/selector 分开评估：`IDX-RETIRE-PREP-01.required_if` 只在下列 1–4 的 preliminary storage predicate 全为 true 时成立；`IDX-RETIRE-01.required_if` 只在 PREP same-epoch terminal PASS 且其 frozen result 明确 `delete_still_required=true` 时成立。泛称或旧 applicability receipt 不够，必须是当前 target attempt selector-selected exact receipt；它不授权 preflight 或 delete。两票分别 applicable 后，触发条件全部满足才可执行各自动作：

本节另外注册三个仅限IDX-RETIRE attempt的typed轴，不能泛化成产品route status：`index_retirement_interlock_status=not_acquired|claim_accepted_pending_acquire|acquired|recovery_required|released|failed`；`interlock_acquire_observation_state=not_attempted|atomic_not_acquired|atomic_acquired|unknown`；`old_index_consumer_admission_state=accepting|draining_locked_for_retirement|blocked_pending_recovery|closed_after_retirement`。claim remote accepted但backend尚未调用/确认时只能是`claim_accepted_pending_acquire + not_attempted + accepting`且delete=false。qualified backend明确atomic abort时唯一合法projection不是多值pending，而是正式14字段tuple：`index_retirement_interlock_status=failed`、`interlock_acquire_observation_state=atomic_not_acquired`、`old_index_consumer_admission_state=accepting`、`logical_invocation_count=1`、`physical_acquire_attempt_count=1`、`active_interlock_generation=null`、`active_fence_token=null`、`lock_readback_state=absent`、`drain_started=false`、`delete_claim_state=absent`、`delete_logical_invocation_count=0`、`delete_physical_call_count=0`、`release_required=false`、`same_claim_reacquire_allowed=false`。`terminal_lifecycle_outcome`、`interlock_acquire_outcome`、`consumer_admission_state`不得作为tuple别名；terminal failed不是abort前置，而只能在冻结abort observation/result并接受独立result review后由lifecycle reducer投影。同一attempt/claim不得再acquire；重试只能在旧attempt terminal后用新attempt重新走A/review/authority/CAS。atomic commit必须同一write set写lock owner/scope/generation/fence、admission=draining、heartbeat/no-auto-unlock并投影`acquired + atomic_acquired`。unknown、timeout或readback inconsistent绝不命中abort tuple，必须`recovery_required + blocked_pending_recovery`并保留recovery/release义务。`draining|blocked`不等于route suspended；任何TTL/handle release不得自动改回accepting。

1. Z 盘作为主 lab，已先尝试按 candidate 分批、释放失败候选和避免同时保留重复环境；
2. exact qualification storage budget 已计算：
   required = download + unpack + env/image + model + corpus copy + index + run artifacts + rollback/export + 20% headroom；
3. 保持 Z 盘安全余量后仍无法完成已批准 candidate；
4. 证明把工作转到 D 盘并清理 index subtree 后可以满足预算；
5. 已冻结closed-world old-index consumer-start registry，逐项列出reader/writer/job/service/scheduler/API/UI/CLI及其admission hook，并证明`index_retirement_execution_interlock`能在同一lock generation阻止全部已登记consumer的新start；漏consumer、无法接入hook或只能做point-in-time进程扫描时delete authority=false；
6. `IDX-RETIRE-PREP-01` 已有 same-epoch/target selector-selected exact applicability=applicable receipt，并完整经过 candidate→作者分离 review→独立 execution authority；其授权动作只允许只读枚举/digest、snapshot/export/restore drill 或 isolated scratch rebuild，以及向 exact evidence paths 写 preflight/recovery artifact，明确禁止删除和 current-route 变化；
7. `IDX-RETIRE-PREP-01` 已冻结 result、接受作者分离 result review 并 terminal PASS；其 result 包含已提交的 exact index retirement manifest、storage trigger、恢复证明和 all-target identity/containment evidence；
8. 已冻结 retrieval suspension 合同和 rebuild acceptance；此时只冻结删除后的预期 typed state，不能在删除前伪报 route 已 suspended；
9. 已向 Owner 在执行更新中报告将触发该授权；
10. `known_non_reproducible_items == []`，每个 producer/config/commit 与全部 retained input digest 都可读且验证成功；
11. 每个删除目标已满足下列恢复路线之一，并有 fresh review：
    - content-addressed old-index snapshot/export 已写入独立受控根，并完成一次 restore drill；或
    - 已从冻结输入在独立 scratch root 成功 rebuild，且通过旧 query/index contract、identifier zero-miss、row/object count、known regression 和 digest/semantic equivalence；
12. `IDX-RETIRE-01` 已有 same-epoch/target selector-selected exact applicability=applicable receipt；candidate冻结该A binding、PREP terminal/`delete_still_required=true` result、manifest/digests、恢复证明、Owner通知、空间、operator、expiry、`interlock→delete`两步primary actions、两张start claims、两套typed operation profiles、各自initial primary-choice与per-call operation-intent locators，以及`index_under_lock_recovery`下的independent safety-choice、normal release/crash handoff/restore members、under-lock outcome/release-review/final result paths和持续consumer/handle/identity/containment验证；candidate自身两primary grants均false；
13. `IDX-RETIRE-01` 接受 fresh、作者分离 candidate review 后，由与 author/reviewer/executor 均不同的 authority issuer 物化 exact execution-authority receipt；decision必须逐字节分别授权interlock与delete两个primary Action ID、same target/qualified durable lock adapter、normal release、preauthorized recovery actors/suboperations与frozen scope。未过期且未消费时也只令interlock start-claim grant=true，`old_index_delete.granted`仍false；terminal/result绝不反向生成任一grant；
14. interlock action的exact start claim先与R/I/Q竞争；R/I/Q先赢则claim/backend call=0。claim接受只commit一个logical invocation并投影pending-acquire；initial primary choice只选择acquire route且physical state仍pre-dispatch，backend acquire属于一次effectful operation，必须由generation-0 exact operation-intent winner授权且physical attempt上限=1。commit按上项原子得到lock/draining；abort只有上项正式14字段tuple才走failed negative closure，不能停回pending、预用terminal outcome或同claim重试。unknown/timeout/inconsistent readback只能query并进入blocked recovery；owner death/crash/heartbeat/lease同理不得auto-unlock/accepting，只可由预装`index_under_lock_recovery` actor在旧generation durable fenced、admission持续blocked后，以independent IDX safety choice选中exact member，再由receipt-bound单调successor operation intent接管。若consumer受控证明失败，立即non-PASS且delete=false；
15. interlock=`acquired+atomic_acquired`且active set归零后锁内冻结final manifest。`old_index_delete`另建claim；claim接受只commit一个logical invocation，remote-accepted initial choice=`dispatch_primary`只选择delete route，generation-0 delete operation intent才授权第一次physical delete。same lock generation持续held、admission=draining、manifest和第1–16门均真时才逐target执行；不得第二claim/primary winner/new key，后继每次非query调用各自使用successor intent。partial/unknown默认只query/readback，不得换operation/member；只有旧generation durable fenced、admission blocked、independent IDX safety choice选中exact预装recovery member且recovery operation intent赢得CAS时才可恢复。manifest/consumer/lock/identity漂移在claim前令call=0，在claim后停止并如实进入under-lock outcome；所有恢复必须在lifecycle result前完成；
16. 只要`interlock_acquire_observation_state IN {atomic_acquired,unknown}`或status为`acquired|recovery_required`，explicit release/recovery-handoff义务就不能省略；只有同一claim对qualified backend得到`atomic_not_acquired`且readback证明lock absent/admission accepting时才可无release负向收口。interlock从final enumeration前持续active到delete全部完成，或partial/unknown outcome已按frozen recovery proof恢复/冻结到一致状态。此时先写immutable `index_retirement_under_lock_outcome_v1`，绑定actual removed/remaining/unknown set、typed retrieval/admission state、root-preserved proof、recovery结论及两张claim；再由与executor/materializer分离的只读`index_retirement_release_safety_review_v1`决定exact release destination。该outcome/review不是ticket lifecycle result/review，不消费interlock grant。review PASS后，normal explicit release必须沿same accepted claim/fence赢得exact successor operation intent才可调用；crash态则先由independent IDX safety choice选中预装recovery-handoff member，再赢得对应recovery/release operation intent。写release observation/receipt并投影为`released + closed_after_retirement`，只有已完整restore且review明确允许时才能`released + accepting`，否则保持`recovery_required + blocked_pending_recovery`。explicit release完成后才冻结ticket lifecycle result→result review→terminal；terminal PASS要求全部exact targets处理成功、根保留、无新增对象、interlock全程连续、release receipt有效。partial delete、stale generation release、缺operation intent、自动expiry unlock或result先于release均不能PASS。

若无法满足第10–16项，即使磁盘不足也必须停止或缩小candidate，不得把“已记录不可复现风险”当删除许可。若PREP尚未创建且1–4 predicate=false，PREP与DELETE可分别由其selector-selected exact receipt判not_applicable。PREP一旦applicable或已创建attempt，就必须保留真实lifecycle并形成result/review/terminal；若terminal result证明`delete_still_required=false`，只有DELETE可在新的exact applicability evaluation中判not_applicable，PREP仍保持terminal。若`delete_still_required=true`但第5或8–16项不满足，DELETE是applicable后的blocked/stopped/failed，不是not_applicable；不得改denominator或伪造Phase3 PASS。

删除前 manifest 至少记录：

- exact root resolved path；
- relative path、type、size、mtime、SHA-256 或对超大文件预声明的流式 digest；
- total file/dir/bytes；
- producer command/config/commit；
- input raw/source/object/corpus digests；
- active consumers；
- old query/index contract；
- known non-reproducible items；
- rebuild command、target new adapter、acceptance；
- snapshot/export/restore 或 isolated rebuild proof；
- target canonical path、volume serial/file identity、link count/reparse status/ADS 检查；
- root ACL/owner/audit policy、active handle/service proof；
- closed-world consumer-start registry、每个admission hook qualification、interlock adapter/lock ID/generation/expiry与start-claim schema；
- before-free-space 和预计释放。

删除边界：

- D:\FIN_Insight_Agent\data\indexes 根本身永远不是删除目标，根必须原位保留，ACL、owner、ADS、audit/mount 语义不变；
- 只枚举执行时该根的 direct children 作为显式 target roots，再在每个已验证 target root 内递归；不得对根执行 recursive delete；
- D:\FIN_Insight_Agent\data\staging 不在范围；
- data\processed_private、raw/source/object、workbench_private、eval/report、R14 evidence、Codex live state 均不在范围；
- 即使存在同名 staging，只有位于 data\indexes 内的后代才属于本授权；
- 正常路径必须在一个PowerShell流程中、持续持有same interlock/lock generation，解析并验证全部absolute target；final manifest冻结后，每个target在真正调用前再次做resolved/canonical path、volume/file identity、descendant/reparse/link/ADS/no-new-object检查，再逐项`Remove-Item -LiteralPath`。优先使用handle-relative/no-follow能力；若当前Windows/PowerShell组合不能证明no-follow与identity稳定，则delete grant=false，不得把一次全树检查当持续保证。若该进程崩溃，durable admission fence必须继续deny并进入`blocked_pending_recovery`；只有预装recovery actor/suboperation可开启新的受控恢复流程，绝不能因OS handle消失或TTL到期自动接受consumer；
- 根或任一 target/descendant 出现 reparse point、junction、symlink、mount point、无法解释的 hard-link/reflink、alternate data stream 或 containment/file-identity 漂移时立即停止；
- final enumeration后、两个direct-child之间或recursive walk中出现新file/reparse/link/ADS/consumer，必须立即停止；若尚未删除则delete action不再调用，若已部分删除则在释放interlock前按frozen snapshot/restore路线恢复或冻结truthful failed/unknown状态，不能继续、隐瞒或直接PASS；
- 禁止 glob、环境变量拼接、跨 shell 删除和 broad recursive target。

删除后：

- 记录实际删除项、释放空间和是否可恢复；
- 在独立 post-delete receipt 中绑定 pre-delete authority、实际 target、实际释放字节和恢复路线状态；
- old retrieval 写入 typed state：`consumer_route_availability="suspended"`、`availability_reason="architecture_rebuild"`；禁止另造 `old retrieval status=...` 复合字段；
- lifecycle result之前先完成under-lock outcome及作者分离release-safety review，再由仍active的interlock claim/fence显式release；release receipt绑定same或唯一monotonic recovery-handoff generation、consumer registry、两张start claims、final/actual manifests、remaining/recovery set与review full ref。只有release receipt accepted后才写ticket result/result-review；任何result先于release、stale generation、crash/expiry自动unlock都fail closed；
- 所有依赖旧 index 的命令/API/UI 必须 fail visibly，不能静默返回空结果或 public gap；
- 新 retrieval 只有通过 input digest、row/object count、identifier zero-miss、query suite、known regression、lineage、rebuild、backup/restore 和 fresh review 后才可恢复 active。

### 10.7 Phase 3 退出门

- 13张`QL-{nn}-DECISION-01`均绑定Phase2 registry完整member denominator并terminal/activation；每类由其decision冻结default/至多一个challenger/managed ceiling，或有证据HOLD/EXCLUDE；
- exact versions、locks、SBOM/license、deployment profile、backup/export 已固定；
- frozen benchmark 和失败 artifacts 完整；
- `QL-CLEANUP-INDEX-01` 已按`cleanup_source_attempt_frontier_v1`得到唯一selected expansion；每个target-producer candidate attempt manifest完整，aggregate/decision/obligation不自指进入frontier，全部cleanup obligations有selected NA或exact-target terminal PASS，pending/漏项/漂移=0；
- critical FIN slices 无未解释退化；
- model/managed runs 有 TokenBudgetBasis 和 receipt；
- 资源、成本、隐私、安全、退出结论完整；
- fresh reviewer 可独立复算主要指标；
- 失败 candidate 未进入正式 requirements/compose/mainline。
- `PHASE-3-CLOSE-01`冻结Phase3 base+expanded exit universe、Phase2 candidate-registry digest、13张decision、selected cleanup expansion和全部dispositions；result review/terminal/activation后才令Phase4 eligible。

## 11. Phase 4：目标架构与迁移方案冻结

### 11.1 目标

只根据 Phase 1 真实依赖和 Phase 3 实测 winner 冻结目标架构；把候选目录结构转为 ADR、ports、data contracts、deployment profiles 和 migration waves。

### 11.2 必须形成的 ADR

1. `ADR-01` FIN domain kernel boundary；
2. `ADR-02` canonical envelopes 与 Pydantic contract source；
3. `ADR-03` source/XBRL/document adapters；
4. `ADR-04` metadata/artifact/storage；
5. `ADR-05` retrieval/ranking primary；
6. `ADR-06` semantic candidate/human escalation；
7. `ADR-07` Agent state/provider/external side-effect receipt，以及“逻辑调用已承诺”与“物理效果已发生”不得混写的执行模型；
8. `ADR-08` observability/eval authority separation；
9. `ADR-09` Workbench review/product boundary；
10. `ADR-10` report/citation/render；
11. `ADR-11` security/privacy/IAM trigger；
12. `ADR-12` legacy bridge/retirement；
13. `ADR-13` deployment profiles；
14. `ADR-14` backup/restore/rollback/exit；
15. `ADR-15` substantive action 的adapter capability、8×6 physical-operation/effect-observation matrix、6×6 route-operation gate、initial primary route choice、per-call one-shot operation intent、typed-predecessor independent safety-suboperation choice、result boundary、三类互斥physical recovery authority basis与独立terminal Git stale no-retry lifecycle。`ADR-15`必须绑定`ADR-01..14` exact result/review/terminal refs、Phase1约束和Phase3 selected winners，并产出/freeze/fresh-review `migration_slice_registry_v1`及全部slice capability/rollback/result-boundary digests。

### 11.3 每个 port 的最低合同

- request/response schema；
- domain ID 与 vendor ID 映射；
- allowed side effects；
- timeout/retry/idempotency；
- 默认拒绝的`adapter_effect_capability_profile_v1`：server-side idempotency的scope/retention/readback语义、fencing、same-server-operation resume/cancel、effect query，以及SDK内建retry是否可被上层关闭和审计；未逐项证明的能力一律为false；
- `physical_operation_kind`对全部registered effect-observation/action-result状态的total allow/deny matrix：initial primary choice只在`pre_dispatch_no_call_proven`选择dispatch/no-call route；第一次`initiate_first_physical_call`还须generation-0 operation intent；`no_call_proven`永久禁止primary dispatch/resume/complete；`no_observation|effect_unknown`默认只允许`query_readback`；有同一server-operation证明的`in_flight_same_server_operation_proven`也须新的successor operation intent才可continue/complete/cancel；result frozen/consumed或attempt terminal后全部physical operation为false；
- 分立且不可互相冒充的durable primitives：`primary_effect_choice_cas_v2`只选initial route；`physical_operation_intent_cas_v1`按generation-0 null predecessor或successor exact observation与generation为每次非query call选择唯一winner；`prearmed_safety_suboperation_choice_cas_v3`只在`accepted_safe_if_no_call_primary_choice_v1|physical_operation_observation_receipt_v2` exact-one predecessor下选exact recovery member。query/readback不选择effect；缺current intent不得物理执行，primary winner不得授权后继call，safety winner的实际调用仍须自己的operation intent；
- `primary_effect_observation_state`及每次readback/continuation/cancel的receipt、vendor operation ID/fence/idempotency/response identity与状态转移；同一个idempotency key本身不等于same-operation proof，也不等于exactly-once；
- 事前随authority/claim预装且按exact action/target/adapter/fence/idempotency/maximum-call冻结的三类互斥physical member：`index_under_lock_recovery`、`ordinary_same_claim_recovery`、`q_quiescence`；只能由对应basis的independent safety choice消费，实际调用再取得自己的operation intent，不能在failure/Q/expiry后补签或跨branch借用。terminal Git stale只走独立no-retry lifecycle closure；
- result boundary：先完成或证明无法继续的physical-effect/recovery closure，再冻结唯一result；result后旧ticket不得query/resume/cancel/compensate，若仍有外部未知效果则该ticket只能FAIL并保留恢复义务，由预注册新ticket/operations authority接管，不能把unknown伪装成完成；
- artifact/receipt；
- privacy/security；
- error taxonomy；
- observability fields；
- test fixture；
- fallback 是否属于产品行为；
- rollback；
- deprecation。

- `ADR-15`还必须逐adapter冻结`physical_operation_observation_receipt_v2`的两分支、三种query binding、same-path revision/expected-old语义、finite read cap及classification projection；safety predecessor只接受latest intent-bound non-null projection。任何把intentless query、selected member或旧route解释为effect/recovery authority的adapter不得进入`migration_slice_registry_v1`；

### 11.4 迁移波次冻结

| Wave | 内容 | 前置 | 回滚 |
|---|---|---|---|
| W0 | namespace/ports/contracts scaffold，不改产品结果 | Phase 4 ADR | 以 `unconsumed_migration_scaffold_retire_delete` 删除新未消费 scaffold |
| W1 | passive trace/experiment/contract validation | QL-01/12 DECISION-selected candidates | 以 `migration_slice_stop_disable` 关闭 exporter，FIN artifact 不变 |
| W2 | source/XBRL/document intake adapters shadow/dual-read | QL-02/03/04/05 DECISION-selected candidates | 以 `migration_slice_stop_disable` 关闭 candidate adapter；旧 capture 始终是 current，保留 shadow artifact |
| W3a | canonical metadata passive projection | QL-01/06 DECISION-selected candidates | 以 `migration_slice_stop_disable` 停止 projection；canonical FIN artifact 不变 |
| W3b | artifact/storage shadow | QL-06 DECISION-selected candidate | 以 `migration_slice_stop_disable` 关闭 shadow writer；以 `unadmitted_shadow_artifact_retire_delete` 删除未晋升 shadow DB/objects |
| W3c | fixed qualified embedder 下的 index-engine A/B | QL-07/08 DECISION-selected candidates | 以 `qualification_candidate_artifact_retire_delete` 丢弃 qualification indexes；旧 index 不变 |
| W3d | retriever dual-read | W3c winner + exact query contract | 以 `migration_slice_stop_disable` 关闭 candidate reader；旧 retriever 始终是 current，不改 admission |
| W3e | target-in-pool 后的 reranker shadow | QL-09 DECISION-selected candidate + W3d pool pass | 以 `migration_slice_stop_disable` 关闭 reranker；保留原 candidate order |
| W4a | provider SDK saved/synthetic fixture parity | QL-11 DECISION-selected provider result | 以 `migration_slice_stop_disable` 关闭 candidate transport；旧 transport 始终是 current，无 live call |
| W4b | workflow/checkpoint over saved provider fixtures | QL-11 DECISION-selected workflow result + W4a | 以 `migration_slice_stop_disable` 关闭新 workflow；current runner/checkpoint namespace 从未切换 |
| W4c | semantic candidate shadow | QL-10 DECISION-selected candidate + qualified human gold | 以 `migration_slice_stop_disable` 关闭 semantic route；以 `unadmitted_shadow_artifact_retire_delete` 删除未准入 shadow projection |
| W4d | provider/workflow/semantic bounded integration | W4a/W4b/W4c 分别 PASS | 以 `migration_slice_stop_disable` 关闭整体 feature flag；三个独立 rollback 均可执行 |
| W5 | non-released report/render/review UX candidate preview | QL-13 DECISION-selected candidate + Workbench contract | 以 `migration_slice_stop_disable` 关闭 candidate preview；旧 renderer/read surface 始终是 current |
| W6 | Phase 6 controlled consumer cutover 与 legacy regression-only | 对应 ACC-Sn terminal PASS＋downstream activation后，CUTOVER-Sn applicability；仅 applicable 时走 candidate/review/execution-authority/result/terminal | compatibility shim re-enable；不恢复任何 namespaced historical R-attempt implementation |

Wave 只是排序容器，不是一次实现提交。W3a–W3e、W4a–W4d 每项必须是独立 `MIG-*` slice，分别具有 input digest、feature switch、result、fresh review 和 rollback；不得用一个 W3/W4 commit 同时改变多项变量。

Phase 4退出前禁止批量移动当前模块、删除旧代码或更换产品主路由。exact 15-member `ADR-01..15` ticket-universe、每票result/review/terminal refs、`ADR-15` owning refs与fresh-reviewed migration-registry digest先由`PHASE-4-CLOSE-01`冻结denominator/result；缺ADR-15、重复/额外ID、ID→subject或digest漂移一律false，只有result-review/terminal PASS/downstream activation后才令Phase5 candidate eligible。

## 12. Phase 5：当前分支实际集成与仓库整理

### 12.1 实施原则

- 先 port/adapter，再 consumer；
- 先 shadow/dual-read，再 cutover；
- 不对同一 authority 双写；
- 每个 slice 可独立 review/test/rollback；
- 不把失败候选加入正式 dependency；
- 不同时改变 data source、parser、retriever、reranker、semantic judge 和 product acceptance；
- legacy bridge 必须有 removal condition，不得成为永久第二架构。

### 12.2 每个 integration slice

必须包含：

- problem/user value；
- inputs/outputs/non-goals；
- affected contracts/API/UI/data；
- exact dependency/version；
- implementation and compatibility files；
- consumer只调用FIN port，vendor SDK/CLI/service必须包在该slice唯一薄adapter内；adapter不得自行扩大retry、fallback、write、delete、resume、cancel或compensation权限，SDK默认retry若不可关闭或不可receipt化则该candidate不得获得有副作用的execution authority；
- 冻结并实测该adapter的`adapter_effect_capability_profile_v1`、registered `physical_operation_kind` total matrix、server operation identity/readback语义、idempotency scope/retention/fence，以及unsupported能力的default-deny fixture；
- 有外部或仓库副作用时，slice必须物化唯一initial primary-choice receipt、全部operation-intent generations/winners及adapter operation/observation receipts；adapter支持的safe-if-no-call/post-dispatch recovery还须保存typed-predecessor independent safety-choice receipt与实际safety call自己的operation intent，并把ordinary same-claim、Q、IDX under-lock三类physical恢复member及其互斥basis预装到candidate/authority/claim；terminal Git stale只绑定no-retry lifecycle profile；无副作用slice显式写canonical not-applicable，而不是省略；
- migration/read/write route；
- feature/config switch；
- epoch-scoped source attempt identity、exact action allowlist及由`action_cleanup_target_capability_registry_v1`派生的frontier role；
- actual action/resource inventory digest，以及实际 scaffold/shadow/route/artifact 的完整 `cleanup_target_manifest_v1`（target producer无target也写canonical empty，non-target强制canonical empty；role/action/inventory不符触发mandatory rollover）；
- deterministic/unit/integration/product tests；
- data migration/rebuild；
- security/privacy；
- observability；
- rollback：区分“切回route/停止后续调用”和“逆转已经发生的外部效果”；前者仍须exact switch authority、primary choice与operation intent，后者只有对应预装member在independent safety choice中胜出、effect observation满足门且实际rollback call另有operation intent时可执行，不能把feature flag关闭冒充外部补偿；result frozen后不得沿旧ticket再做rollback物理调用；
- known gaps；
- fresh reviewer verdict。

### 12.3 依赖与配置治理

- pyproject 只加入胜出且被当前 slice 消费的 dependencies；
- large-model/optional stacks 分 optional profile；
- lockfile、image digest、model revision 和 browser/runtime version 固定；
- compose 只包含正式 profile；
- qualification-only service 不进入默认 startup；
- secrets 只用 env/secret store；
- no-data、partial-data、service-down 和 rollback 路径都 fail visibly。

### 12.4 旧代码处置

每个旧模块只能进入：

- active compatibility；
- shadow comparison；
- regression fixture；
- archived/retired。

retire 前必须：

- active consumer=0；
- import/runtime registry=0；
- equivalent/new product path passed；
- rollback snapshot 可恢复；
- docs/redirect map 更新；
- historical failure/evidence preserved；
- fresh audit。

### 12.5 Phase 5 退出门

- 每个计划执行的 MIG slice 都有同 epoch candidate/review/execution authority/result/result-review/terminal，且单变量、feature switch、stop/rollback可复算；
- 每个 MIG source attempt 的实际target manifest完整，source-attempt frontier已关闭，所有retry/new-attempt grants均consumed/expired/revoked；
- `MIG-CLEANUP-INDEX-01` 有唯一 selected `cleanup_obligation_expansion_v1`，其frontier digest等于current canonical source frontier；
- expansion内每个source attempt×target obligation均由selected exact applicability receipt排除，或由独立exact-target cleanup ticket terminal PASS收口；pending、漏target、重复target、expansion外target、旧expansion、cross-obligation reuse均为0；
- legacy/current route、shared/admitted data、其他slice和冻结历史证据未被cleanup authority触及；失败/held slice保留immutable result，不因清理而改写为PASS；
- Phase 6只能消费通过本门并由independent downstream activation明确开放的slice；不得用cleanup完成度代替产品能力通过。
- `PHASE-5-CLOSE-01`绑定上述slice/cleanup/rollback denominator并通过result review、terminal PASS与activation后，才令Phase6 candidate eligible。

## 13. Phase 6：真实 case 与 S1–S5 产品验收

### 13.1 case 设计

至少覆盖：

- DELL、MU、NVDA 当前案例；
- 多个未用于实现调参的异质公司；
- 10-K、10-Q、8-K、earnings transcript、IR HTML/PDF、产品页、客户/供应商/监管来源；
- digital-native、扫描、双栏、复杂表、跨页表、脚注、重述、冲突；
- exact identifier、period、unit、scale、amendment；
- counterevidence、hard negative、source role、rights；
- malicious/malformed intake fixture；
- source absent、object failure、index failure、provider failure、human defer 等负路径。

开发、valid temporal、frozen test 和 external holdout 分离；test 结果不能反向调 threshold/route/model。

### 13.2 S1–S5 验收链

~~~text
S1 source/capture/parse/object/index/retrieval/rank
   -> CandidateDecision / Evidence admission / GapEligibility
S2 NumericFact / PIT / unit-period-vintage-conflict / financial bridge
S3 research plan / evidence-bound judgment / counter / WWC / report model
S4 review / new-attempt repair / approval / reader citation / source appendix
S5 product, operations, security, rollback and release decision
~~~

每一阶段独立通过；后序 PASS 不能抵消前序失败。

S4/S5 的机器顺序采用 6.4 的完整序列。`ACC-S4-CANDIDATE` 票据仅存在、仅 candidate commit 或自由文本状态都不产生权限；每次生成使用不可变 `ACC-S4-CANDIDATE-Cnn`。只有合法 execution-authority receipt 才能临时打开 `report_candidate_generation`。report result 必须先冻结，再由作者分离 reviewer 只读审查；PASS 才能得到 terminal PASS，FAIL 必须先形成 immutable failure receipt，并以新 Cnn candidate/review/authority/result 修正，reviewer 不在同一结果上 repair。terminal PASS 后还必须单独物化 `ACC-S4-CANDIDATE downstream_activation_receipt`；主票 `ACC-S4.predecessor` 同时绑定 candidate terminal result digest 与该 activation identity，之后 `ACC-S4` 自己的 candidate review 和 execution-authority receipt 才能打开 exact human action。human result/denominator/authority review 后才能 terminal PASS，并另行 downstream activate。`ACC-S5.predecessor` 必须绑定该 terminal digest 与 downstream receipt；qualified-human product acceptance 完成并审阅前不得 ACC-S5 terminal PASS。全过程 current reader publication/release 继续为 false。

### 13.3 质量门

S1：

- source/capture/object/index route receipts；
- identifier zero-miss；
- target-in-pool；
- Recall@k/MRR/nDCG 与 material slices；
- exact locator；
- Candidate 不冒充 Evidence；
- genuine gap 只有在本地/可达路线穷尽后。

S2：

- entity/period/unit/scale/dimension/vintage/conflict；
- XBRL 与 visual channel 冲突显式；
- units/share → ASP/mix → PVM → product profit → working capital；
- 输入不足时 null/gap，不伪造。

S3：

- 每个模型节点独立 TokenBudgetBasis；
- strict schema、visible source authority、hard validator；
- counterevidence、causal boundary、materiality、WWC；
- no ungrounded numeric or hidden authority borrowing；
- interrupted/timeout/duplicate-risk 可恢复。

S4：

- admit/reject/rebind/defer/reopen；
- required-item denominator 完整，deny/defer/timeout 不当 PASS；
- author/reviewer/human 身份与角色分离；
- claim→passage/page/table/cell；
- reader-visible title/issuer/date/period/locator/URL；
- source appendix；
- repair 不覆盖原失败；report result FAIL 必须用新的 `ACC-S4-CANDIDATE-Cnn` attempt。

S5：

- end-user workflow solves the task；
- latency/cost/resource；
- backup/restore/upgrade/rollback；
- security/privacy/license/IAM profile；
- accessibility、desktop/mobile、no-data/service-down；
- immutable qualified-human product-acceptance result 与 denominator/review digest；
- publication/release 独立决定。

### 13.4 failure injection

- claim接受后、choice前stale，以及choice接受后、intent前stale：两者都必须用intentless query receipt、projection=null、zero push形成non-PASS→独立review→old terminal；另注入nonquery intent=null、query伪造complete/absent/in-flight、member已选后observation降为unknown、stale read cap后第二query/第二push、observation revision sibling/replay和intentless query被safety choice消费，全部必须fail closed；

必须覆盖：

- source/network timeout、partial、redirect；
- parser crash/timeout/resource；
- database/index unavailable/corrupt；
- provider complete 后 receipt 前崩溃；
- checkpoint 前后崩溃、duplicate resume；
- claim accepted后/initial primary choice前、primary choice accepted后/first operation intent前、intent accepted后/first adapter call前分别崩溃；证明primary choice不伪造in-flight，且只有qualified no-call closure可令`pre_dispatch_no_call_proven→no_call_proven`并永久禁止同一ticket再dispatch/resume/complete；
- dispatch已发出但零观测、response丢失、operation ID丢失、timeout/unknown、进程死亡与重启，分别覆盖`no_observation`、`effect_unknown`和`in_flight_same_server_operation_proven`；前两者只能query，后者除same-server-operation证明完整外还必须让continue/complete/cancel各自竞争新的successor operation intent；cancel是effecting，response lost/timeout保持unknown而不得投影absent；
- SDK暗中重试、idempotency retention过期、同key不同scope/target/epoch、fence失效、readback返回foreign/ambiguous operation；均不得因“key相同”重放或判完成；
- 两个initial primary choices并发、continue/complete/cancel在同一predecessor generation竞争operation intent、primary continuation与safety choice并发、两个safety members并发、任一choice/intent remote结果未知及winner已定后alternate；每类CAS必须只有一个remote-accepted winner，未知只readback，loser物理调用次数为0；safety winner成立后同predecessor的primary continuation永久false，实际safety call仍须自己的operation intent；
- ordinary same-claim、Q quiescence、IDX under-lock三类member各自的safety-choice→safety-operation-intent→partial/crash/recovery，以及terminal Git stale的query/readback→non-PASS→old terminal→fresh-ref new attempt，以及cross-branch member替换、晚补签、缺intent、超过maximum-call；后者全部拒绝；
- result冻结前崩溃与result冻结/消费/terminal之后的query/resume/cancel/compensation；后者全部拒绝并要求新authority处理仍存在的外部恢复义务；
- IDX acquire `idx_atomic_abort_negative_closure_v3`正式14字段正例、每字段single flip反例、`terminal_lifecycle_outcome`被误作前置的反例，以及timeout/unknown/inconsistent readback保持`blocked_pending_recovery`和release义务；
- malformed/empty/truncated schema；
- human deny/defer/timeout；
- telemetry backend unavailable；
- renderer failure；
- rollback and rebuild。

任何未知 completion、duplicate risk、data drift 或 authority mismatch 必须 fail closed。fail closed不是“把结果写成失败后继续用旧ticket重试”：effect仍unknown时先保留query-only/recovery义务；一旦旧ticket result frozen，就只能由事前注册的后续operations/recovery authority承接，禁止结果后物理调用。

### 13.5 `DELL-03B-S1-R14-REPLACEMENT-CLOSURE` 同阶段关闭票

这张票独立于每个QL-10 candidate安装/小切片资格及`QL-10-DECISION-01`，也独立于通用`ACC-S1`。任一candidate PASS或family decision只说明所选semantic路线可继续；不能关闭RC-S1-109/110或退役旧回归证据。

固定输入至少包括：

- `dell_03b_s1_internal_chain.r14_implementation_freeze="7e25cad95ee84b39fb2a51063100405bc27da6e5"`；
- frozen source：1,888 rows，SHA-256=`d4c7e51790713d32fc10a9d0382b617f8ebd60861a3741d3adcee34392045d45`；
- frozen compiled：34,199 rows，SHA-256=`1c3e48486f933d23306dbabacb1641e26cb9bbc5b474da932d602752dff3fa92`；
- full corpus=`27,026`，原 preview=`26,787 pass / 239 fail`；
- case failure inventory digest=`49acf114c03ab97e059ee3bd928736d06d70b1d5a6d8d3af2dcdfabc68e2a5d1`；
- event mismatch inventory digest=`68d267f77400a350cd698bf3c4baf7152067b437290084596a4fa370965276e5`；
- mismatch events=`277`，四形态=`246/9/5/17`；
- 原 population/event/price/property/mutation/resource/transaction/privacy validators 与 gates。

必须分层保存 old parser output、new deterministic/LLM SemanticCandidate、hard-validator output、abstain/human route 和 case-correct human-adjudicated gold。旧 parser 与旧 validator 的错误输出只是 regression/adversarial baseline，不是真值；模型输出也不是真值。

关闭验收同时要求：

1. 239/239 failed cases 与 277/277 mismatch events 均有 case-correct human adjudication；
2. replacement 对 full frozen corpus 的 validation 为 27,026/27,026，277/277 mismatch 已依新真值消除；允许模型 abstain，但必须在该冻结资格内由预声明 human route 形成最终 adjudicated outcome，不能把 abstain 当自动 PASS；
3. zero new failure code，原 population/event/price/property/mutation/resource/transaction/privacy gates 不弱化；
4. 禁止 case key、text SHA、event ID、first-exception 或已知例句特判；
5. exact-span、strict schema、hard validator、abstain/escalation、materiality slice 与未观察异质 case 均通过；
6. old/new full-corpus regression、mutation 与 fresh author-separated read-only review 为 PASS；
7. review PASS 后仍由 Owner 以独立 receipt 明确关闭、合并或重新处置 RC-S1-109/110；R14 自身永久保持 not PASS。

任何一项不满足，RC-S1-109/110 继续 open，replacement 不能成为 current consumer，Phase 7 只能标 `partial/stopped`，不得标 `complete`，也不得删除冻结 R14 failure/corpus/validator/harness。

- 每张observation按`receipt_kind`验收：nonquery必须有exact current-call intent；query不得有本次call intent。intentless terminal-stale query必须projection=null、read count≤1、same-attempt future nonquery=0并已进入non-PASS/old-terminal链；不能再用“每张observation都有intent”或“query无intent所以任意readback可投影effect”两种错误简化；

### 13.6 Phase 6 退出门

- 关键金融 slice 无未解释 P0/P1；
- deterministic、model、human 三层结果分开；
- DeepSeek 真实 case 不是 self-judge 唯一标准；
- `ACC-S4-CANDIDATE-Cnn` report result frozen/read-only review/terminal PASS、stable candidate downstream activation、`ACC-S4` qualified-human immutable result/denominator/authority review terminal PASS 与其独立 downstream activation 均完成；
- `ACC-S5.predecessor` 精确绑定 ACC-S4 terminal digest/downstream receipt，qualified-human product acceptance 与 product review terminal PASS；
- cost/latency/resource/security/operations 可接受；
- old/new comparison 与 rollback 演练通过；
- 所有执行过substantive action的票据均有唯一initial primary-choice winner或canonical no-effect/NA projection；每个非query call均有唯一remote-accepted operation-intent及对应observation receipt；adapter支持的safe-if-no-call/post-dispatch recovery另有唯一safety-choice与recovery operation-intent，terminal Git stale则明确无safety call。没有未决intent generation、未收口`in_flight_same_server_operation_proven`、未解释`no_observation|effect_unknown`、cancel ambiguous被误判absent、未消费safety choice、超额调用、cross-branch恢复或result后旧ticket物理操作；若外部效果仍无法判定，相关能力/phase必须FAIL或partial并生成明确后续恢复义务，不能normal PASS；
- S1–S5 各自 verdict 明确；
- `DELL-03B-S1-R14-REPLACEMENT-CLOSURE` 已 PASS 且 Owner 已处置 RC-S1-109/110，或程序明确以 partial/stopped 收口而非 complete；
- 不通过弱化 validator、删 case 或隐藏失败得到 PASS。
- normal route由`PHASE-6-CLOSE-01`冻结上述全部denominator并result-review/terminal PASS/activation，才令Phase7 normal eligible；若无法normal PASS，只能在全部attempt terminal、无in-flight/unknown且independent review确认后走`PHASE-6-PARTIAL-CLOSEOUT-01`，令Phase7 partial-closeout-only，release/retire/cutover永远false。

## 14. Phase 7：迁移收口与最终可行性方案

### 14.1 收口工作

1. 正式 default/challenger/managed ceiling 清单；
2. 清理失败/未消费实验 dependency、service 和 image；
3. legacy code 的 `implementation_lifecycle` 转为 compatibility/regression_only/retired；
4. 完成 archive/redirect/import/runtime consumer map；
5. 固定 lock、SBOM、license、security、deployment；
6. 备份/恢复/升级/rollback/exit runbook；
7. 运维、监控、成本和容量计划；
8. 最终 architecture、feasibility、migration、operations 文档；
9. Project OS、worklog、README、checklist、public docs 一致；
10. clean branch、exact staging、commit、冻结remote identity校验、lease-guarded exact-expected-old fast-forward publication/readback与local/origin-tracking exact reconciliation；
11. `CLS-06` fresh engineering/Evidence/report/product/security independent review 与 Owner recommendation；
12. 先由`phase7_pre_release_decision_ready_v1`从CLS-01..06 exact denominator、quiescence/grants与scope重算true；随后`CLS-07-OWNER-RELEASE-DECISION`生成绑定该digest的packet，接受只读完整性review，再由Owner签发sovereign external payload；该payload按预注册no-execution分支物化frozen result、接受独立binding/result review、最后terminal，不由程序或CLS-06自动推断；
13. 只有pre-release gate仍true且digest未漂移、Owner decision=`release`、CLS-07 result-review/terminal passed后，才评估独立conditional `RELEASE-EXECUTION-01`；request=false走exact not-applicable，true才完整走candidate/review/execution-authority/publication/result/review/terminal，decision receipt本身不执行发布。
14. `program_terminal_ready_reducer_v1`先从最终immutable evidence得到ready状态；随后仅`PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01`可在terminal-ready root下，用`terminal_project_os_three_path_publication_v3`和逐字节绑定的`terminal_git_publication_adapter_effect_capability_profile_v3`，经start claim→initial primary choice→generation-0 publication operation intent→一次expected-old direct-child Git call→qualified readback，把Owner/RELEASE/Z/partial结果同步到 capability ledger、current context pack、root-cause ledger这exact三条Project OS路径并完成独立review/terminal。该adapter仅允许query/first-initiate、其余operation=false、internal retry=0，unknown只query；stale后同attempt只readback并non-PASS terminal，fresh exact-ref前new call=0；control transport不得代发。它不允许写计划、source worklog、R14证据或第四路径。只有该sync PASS、digest未漂移且pre-seal stream=`open_clean|integrity_failure_closed`，`program_terminal_and_incident_seal_reducer_v1`才一次输出final terminal＋sealed pair；不得在CLS-05或三份sync正文中预写future Owner outcome/final pair、分两步互相推导terminal/sealed，也不得在final pair后再寻找写权限。

`CLS-03` 的 legacy retirement 硬门为：historical implementation package discovery=0、active imports=0、Runtime Registry refs=0、current consumers=0、archive redirect/origin/digest complete、`verify_active_baseline.py` 与 clean-main proof 通过。rollback 只能重新启用已资格验证的薄 compatibility adapter，不能把任何责任命名空间下、文件名可能标作 R3–R14 的 attempt-specific implementation 还原为活动实现。

`CLS-07-OWNER-RELEASE-DECISION` 是稳定票据，不是自由文本签字。固定输入至少包括：`phase7_pre_release_decision_ready_v1=true`及full digest；CLS-01..06 denominator；`ACC-S5` terminal/activation；DELL closure与RC-S1-109/110处置；exact product/version/deployment/consumer/publication/rollback scope、known boundaries与expiry。先冻结candidate packet，再由作者分离packet reviewer只读审完整性；reviewer不替Owner决策。Owner随后签发`owner_sovereign_release_decision_payload_v1`，固定`decision=release|no_release|hold`、显式boolean request、scope refs、packet/gate digest、owner identity/signed_at。release时request必须显式true/false；no_release/hold时false；缺失、默认、翻转或scope/gate drift均invalid。request只控制RELEASE applicability。no-execution sovereign branch写`execution_authority_receipt=null/not_applicable`；result materializer只嵌入exact Owner bytes，独立result reviewer只核验identity/binding/vocabulary/request/scope/completeness，不改决策；terminal才映射release→passed、no_release→stopped、hold→held。`sovereign_decision_role_constraints_v1`固定candidate author≠packet reviewer/result reviewer，packet reviewer≠Owner，Owner≠materializer/result reviewer，packet reviewer≠result reviewer，result reviewer≠materializer/commit writer；所有reviewer还受6.4.1通用只读不等式约束。任何相等、direct decision→terminal、result覆盖或无result-review均fail closed。

Owner decision result与发布执行必须分离。registry提前冻结CLS-07 candidate、packet review、Owner payload、frozen result、independent result-review与terminal不同paths；sovereign branch只能用于CLS-07。发布链全部属于`RELEASE-EXECUTION-01` compact namespace。只有pre-release gate在RELEASE applicability snapshot和action-start两次均重算true且digest/scope与CLS-07完全一致、decision=release、CLS-07 terminal passed，才依据immutable request评估/执行；true时candidate绑定gate、decision-result/terminal、scope、allow/deny、operator、start/expiry、rollback/post-action，review PASS后由独立issuer物化authority；false只有selected exact NA。no_release/hold不得产生RELEASE candidate/authority；即便release，gate drift、authority缺失/过期/消费时granted=false。

CLS-07/RELEASE mutation必须拒绝：CLS-01..05 pending/in-flight或pre-release gate false仍建CLS-07；gate digest漂移；Owner payload前result/terminal；decision直跳terminal；任一sovereign role equality；非法vocabulary；missing/default request；no_release/hold＋request=true；request只存在candidate/自由文本/materializer；result后翻转；scope不一致；reviewer改decision；共用path；no_release/hold产生RELEASE；release未terminal评估RELEASE；request false建candidate；request true判NA；gate false/drift仍authority/action；缺RELEASE authority/action-start仍publication；CLS-07冒充RELEASE authority；RELEASE issuer=operator；ready/final sync预写或覆盖；cross-epoch replay。

### 14.2 完成定义

本程序只有在以下全部成立时才 complete：

- R14 已正确 strategic close，未假装 PASS；
- `DELL-03B-S1-R14-REPLACEMENT-CLOSURE` 已 PASS 且 Owner 已明确处置 RC-S1-109/110；若仍 open，程序只能 partial/stopped；
- 旧规划已被当前程序和 ADR 正确 supersede；
- S1–S5 每个能力有 Build/Adopt/Hold/Retire；
- Adopt 能力有广泛 longlist、排除记录和 research saturation；
- 胜出组件已实际安装、固定版本、SBOM/license 和部署画像；
- Z 盘 lab 可复现；
- 当前分支已按目标架构集成；
- FIN domain kernel 与 vendor/framework 隔离；
- 旧代码有兼容、回归、退役和 rollback，历史实现不在默认 package/Runtime/API/UI/CI 主路径；
- 真实 case 和 DeepSeek 允许节点已验证；
- critical financial slice 无未解释重大错误；
- S1–S5、人审、报告、产品、运维、安全分别有 verdict；
- 每个有副作用的finished attempt都满足：initial primary choice唯一且只选route；每个已发生的非query call都有对应remote-accepted operation intent，同一predecessor generation至多一个intent winner；每个adapter-supported safe-if-no-call/post-dispatch recovery都有对应typed-predecessor independent safety choice和recovery operation intent；terminal Git stale明确没有safety call；query/readback不计为新primary effect，cancel lost/timeout保持unknown；全部physical operation与effect-observation转移可receipt复算；没有借SDK retry、same idempotency key或新scope造成第二logical/physical effect；没有result frozen后沿旧ticket查询、继续、取消或补偿；
- ordinary same-claim、Q quiescence、IDX under-lock三类事前预装physical member均由且仅由对应basis的independent safety choice消费，实际调用另有operation intent并进入唯一terminal projection；terminal Git stale只query/readback并进入non-PASS lifecycle terminal；预装本身不等于已选择或可调用，未消费member明确revoked/expired，不能在program terminal后复活；
- 任何仍为`no_observation|effect_unknown|in_flight_same_server_operation_proven`的外部效果均已使原票据非PASS，并有可审计的新authority/operations handoff；不得用“最终文档已写完”覆盖未关闭物理义务；
- finite `pre_release_completion_gate_registry_v1`与`phase7_pre_release_review_denominator_v1`全部满足，`phase7_pre_release_decision_ready_v1=true`且digest从CLS-07到RELEASE action-start无漂移；`CLS-06` review terminal PASS、`CLS-07-OWNER-RELEASE-DECISION` release且result-review/terminal passed；RELEASE在request=false时有selected exact NA，true时有事前独立authority、已消费one-shot、post-action result/review/terminal PASS；
- 没有隐藏失败、削弱 validator 或用 vendor benchmark 替代产品证明；
- `program_terminal_ready_reducer_v1=ready_complete`，`PROGRAM-TERMINAL-SYNC-01`绑定同一digest并result-review/terminal PASS；Git/Project OS clean、同步、可恢复，随后joint reducer唯一输出`(program_terminal=complete,incident_stream=sealed)` pair；

若某组件或产品门失败，可以由program reducer形成partial/stopped/failed结论或HOLD_TRIGGER，不能偷换complete。只有pre-release gate已证明全链静默后，CLS-07 hold/no_release才能分别进入serial hold resolution或直接stopped；更早的Owner停止必须走program-stop/Q/Z。运行中stop只形成latch并强制安全收口；只有Z projection形成terminal。mandatory＋stop同时存在时完整性证据保留，但安全closure stopped、unsafe failed且无successor。

## 15. 跨阶段质量、测试与证据等级

### 15.1 测试层级

V1/V3结构测试另设observation-union suite：逐字段校验discriminant、required-vs-null、三个query binding kind、revision predecessor、intent-bound projection、intentless projection=null、finite stale read cap、same-attempt nonquery永久deny、safety predecessor eligibility及terminal Git conditional idempotency/fence scope；该suite独立于48格physical与36格route分母，不新增第九state、第七operation、路径或authority。

| Level | 内容 | 何时运行 |
|---|---|---|
| V0 | compile、JSON/schema、changed-file static、diff | 每次小改 |
| V1 | ticket direct unit/contract/security fixture | 每张票 |
| V2 | adjacent adapter/consumer/regression | 每个 slice |
| V3 | phase integration、frozen benchmark/rebuild/recovery | phase freeze |
| V4 | full repository、frontend/build/browser/secret | shared surface 或 phase close |
| V5 | real-case S1–S5、model/human/product/operations | Phase 6 |

governance mutation suite 是V1/V3硬组成：applicability覆盖R9/R10、A9/A10及11个unique events组成的I0..I10 chain、raw-trigger→A intake mode、same-target reevaluation、candidate后plan-change修改/撤回supersession、latch-effective verdict、gap/duplicate/sibling、X→Y→X、compact path/budget与cross-epoch；qualification覆盖A/B sibling candidate、DECISION cutoff前retry/后freeze、完整member decision与cross-candidate cleanup deny；cleanup覆盖action-target-role truth table、producer-frontier/phase-universe分轴、自指/漏producer/non-target actual target/derived obligation、多attempt/targets/empty set、pre/post-Phase PASS drift→mandatory rollover、漏/重/late/old/family grant及所有same-epoch refresh deny；epoch覆盖pre-G remote-CAS acceptance、change/stop/integrity intake、trigger truth table、late escalation各stage、hold→stop/mandatory、combined mandatory+stop、G/Q/terminal-ready root variants、revoked-unstarted/current rollover closure、safe stopped/unsafe failed/mandatory-only superseded、post-Z destination discriminant；Phase7覆盖finite pre-release denominator、Q优先、effective cutover rollback、ready→exact三路径sync→joint terminal/seal pair、covered anomaly failed-only、无Phase7 close、normal/partial reducer、exact NA、terminal/hold/invalid priority；CLS-07/RELEASE覆盖sovereign role inequalities、Owner request及missing/default/flip/scope/gate drift和publication-before-ready deny。任何非法fixture被接受都阻止对应Phase PASS/program terminal或action grant。

本摘要不得缩减§7.5。V1/V3必须逐项包含：五个pre-H0 canonical对象及各自expected SHA、seed内remote-identity subhash、E0→E1 reservation/root vectors；compact authority key的exact四字段RFC8785 preimage、static source kind仅作evidence、H0-backed/H0-less同输入同key；全部special source/epoch/sequence/operation/safety-generation token的有限grammar、typed resolver唯一命中、uint64 `Emax` successor请求必经integrity I→failed Z且没有successor；exact expected-old/readback/endpoint mutations；closed-world role slots、16 Action IDs及special action→role/path/root map；compact short leaves与240 UTF-16 unit path fixtures；current selector固定`program-finalizing|program-finalizing-intake|program-terminal` mode并把ready/final status置于两个独立typed nullable字段，finalizing两mode统一读取`terminal_ready_incident_effect_v1`同一profile/digest的last-fully-accepted effective-ready，pending/received-only不入fold且所有suffix mode拒绝；ordinary-vs-finalization artifact tagged union、H0-less三路径publication与`terminal_git_publication_adapter_effect_capability_profile_v3`正反fixture。

所有primary substantive action还必须覆盖：R/I/Q先赢时logical/physical call count均为0；claim先赢只承诺恰一logical invocation而不伪称跨系统exactly-once；initial primary choice只在`pre_dispatch_no_call_proven`选择route且不改变physical state，generation-0 operation intent后才可首次call；从canonical enum派生完整8 states×6 operations physical矩阵与6 routes×6 operations gate；`no_call_proven`永久禁止dispatch/resume/complete；`no_observation|effect_unknown`仅query；`in_flight_same_server_operation_proven`除proof外还须新的successor intent才可继续同一server operation；continue/complete/cancel同predecessor generation恰一winner，cancel lost保持unknown；alternate primary choice/第二claim/new key/new scope/new logical invocation均拒绝。ordinary same-claim、Q quiescence、IDX under-lock三类事前预装physical member按authority basis互斥，由independent safety choice消费且实际调用另须operation intent，cross-branch/晚补签拒绝；terminal Git stale无member/call，只按no-retry lifecycle收口；safe-if-no-call sentinel与post-dispatch observation两种predecessor须exact-one，mixed/unlisted/fake均拒绝；result frozen/consumed/terminal后旧ticket全部physical operation为false。terminal Git adapter须覆盖supported/unsupported operation与缺profile/locator；IDX还必须覆盖`idx_atomic_abort_negative_closure_v3`正式14字段tuple、逐字段flip、terminal timing-cycle反例、atomic-acquired、timeout/unknown/inconsistent readback保持recovery/release义务，以及第1–16项durable crash-fail-closed destructive-boundary正反例。任一成员、正例或反例缺失即suite incomplete。

高风险迁移不得仅用 V0/V1。全仓也不应在每次小改重复运行；trigger 和超时写入 ticket。

V1/V3最低分母必须包含selector exact nullable source-field resolver，以及observation union的全部正反例；尤其stale-before-choice/stale-before-intent不能只断言push count=0，还须证明合法query receipt可构造、physical projection=null、non-PASS/old-terminal可达。selected-member/current-unknown、old observation revision与old compensation intent均不得绕过current query-only gate。

### 15.2 证据等级

- L0：文档/计划；
- L1：schema/fixture；
- L2：本地组件；
- L3：相邻 integration；
- L4：真实 frozen case；
- L5：独立 review/human/product；
- L6：release/operations。

安装成功最高只能证明 L1/L2；真实 case 没有人审不能自动到 L5。

### 15.3 评审分离

- 计划作者不能签自己的 final plan review；
- 组件实施者不能单独签 winner；
- 模型输出不能自评为 release；
- reviewer 默认只读；
- qualified-human 只在明确授权的 Evidence/product gate 中签发；
- reviewer finding 必须按 P0/P1/P2/P3 和最早责任层记录。

## 16. 安全、隐私、许可和供应商退出

每个候选在下载/安装前回答：

- 官方 source 和 exact revision；
- core/model/data/license 是否允许当前及未来商业形态；
- 是否有 AGPL/SSPL/ELv2/NC/open-core/hosted 限制；
- transitive SBOM；
- CVE/security update 和维护状态；
- source data 是否离开本机；
- region、retention、training/use-of-data；
- log/trace 是否含 private content；
- egress/domain/port；
- credential scope/rotation；
- adapter是否能禁用SDK隐式retry/fallback，以及每次非query调用是否有durable operation-intent winner、SDK retry是否可能绕过intent generation、每次实际物理调用、operation ID、readback、resume/cancel和server-side idempotency receipt是否可审计；cancel是否真正触达外部系统、lost/timeout能否qualified readback，不可证明时cancel及对应capability默认deny；
- idempotency key的真实作用域、保留时长、跨region/account/endpoint行为和过期后语义；禁止跨target/attempt/authority epoch复用key，也禁止把“服务商接受key”写成exactly-once证明；
- fencing/token是否由被调用系统实际强制，query/readback是否区分不存在、无观测、in-flight、完成、失败和ambiguous；query receipt只更新observation，不得自行成为completion/result receipt；
- 外部效果unknown、进程死亡、Q/stop/terminal时需要的recovery/compensation是否已在原authority/claim中预装且具备maximum-call和退出路径；member只有被对应basis的independent safety choice选中后才可进入调用门，实际recovery/compensation仍须自己的operation intent；若只能blind replay或事后补签，该candidate不得取得有副作用authority；
- export、backup、delete、uninstall、vendor exit；
- Windows/WSL2/Docker/remote/managed 实际资格。

法律意见未完成时只能标 legal_review_pending，不能写 license pass。

安全审计必须区分adapter raw classification与normalized physical projection。intentless query即使观察到terminal Git `exact_new|exact_old`也只能记录remote-state事实，不能归因成该attempt effect或absence；`valid_competitor|foreign|unknown`不能投影complete/absent/in-flight。query receipt不是completion/result receipt，terminal Git永远不能由readback产生same-server-operation proof或safety member。

## 17. 程序风险与预置应对

| 风险 | 早期信号 | 处置 |
|---|---|---|
| 引入框架后胶水更多 | 多套 state/schema/trace 并存 | 每类只保留一个 default；停止第二控制面 |
| vendor schema 渗透 | domain 层出现 vendor ID/score/state | 退回 port/adapter，禁止 consumer cutover |
| benchmark 追平均分 | critical period/unit/source slice 退化 | critical slice hard gate |
| LLM 变事实裁判 | model output 直接进入 Evidence | fail closed，恢复 SemanticCandidate 边界 |
| 迁移变量过多 | 一次同时换 parser/search/model/workflow | 拆 slice，单变量/可归因 |
| 磁盘再次不足 | lab budget 无 exact bytes | 停止下载；先分批或触发受控 index cleanup |
| 删除后无法重建 | index 无 input/producer digest | 禁止删除 |
| 旧代码永久桥接 | removal condition/consumer map 缺失 | 不允许切换完成 |
| 观测平台变权威 | MLflow/trace 状态影响 Evidence | 断开 authority，恢复 FIN manifest |
| SDK retry绕过控制面 | logical invocation=1但wire call>1，或SDK日志无法给出call receipt | 默认关闭SDK retry；不能关闭/核验则该adapter只读或淘汰，绝不以外层idempotency掩盖 |
| provider idempotency scope/retention不足 | 同key跨endpoint结果不同、key过期或readback查不到原operation | 冻结真实scope/TTL/fence；窗口外不得blind replay，转query-only/FAIL并交预注册recovery authority |
| result过早冻结 | result后仍有in-flight/unknown effect或继续/取消调用 | phase/action FAIL；旧ticket物理operation全关，保留义务并用新operations authority收口 |
| 计划过度刚性 | 新证据明显推翻 shortlist | 走 change control，不机械执行 |

## 18. 当前 authority matrix

authority matrix中“observation”均指`physical_operation_observation_receipt_v2`：nonquery分支继续要求exact intent；query分支只在exact query gate下免除本次call intent。terminal stale允许的intentless query basis仅为`terminal_git_stale_no_retry_lifecycle_closure_v2`，read cap=1、projection=null、same-attempt future nonquery=false；这不改变H0、Phase1–7、模型/API、index-delete、migration、publication或release的false状态。selector的ready source key固定为`last_fully_accepted_effective_ready_status_or_null`并须producer/invalid-output恰一命中。

计划候选尚未冻结时：

本表各行只列ticket-specific附加条件。16类control transition每次都必须重算冻结canonical remote identity/config projection，使用control profile将detached prepared direct child投入`lease_guarded_fast_forward_expected_old_v1`，remote acceptance后才成立；所有substantive行的execution authority只产生primary-claim eligibility。exact `substantive_action_start_claim_v5`在同一remote/ref赢得R/I/Q expected-old竞争后，只承诺恰好一个logical primary invocation；它不证明外部exactly-once。随后`primary_effect_choice_cas_v2`只选择dispatch/no-call route且不改变physical state；每个非query call必须由`physical_operation_intent_cas_v1`在generation-0 null predecessor或successor exact observation与generation上产生唯一remote-accepted winner；adapter支持的safe-if-no-call/post-dispatch recovery另由`prearmed_safety_suboperation_choice_cas_v3`选exact预装member，实际调用仍须自己的operation intent。缺对应winner时物理调用为0，任一winner不得代替另外两个责任面。

每个adapter先通过default-deny capability profile和registered `physical_operation_kind` total matrix：仅`pre_dispatch_no_call_proven`可选择initial dispatch route，首次call另须generation-0 operation intent；`no_call_proven`永久禁止primary dispatch/resume/complete；`no_observation|effect_unknown`只允许query；只有`in_flight_same_server_operation_proven`可在同一server-operation证明下竞争continue/complete/cancel的successor intent，同一idempotency key不是该证明。cancel为effecting，lost/timeout保持unknown。ordinary same-claim、Q quiescence、IDX under-lock三类预装physical member由exact authority basis的independent safety choice互斥消费，实际调用再取operation intent，不能晚补签或跨branch替换；terminal Git stale同attempt只query/readback并non-PASS terminal。先关闭physical effect/recovery义务再冻结result；result frozen/consumed或attempt terminal后旧ticket全部physical operation为false。repo artifact只能由ticket-bound ordinary/finalization tagged publication profile发布；无动作pure aggregate或外部只读review payload才不走substantive claim。current selector只使用固定mode `program-finalizing|program-finalizing-intake|program-terminal`，ready status与terminal status各占独立typed nullable字段，两个finalizing mode均读取同一`terminal_ready_incident_effect_v1` profile/output digest的last-fully-accepted effective-ready，received-only/pending不进入fold且任何mode suffix编码拒绝。

| 动作 | 当前 |
|---|---:|
| 写作、审查本计划 | true |
| Hn governance/source-doc/Project OS candidate 写入 | H0：plan fresh PASS后，先从plan逐字节重算五个canonical对象及expected SHA、seed remote-identity subhash、E0→E1 reservation/root vectors；验证actual remote/config、closed role slots/16 Action IDs/special action map、compact path schema与exact四字段pre-H0 authority-key derivation、special token/resolver totality和uint64 Emax无successor、H0-backed/H0-less同key、primary choice/per-call operation-intent/observation/typed-predecessor independent safety-choice合同、8×6 physical矩阵＋6×6 route gate、terminal-ready effect reducer profile、terminal Git stale no-retry、cancel effecting语义、`terminal_git_publication_adapter_effect_capability_profile_v3`、使用正式typed axes且terminal outcome非前置的IDX exact14字段abort projection；再要求finite pre-G source/stopper snapshot complete＋total reducer no-event。任一不符或任何Phase1–7/下载/模型/删除动作出现均令H0 grant=false。implementation successor：Fn/Sn各自仍须current no-event reducer后新 attempt；material/stop改走pre-activation Z，不激活 Phase 1 |
