## 19. 计划冻结后的第一执行队列

本队列在H0前还必须逐字节验证observation v2 union、terminal adapter v3 conditional scope、stale closure v2 read cap、selector nullable source-field resolver、unknown compensation两格deny与selected-member/current-unknown交集deny；H0复制同一profile并生成对应fixtures。任一query binding/nullability/revision/classification/safety-predecessor规则缺失或宽松alias成立，H0 normal grant=false。

严格顺序：

1. working-tree precheck 达到 P0/P1/P2=`0/0/0` 后，只暂存本计划；当前首次队列属于E0，按canonical predecessor确定连续的next alias Cj（machine ID=`E0-Cj`），生成不移动canonical local/tracking ref的plan-only detached prepared direct-child commit。publication前后逐字节核验冻结remote identity/config projection，以control profile和exact expected-old lease更新固定ref；结果未知只对同endpoint/repository/ref readback并按exact-new、exact-old、registry-valid competitor、missing/rewind/foreign integrity四类处理，remote accepted后才做local/tracking exact fast-forward reconciliation。冻结epoch/index/commit/tree/parent/blob/raw SHA-256/bytes/changed-path、resolved remote-identity digest、expected old/new、direct-parent proof、lease invocation digest、readback classification及alignment receipt；
2. fresh、作者分离、只读审查 exact Cj；reviewer 不写不修。若存在任何 P0/P1/P2，不得创建 H0；只能形成 `C(j+1).parent=Cj` 的新 plan-only revision，重新 commit/push/identity/review；唯一 latest 得到 `PASS 0/0/0/*` 的 revision 才被选为 accepted Ck，旧/失败/superseded Cj 不再授权；
3. accepted exact E0-Ck plan PASS后严格按下列不可交换顺序执行plan-to-H0验证：①逐字节读取并重算remote-bound `e0_pre_activation_bootstrap_seed_v4`、`post_z_destination_contract_template_v2`、完整`e0_pre_activation_bootstrap_bundle_preimage_v4`、reservation template、stable-root template五个canonical对象及expected SHA，并核验seed内remote-identity subhash；②只用exact模板替换算法生成E0 reservation digest与stable root，复算E0→E1 reservation/root vectors；③以`authority_epoch_id + compact_lifecycle_path_schema_sha256 + pre_activation_reservation_root_digest + program_id`四字段RFC8785 preimage生成compact authority key，证明static source kind仅作evidence，且H0-backed/H0-less在相同输入下生成同key；④验证全部special token grammar、finite source ID、typed reference resolver恰一命中、所有compact路径长度与uint64 Emax successor请求只能走integrity I→failed Z且绝不产生successor；⑤验证actual alias/fetch/push/pushurl/repository/common-dir/worktree/object-format/refspec/upstream projection及pre-H0 control profile只能访问冻结endpoint/ref/control paths；⑥验证closed-world roles、16 Action IDs/subsets、special action→role/path/root map、selector fixed modes/独立status字段、`terminal_ready_incident_effect_v1` profile解析及last-effective-ready accepted-prefix来源、initial primary choice/per-call operation intent/observation/typed-predecessor independent safety choice、8×6 physical matrix＋6×6 route gate、terminal-ready effect profile、terminal Git stale no-retry、cancel effecting、three-safety-branch/terminal-stale-lifecycle/result contracts、`terminal_git_publication_adapter_effect_capability_profile_v3`与H0-less terminal三路径profile/caps，以及正式typed axes且terminal outcome非前置的IDX exact14字段abort projection。任一步失败即H0 normal grant=false，不得跳到后项补救；

完成上述静态序列后，才逐项核验finite sources/principals/stoppers/receipt namespace/total class map/terminal-sync allowlist，并从Ck review cutoff到current remote parent重算per-source complete `pre_g_transition_snapshot_v1`。漏static member/hash/receipt/pending payload、sequence gap/duplicate/replay/unknown、invalid stopper或material/stop/integrity均按total reducer走event receipt、recoverable intake、reviewed Z或failed-Z。只有`accepted_Ck_no_event_to_H0`才准备E0-H0 commit并以expected remote parent做lease-guarded exact-expected-old fast-forward CAS；remote接受后再fast-forward reconcile。H0只可copy/verify上述对象，物化plan/epoch/ticket/phase/program registries、manifest/root/runtime/source/ProjectOS/worklog/tests；落下16类control action及全部special variants、authority-neutral exact-ref core和ticket-bound artifact profiles、primary claim/R-I-Q race、initial primary choice、per-call operation intent与observation receipt、typed-predecessor independent safety choice、canonical 8×6 total physical-operation matrix＋6×6 route-operation gate、cancel effecting语义、三类预装physical recovery member、terminal Git stale no-retry lifecycle与result boundary、terminal-ready effect reducer profile、terminal Git adapter、8态raw-trigger、typed selector、phase close、qualification/cleanup、IDX durable interlock与10.6测试、Q/Z/handoff/joint-final-seal/RELEASE schemas。H0不改本计划、不成为bundle来源、不安装/下载/调用/删除或开启Phase action；验证remote acceptance/local aligned；

第3步的H0复制/验证不得用自然语言摘要代替machine fixtures：五对象/hash/vector、authority-key preimage、special resolver/Emax、short leaf/path budget、role/action map、selector mode/status/value-source及terminal-ready effect profile/digest equality、initial primary choice/per-call operation intent/observation/typed-predecessor independent safety choice/result、`physical_operation_matrix_totality_profile_v1` 48格与`effect_route_operation_gate_profile_v1` 36格、cancel lost、ordinary/finalization publication、terminal Git adapter capability、H0-less三路径caps、post-Z discriminant、terminal-ready accepted-prefix reducer正反例、ADR-15 exact denominator和IDX正式14字段abort/recovery projection必须同时存在。缺任一成员、映射、正例或反例时H0 grant=false。

4. 对 clean、已 commit 且已用lease-guarded exact-expected-old fast-forward发布、local origin-tracking aligned 的 exact Hn 做 fresh read-only review；reviewer 不写不修；
5. PASS review本身仍不足：先以logical head=Hn及remote current materialization parent重算total pre-G snapshot/reducer；只有输出`Hn_no_external_event_to_Tn`时才prepared one-path Tn，绑定logical parent=Hn、continuous I-set、Git/expected remote parent=current materialization parent，并以该parent做lease-guarded exact-expected-old fast-forward CAS；event/material/stop/integrity receipt先被remote接受则Tn CAS失败并走对应分支。remote接受Tn后才fast-forward reconcile，验证changed path、single parent、envelope、I ancestry和plan immutability；不得在同一prepared commit中创建G0；
6. 第5步通过后以logical head=Tn及remote current materialization parent重算exact seed、全部per-source accepted/pending receipt high-watermarks与uncovered anomaly。只有complete no-event snapshot、reducer=`Tn_no_event_to_G`时，才构造logical parent=Tn、Git parent=current materialization parent的detached/prepared exact G0 commit object而不移动local canonical/tracking ref；随后以该expected remote materialization parent做lease-guarded exact-expected-old fast-forward CAS。event receipt或其他transition先占remote parent时G push必须失败并从new remote head重算；remote结果未知只读查询：仍为expected parent则prepared orphan无authority，等于exact G则只做本地幂等fast-forward reconciliation，其他合法transition按其new head重算，foreign/非法ref进入integrity control。remote exact G acceptance是route boundary，本地aligned前eligibility仍false；验证G0只有fixed activation path、无substantive grant，禁止reset/unconstrained force/ref delete-or-recreate/孤儿冒充G；
7. FAIL review先由current total reducer区分implementation/material/external event，再按相同prepared-object＋expected-remote-parent CAS只接受允许的one-path Fn。implementation Fn后每次Sn和H(n+1)均从remote current head重算no-event snapshot/reducer并独立CAS；任一event/integrity receipt先赢则normal successor失败并走Z。material-plan finding令same finding的Sn/Hnext=false，经独立review＋snapshot＋empty-authority proof单独remote-accept Z superseded；valid stop→Z stopped；irrecoverable integrity→I/review/Z failed。same-Fn/same-finding分支不得并存；historical Sn后distinct event/stop仍处理；
8. machine test必须证明k/n各边界值可计算effective Phase0 PASS/G(e) activated/Phase1与Phase2 candidate eligible，并拒绝旧/失败/superseded/断链/merge/tracking/sibling；同时断言Phase1 audit substantive grant=false、Phase2 RSH network grant=false。Phase2仍false或任一action被G(e)置true、仅Phase1 eligible而registry声明双setter均FAIL；
9. valid remote-accepted且local/origin-tracking aligned的G(e)后只可提出P1和RSH candidates；每张P1/RSH execution authority只令其exact primary start claim eligible，claim须在冻结remote/ref上与R/I/Q以expected-old lease竞争。R/I/Q先赢时logical/physical call count=0；remote接受exact claim、fence/idempotency/invocation-key/action/target/adapter后，只承诺恰好一个logical invocation。其后initial primary choice只选择dispatch/no-call route；winner=`dispatch_primary`时physical state仍为`pre_dispatch_no_call_proven`，generation-0 operation intent胜出后才可首次call。真实observation后，continue/complete/cancel各自竞争绑定exact predecessor的successor intent；`no_call_proven`永不dispatch/resume/complete，`no_observation|effect_unknown`默认只query，in-flight还须same-server-operation proof，cancel lost/timeout保持unknown。adapter支持的safe-if-no-call/post-dispatch recovery先由typed-predecessor independent safety choice选中ordinary/Q exact预装member，实际safety call再取operation intent。RSH在claim、primary choice、每次intent/operation及safety choice时持续绑定relevant P1 constraint terminal/activation；SDK retry不得越过该矩阵。prepared/local-only/timeout未接受claim的调用次数为0；第二claim/new-key/new scope/alternate primary choice、同generation intent双winner一律拒绝。先关闭physical义务再冻结result，result后旧ticket operation全部false。repo输出另须使用ticket-bound artifact publication profile，control transport不得代发。Phase3–7只能由`phase_entry_gate_registry_v1`的exact close-ticket activation依次开启。

对普通P1/RSH，`accepted_claim_pre_choice|accepted_choice_pre_intent` intentless query默认不开放；只有adapter capability另有新版本明确opt-in才可申请。当前唯一opt-in是terminal Git stale closure，且它不是ordinary ticket新调用权限。`accepted_intent` query refinement必须引用latest exact intent/observation revision；query仍不创建新的operation generation。

本队列不包含 package install、模型调用、外源、formal、索引删除或生产迁移。

correction/terminalization走独立队列：G已激活时raw trigger reducer→initial/reevaluate A→selector/latches→candidate→review→independent authority/Q；candidate sealed后的late stop/mandatory写append-only I evidence-set并真实终结旧attempt再retry，review PASS/no-authority必须先denial-only decision，Q后的I由同一closure/Z消费。Q冻结complete grant universe与pure Q-root：started ordinary真实收口，revoked-unstarted authorized写negative/review/stopped，candidate-only先用pre-Z freeze证明完整、Z后再按Z outcome纯投影，current rollover写result/review/terminal；interrupt intake完整后unsafe→Z failed，safe stop（含mandatory+stop）→Z stopped，无stop的clean plan/mandatory→Z superseded。Z body只冻结destination template；commit后才导出actual positive successor handoff/next-E C0 parent与negative-ready origin。G从未存在时只按bootstrap bundle＋finite pre-G sources/stoppers＋total snapshot/reducer走pre-activation reviewed Z；H0存在也不创建rollover，Tn后event令G false，同一Fn finding不得Sn/Z双分支，historical Sn后distinct event可Z。无latch plan撤回可A=false回active；latch不得因失败清除。pre-Q失败不造Q-path Z，standalone stop不直接terminal，各root-map/member/subject不可互换。negative Z与normal/partial/no_release任一base-ready建立terminal-incident origin与received/accepted high-watermarks；pending/gap/uncovered-corrupt保持intake-only，included-source drift先deterministic integrity I。若old sync attempt已存在，按candidate/review/authority/action-start/result/result-review stage用stale root真实non-PASS收口，review PASS/no-authority先denial-only、started不得走negative shortcut，old terminal前不得retry；current observation/source projection稳定、pre-seal stream=`open_clean|integrity_failure_closed`且sync PASS后，只能由joint reducer一次输出唯一terminal＋sealed pair，其中integrity-closed只允许failed。

terminal Git stale stage按claim/choice/intent存在性选择三种query binding；pre-choice与pre-intent的choice/intent nullability必须逐字节满足、projection固定null、同attempt nonquery永久false。一次readback仍unknown时直接形成non-PASS obligation，不允许无界query或第二push；old terminal后仍须fresh exact remote-ref observation，fresh ref unknown则new attempt physical call=0。

上述所有started closure仍受typed operation/result边界约束：ordinary、Q与IDX recovery分别只能由对应basis的independent safety choice消费`ordinary_same_claim_recovery`、`q_quiescence`、`index_under_lock_recovery` member，实际closure call还须自己的operation intent；terminal Git stale只query/readback、non-PASS review/terminal并在fresh-ref后新attempt；未知效果只query，不能以Q/Z/terminal名义blind replay；member未预装、safety winner或operation intent缺失、或result已冻结时物理调用为0，未关闭义务必须成为显式non-PASS与新authority handoff。

## 20. Plan review history 与 v1.6 修正

本节在记录旧 revision 时保留当时使用的票名和问题摘要（例如旧 `H0→G0`、未命名空间化的 `R14-REPLACEMENT-S1-CLOSURE`、`review/repair`）；它们只是不可变审计历史，不是 v1.6 的现行机器语义。现行权威只取第 2–19 节及本节明确列出的 v1.6 修正。

首个 authority epoch=`E0` 的历史 identity 固定为：recovery baseline=`34589b6b8552e0236912dacb0664d7714bbf275c`；unreviewed plan seed=`9b848b3fe372f908b27bc541682b8af656562a0a`、parent=baseline；v1.0人类alias=`C0`/machine=`E0-C0`、parent=seed；v1.1..v1.5分别为`E0-C1`..`E0-C5`，各自direct parent=前一同epoch Cj。seed不属于review chain且无authority。当前v1.6 working tree只有在parent=E0-C5 alias commit、changed path仅本计划、commit/push/identity全部通过后才成为`E0-C6`；若exact review FAIL，下一revision是`E0-C7`。E0-H0只绑定E0内unique latest exact-review PASS Ck。H0后的plan修订绝不继续全局C号，而走Z0→E1-C0。

exact v1.4 commit `eeb75e1185898802b081577c1d20c7c32b71c476`、tree=`9b80346b347f4fb313b76b0d97fc3f6e69fc7b9a`、parent=`b1c961edde4689c18d12aee0db4260a5021b93cb`、plan blob=`84a217b92038bb2406f0ded46af4b426e0c85737`、raw SHA-256=`e65534997487383dd6a25944ac5a24b5cb192e360af7d104fba5a345fa275c93`、bytes=`93045`，经作者分离、只读、对 exact v1.4 首次完整阅读的 reviewer 审阅得到 `PLAN_FAIL_REVISION_REQUIRED / P0-P1-P2-P3=0/4/3/1`。受协作树硬节点上限影响，该 reviewer 复用了曾做 exact v1.1 只读 review 与 v1.2 H0 machine mapping、但未参与 v1.4 写作的既有任务身份，不冒充新的 fork-none 节点；审阅前后 repo clean、local origin-tracking ref aligned，且 writes/network/model/install/formal/delete/old-live-state 均为 0。该失败保持不可变；v1.5 针对八类 finding 作出：

第四个 v1.6 working-tree precheck candidate（raw SHA-256=`d4580ba2204f6d6e602196b7c4eff77a84003879438eaab59f8fccd01b8cf739`、bytes=`159996`、lines=`1565`）经同一只读 reviewer 从第1行到EOF全量复核并稳定postflight，得到`PRECHECK_FAIL / P0-P1-P2-P3=0/3/0/0`；reviewer未参与该candidate写作，writes/stage/commit/push/network/model/API/install/formal/delete/old-live-state均为0。这仍不是exact commit review。v1.6在提交前继续针对三项machine-authority finding修正：

1. 为conditional ticket增加独立于candidate的epoch-scoped `target-attempt-APP-A{a}` identity、append-only A chain、RFC8785 grant preimage、numeric unique selector与candidate exact full-receipt binding；旧/stale/sibling/cross-epoch receipt、candidate后追加A、selector empty/ambiguous均fail closed；
2. 把QL/MIG fallback cleanup从“每source stable ID一张family票”改为：两个always/no-action CLEANUP-INDEX aggregate冻结closed source frontier和`cleanup_obligation_expansion_v1`，再按每个`source attempt × exact target`的full-digest obligation instance计算applicability/terminal/phase denominator；frontier drift/late target/漏项/只清一个都阻止phase PASS；
3. 把post-H0 plan change从一句概念规则改成same-program multi-authority-epoch协议：epoch-scoped plan/H0/ticket identities，预注册`PROGRAM-EPOCH-ROLLOVER`，Q(e) cutoff/grant freeze/真实quiescence、Z(e) terminal、bounded E(e+1)-C0 escape、unique current selector、cross-epoch deny与mutation matrix；旧epoch evidence可只读revalidate，但旧PASS/grant/activation不carry。

第五个v1.6 working-tree precheck candidate（raw SHA-256=`fa17d51d2b55b9365e0911712e308c306e176948d4dbc75bd1ec06659395b67a`、bytes=`185973`、lines=`1643`）经同一只读full reviewer从第1行到EOF审阅并稳定postflight，得到`PRECHECK_FAIL / P0-P1-P2-P3=0/5/1/1`；两名定向reviewer另分别报告applicability-path P1=`0/1/0`与epoch-closure=`0/1/1`。三者均未参与该candidate写作且writes/stage/commit/push/network/model/install/formal/delete/old-live-state=0。v1.6继续在提交前修正：

1. 用full-64 `target_attempt_path_key`统一全部lifecycle短路径，完整logical IDs保留在envelope，冻结Windows absolute UTF-16 path budget=240；generic/cleanup双路径、key mismatch/collision/截断/case alias拒绝；
2. 注册`phase_entry_gate_registry_v1`与PHASE-1..6-CLOSE no-action aggregates；G(e)只开Phase1/2 candidates，RSH authority绑定relevant P1 constraints，Phase3–7逐close activation开启，另有互斥partial-closeout-only入口；
3. 该候选曾为CLEANUP-INDEX设计“所属Phase未PASS时PASS→frontier-drift refresh”；第六次precheck证明same-epoch refresh没有合法frontier producer，现行v1.6已将其撤销并统一改为mandatory PROGRAM-EPOCH-ROLLOVER；
4. 把Q(e)后closure whitelist按exact subject/action冻结：旧ordinary只可truthful result/review/terminal，当前rollover可完成自身result/review/terminal/activation/Z；其他activation拒绝；
5. 把Z分为互斥正负分支：clean PASS/activation→superseded＋successor；truthful unresolved/review FAIL/rollover stopped-or-failed/Owner stop→negative Z、无activation/successor，避免负路径死锁；

因此，第十二轮历史第1项中的`pre_g_route_order_v1`、global sequencer、preflight CAS token与route-cutoff token只保留为当时finding修复历史，不是第2–19节的现行机制；第十三轮修正明确以exact seed＋per-source remote-accepted receipts＋Git remote expected-parent CAS取代它们。
